```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { and, asc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { agendaBlocks, appointments, auditLog, contacts, deals, lists, professionals, professionalServices, stages, visits } from "../../db/schema";
import { resetDb } from "../../../../test/db";
import { ap, bahia, CASA_TER_SAB, cliente, NOW, setAgendaMode, setSetting, setupAgenda } from "../../../../test/agenda-fixtures";
import { createVisit, ExistingAppointmentError, type CreateVisitInput } from "./visits";

let s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setupAgenda>>;
beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(); s = await setupAgenda(); });

const at10 = bahia(16, 10).toISOString();
const book = (contactId: string, over: Partial<CreateVisitInput> = {}): CreateVisitInput => ({
  requestKey: randomUUID(), client: { contactId }, startsAt: at10, items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.ana.id }], ...over,
});
const apptsOf = (visitId: string) => db.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.visitId, visitId)).orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt), asc(appointments.professionalId));
const stageOf = async (dealId: string) => (await db.select({ k: stages.key }).from(deals).innerJoin(stages, eq(stages.id, deals.stageId)).where(eq(deals.id, dealId)))[0].k;

describe("marcar", () => {
  it("1 serviço: visita do dia, horário do CRM com preço e duração copiados do serviço, e auditoria", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    const r = await createVisit(book(p.contactId, { notes: "trazer foto" }), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ day: "2026-10-16", reused: false });
    expect((await db.select().from(visits).where(eq(visits.id, r.visitId)))[0]).toMatchObject({ contactId: p.contactId, day: "2026-10-16", origin: "crm", createdBy: s.cati.id });
    const [a] = await apptsOf(r.visitId);
    expect(a).toMatchObject({
      id: r.appointmentIds[0], externalKey: `crm:${r.appointmentIds[0]}`, origin: "crm", status: "agendado", professionalId: s.ana.id, serviceId: s.corte.id,
      serviceLabel: "Corte", durationMin: 60, priceCents: 12000, notes: "trazer foto", fitIn: false, outsideHours: false, anyPro: false, createdBy: s.cati.id,
    });
    expect(a.startsAt.toISOString()).toBe(at10);
    expect(a.crmTouchedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect((await db.select().from(auditLog).where(eq(auditLog.entityId, a.id)))[0]).toMatchObject({ action: "agendamento_marcado", userId: s.cati.id });
  });

  it("2 serviços: 'logo depois' começa no fim do anterior; 'ao mesmo tempo' exige outra profissional", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    const r1 = await createVisit(book(p.contactId, { items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.ana.id }, { serviceId: s.hidratacao.id, professionalId: s.ana.id, mode: "depois" }] }), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r1.visitId)).map((a) => a.startsAt.toISOString())).toEqual([bahia(16, 10), bahia(16, 11)].map((d) => d.toISOString()));
    const q = await cliente("Joana Lima");
    const at14 = bahia(16, 14).toISOString();
    const r2 = await createVisit(book(q.contactId, { startsAt: at14, items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.ana.id }, { serviceId: s.hidratacao.id, professionalId: s.bia.id, mode: "junto" }] }), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r2.visitId)).map((a) => a.startsAt.toISOString())).toEqual([at14, at14]);
    await expect(createVisit(book(q.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, 16).toISOString(), items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.ana.id }, { serviceId: s.hidratacao.id, professionalId: s.ana.id, mode: "junto" }] }), s.recepcao, NOW))
      .rejects.toThrow("Ao mesmo tempo, as profissionais precisam ser diferentes.");
  });

  it("cliente nova: telefone já cadastrado reaproveita o contato (nunca duplica) e 'Não é cliente' volta a ser cliente", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    await db.update(contacts).set({ ignored: true }).where(eq(contacts.id, p.contactId));
    const r = await createVisit(book(p.contactId, { client: { name: "Outro Nome", phone: p.digits } }), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r.visitId))[0].contactId).toBe(p.contactId);
    expect((await db.select().from(contacts).where(eq(contacts.id, p.contactId)))[0]).toMatchObject({ ignored: false, name: "Paula Souza" });
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId, { client: { name: "Sem Fone", phone: "123" } }), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Celular inválido. Use DDD + número, ex.: 71 99999-9999");
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId, { client: { name: " ", phone: p.digits } }), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Escreva o nome da cliente.");
  });

  it("conflito com horário do CRM: sem 'Encaixar mesmo assim' (ou sem a permissão) recusa com o texto; com os dois, grava o encaixe", async () => {

    await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW);

    const outra = (fitIn?: boolean) => book(q.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, 10, 30).toISOString(), fitIn });
    await expect(createVisit(outra(), s.recepcao, later)).rejects.toThrow("Ana já tem Paula das 10:00 às 11:00");
    await expect(createVisit(outra(true), s.semEncaixe, later)).rejects.toThrow("Ana já tem Paula das 10:00 às 11:00");
    const r = await createVisit(outra(true), s.recepcao, later);
    expect((await apptsOf(r.visitId))[0]).toMatchObject({ fitIn: true, fitInBy: s.cati.id, outsideHours: false });
  });

  it("conflito com horário do Avec e com bloqueio", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    await ap({ startsAt: bahia(16, 14), professionalId: s.ana.id, clientName: "Maria Avec" });
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, 14).toISOString() }), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Ana já tem Maria das 14:00 às 15:00");
    await db.insert(agendaBlocks).values({ professionalId: s.ana.id, title: "Almoço", startsOn: "2026-10-16", endsOn: "2026-10-16", startTime: "12:00", endTime: "13:00", externalKey: "crm:blq:teste", origin: "crm", reason: "almoco" });
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, 12, 30).toISOString(), fitIn: true }), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow(/bloqueada nesse horário/);
  });

  it("a mesma cliente com outra profissional no mesmo horário", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW);
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, 10, 30).toISOString(), items: [{ serviceId: s.hidratacao.id, professionalId: s.bia.id }] }), s.recepcao, bahia(15, 9, 10)))
      .rejects.toThrow("Paula já tem Corte às 10:00 com a Ana");
  });

  it("Review Focus 2: profissional sem horário próprio na segunda, com a Casa fechada: só com encaixe, e fica registrado", async () => {
    await setSetting("business_hours", CASA_TER_SAB);
    const [carla] = await db.insert(professionals).values({ name: "Carla" }).returning();
    await db.insert(professionalServices).values({ professionalId: carla.id, serviceId: s.corte.id });
    const p = await cliente();
    const seg = (fitIn?: boolean) => book(p.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(19, 10).toISOString(), items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: carla.id }], fitIn });
    await expect(createVisit(seg(), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Fora do horário de trabalho de Carla.");
    await expect(createVisit(seg(true), s.semEncaixe, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Fora do horário de trabalho de Carla.");
    const r = await createVisit(seg(true), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r.visitId))[0]).toMatchObject({ outsideHours: true, fitIn: false });
  });

  it("Review Focus 3: 23:30 por 60 min é recusado, mesmo com encaixe", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, 23, 30).toISOString(), fitIn: true }), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Esse horário passa da meia-noite");
  });

  it("serviço sem duração: pede a desta vez; com ela, marca; 'Salvar como duração do serviço' só com config.editar", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    const mechas = (d?: number, save?: boolean, startsAt = at10) => book(p.contactId, { startsAt, items: [{ serviceId: s.mechas.id, professionalId: s.ana.id, durationMin: d, saveDuration: save }] });
    await expect(createVisit(mechas(), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Mechas não tem duração cadastrada. Diga quanto tempo leva desta vez.");
    await expect(createVisit(mechas(97), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Confira a duração: de 5 em 5 minutos, até 12 horas.");
    const r = await createVisit(mechas(90, true), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r.visitId))[0].durationMin).toBe(90);
    expect((await db.select().from(lists).where(eq(lists.id, s.mechas.id)))[0].durationMin).toBeNull();
    await createVisit(mechas(120, true, bahia(16, 14).toISOString()), s.gestao, NOW);
    expect((await db.select().from(lists).where(eq(lists.id, s.mechas.id)))[0].durationMin).toBe(120);
  });

  it("a profissional precisa fazer o serviço; com config.editar dá para marcar 'Ela faz esse serviço' ali mesmo", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    await db.delete(professionalServices).where(and(eq(professionalServices.professionalId, s.ana.id), eq(professionalServices.serviceId, s.hidratacao.id)));
    const item = () => book(p.contactId, { items: [{ serviceId: s.hidratacao.id, professionalId: s.ana.id, linkService: true }] });
    await expect(createVisit(item(), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Ana não faz esse serviço.");
    await createVisit(item(), s.gestao, NOW);
    expect(await db.select().from(professionalServices).where(and(eq(professionalServices.professionalId, s.ana.id), eq(professionalServices.serviceId, s.hidratacao.id)))).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("Qualquer uma: a livre com menos horários no dia; empate pela ordem da agenda", async () => {
    const [p, q, r0] = [await cliente(), await cliente("Joana Lima"), await cliente("Rita Melo")];
    const any = (contactId: string, h: number) => book(contactId, { startsAt: bahia(16, h).toISOString(), items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: null }] });
    const r1 = await createVisit(any(p.contactId, 10), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r1.visitId))[0]).toMatchObject({ professionalId: s.ana.id, anyPro: true });
    const r2 = await createVisit(any(q.contactId, 14), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await apptsOf(r2.visitId))[0].professionalId).toBe(s.bia.id);
    const r3 = await createVisit(any(r0.contactId, 10), s.recepcao, bahia(15, 9, 10));
    expect((await apptsOf(r3.visitId))[0].professionalId).toBe(s.bia.id);
  });

  it("Encaixe agora: a cliente chegou sem hora e o horário nasce como Chegou", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    const now = bahia(16, 10, 5);
    const r = await createVisit(book(p.contactId, { walkIn: true }), s.recepcao, now);
    const [a] = await apptsOf(r.visitId);
    expect(a.status).toBe("chegou");
    expect(a.arrivedAt?.toISOString()).toBe(now.toISOString());
  });

  it("o mesmo horário que já veio do Avec: não cria outro e aponta o que existe", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    const avec = await ap({ startsAt: bahia(16, 10), contactId: p.contactId, serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.bia.id });
    const e = await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW).catch((x: unknown) => x);
    expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(ExistingAppointmentError);
    expect(e).toMatchObject({ message: "Paula já tem Corte às 10:00 (veio do Avec). Abrir o horário", appointmentId: avec.id, day: "2026-10-16" });
    expect(await db.select().from(visits)).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it("modo consulta recusa marcar", async () => {
    await setAgendaMode("consulta");
    const p = await cliente();
    await expect(createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("A agenda do CRM está só para consulta. A gestão muda em Configurações › Agenda.");
  });
});

describe("Review Focus 4: dois cliques ao mesmo tempo", () => {
  it("duas transações para a Ana às 10:00: uma passa, a outra recebe 'acabou de ser ocupado'", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza"), q = await cliente("Natália Salgado");
    const rs = await Promise.allSettled([createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW), createVisit(book(q.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW)]);
    expect(rs.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled")).toHaveLength(1);
    const fail = rs.find((r) => r.status === "rejected") as PromiseRejectedResult;
    expect(fail.reason.message).toBe("Esse horário acabou de ser ocupado (Cati marcou Paula às 09:00). Escolha outro.");
    expect(await db.select().from(appointments)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("o mesmo formulário enviado duas vezes (mesma request_key) cria uma visita só", async () => {
    const p = await cliente();
    const input = book(p.contactId);
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([createVisit(input, s.recepcao, NOW), createVisit(input, s.recepcao, NOW)]);
    expect(a.visitId).toBe(b.visitId);
    expect([a.reused, b.reused].sort()).toEqual([false, true]);
    expect(await db.select().from(visits)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(await db.select().from(appointments)).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe("funil (1C)", () => {
  it("marcar de dentro da conversa grava visits.deal_id e leva a Agendado", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza", { conversaHaDias: 1, stage: "atendimento" });
    const r = await createVisit(book(p.contactId, { dealId: p.dealId }), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect((await db.select().from(visits).where(eq(visits.id, r.visitId)))[0].dealId).toBe(p.dealId);
    expect(await stageOf(p.dealId!)).toBe("agendado");
  });
  it("decisão 4: sem conversa nos últimos 30 dias, marcar não cria cartão", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Joana Lima", { conversaHaDias: 40 });
    await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW);
    expect(await db.select().from(deals).where(eq(deals.contactId, p.contactId))).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { and, asc, eq, inArray, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db, type Tx } from "../../db";
import { appointments, auditLog, contacts, deals, lists, professionals, professionalServices, visits } from "../../db/schema";
import { UserFacingError } from "../errors";
import { normalizePhone } from "../phone";
import { assertProfessionalFor } from "../professionals";
import { normText } from "../avec/normalize";
import { dayOf, minuteOfDay } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { ACTIVE_STATUSES } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { conflictText, justTakenText, refusalText } from "@/lib/agenda-text";
import { firstName } from "@/lib/authorship";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { assertCanBook } from "./mode";
import { lockAgendaTx } from "./locks";
import { checkSlotTx, proDayCounts, prosForService, type SlotCheckResult } from "./slots";
import { hhmm, pickAny } from "@/lib/free-slots";
import { agendaFunnelTx } from "./funnel-hook";
import { afterAgendaChange } from "./notify";
import { canFitIn, type Actor } from "./actor";

/**
 * Marcar, remarcar e cancelar na Agenda (Lote 6B). Cada função é uma porta só, numa transação (6.4, regra 1): confere o modo,
 * trava a agenda, relê o banco e roda o motor, grava e audita, chama o gancho do funil e, depois do commit, avisa as telas.
 */
export const MAX_ITEMS = 4;
export const DURATION_STEP = 5;
export const DURATION_MAX = 720;
/** Conflito criado há menos disso: "Esse horário acabou de ser ocupado". */
export const JUST_TAKEN_MS = 2 * 60_000;

export type ClientRef = { contactId: string } | { name: string; phone: string };
const itemSchema = z.object({
  serviceId: z.string().uuid("Escolha o serviço."),
  professionalId: z.string().uuid().nullable(),
  durationMin: z.number().int().nullable().optional(),
  saveDuration: z.boolean().optional(),
  linkService: z.boolean().optional(),
  mode: z.enum(["depois", "junto"]).optional(),
});
export const createVisitSchema = z.object({
  requestKey: z.string().uuid(),
  client: z.custom<ClientRef>((v) => !!v && typeof v === "object", "Escolha a cliente."),
  dealId: z.string().uuid().nullable().optional(),
  startsAt: z.string().datetime({ offset: true, message: "Escolha o dia e a hora." }),
  items: z.array(itemSchema).min(1, "Escolha o serviço.").max(MAX_ITEMS, `Até ${MAX_ITEMS} serviços por visita.`),
  notes: z.string().trim().max(1000, "Use até 1000 caracteres na observação.").nullable().optional(),
  fitIn: z.boolean().optional(),
  walkIn: z.boolean().optional(),
});
export type BookingItemInput = z.input<typeof itemSchema>;
export type CreateVisitInput = z.input<typeof createVisitSchema>;
export type CreateVisitResult = { visitId: string; appointmentIds: string[]; day: string; reused: boolean };

/** Já existe o mesmo horário (mesma cliente, início e serviço): a tela oferece "Abrir o horário". */
export class ExistingAppointmentError extends UserFacingError {
  constructor(message: string, readonly appointmentId: string, readonly day: string) { super(message); }
}

/** A cliente do horário: pelo contato, ou pelo telefone (nunca duplica, nunca troca o nome). Quem estava como "Não é cliente" volta a ser cliente. */
export async function resolveClientTx(tx: Tx, ref: ClientRef): Promise<{ id: string; name: string | null }> {
  if ("contactId" in ref && ref.contactId) {
    const [c] = await tx.update(contacts).set({ ignored: false }).where(eq(contacts.id, z.string().uuid().parse(ref.contactId))).returning({ id: contacts.id, name: contacts.name });
    if (!c) throw new UserFacingError("Cliente não encontrada.");
    return c;
  }
  const name = ("name" in ref ? ref.name : "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  if (!name) throw new UserFacingError("Escreva o nome da cliente.");
  if (name.length > 120) throw new UserFacingError("Use até 120 caracteres no nome.");
  const phone = normalizePhone("phone" in ref ? ref.phone : "");
  if (!phone) throw new UserFacingError("Celular inválido. Use DDD + número, ex.: 71 99999-9999");
  const [c] = await tx.insert(contacts).values({ phoneE164: phone, name })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: contacts.phoneE164, set: { ignored: false, name: sql`coalesce(${contacts.name}, excluded.name)` } })
    .returning({ id: contacts.id, name: contacts.name });
  return c;
}

type Svc = { id: string; label: string; durationMin: number | null; bufferMin: number | null; priceCents: number | null };
type Planned = { service: Svc; professionalId: string | null; durationMin: number; start: Date; mode: "depois" | "junto" };

async function planItems(tx: Tx, input: z.output<typeof createVisitSchema>, actor: Actor): Promise<Planned[]> {
  const ids = [...new Set(input.items.map((i) => i.serviceId))];
  const rows = await tx.select({ id: lists.id, label: lists.label, durationMin: lists.durationMin, bufferMin: lists.bufferMin, priceCents: lists.priceCents, active: lists.active })
    .from(lists).where(and(inArray(lists.id, ids), eq(lists.kind, "service")));
  const byId = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const out: Planned[] = [];
  for (const [k, it] of input.items.entries()) {
    const s = byId.get(it.serviceId);
    if (!s?.active) throw new UserFacingError("Esse serviço não está mais na lista. Escolha outro.");
    let durationMin = s.durationMin;
    if (durationMin == null) {
      const d = it.durationMin ?? null;
      if (d == null) throw new UserFacingError(refusalText("sem_duracao", { proName: "", serviceLabel: s.label }));
      if (d < DURATION_STEP || d > DURATION_MAX || d % DURATION_STEP) throw new UserFacingError(refusalText("duracao_invalida", { proName: "", serviceLabel: s.label }));
      durationMin = d;
      if (it.saveDuration && can(actor.perms, "config.editar")) {
        await tx.update(lists).set({ durationMin: d }).where(eq(lists.id, s.id));
        await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actor.id, action: "editou", entity: "list", entityId: s.id, data: { label: s.label, durationMin: d, origem: "agenda" } });
      }
    }
    const prev = out[k - 1];
    const mode = k === 0 ? "depois" : it.mode ?? "depois";
    if (prev && mode === "junto" && it.professionalId && it.professionalId === prev.professionalId) throw new UserFacingError("Ao mesmo tempo, as profissionais precisam ser diferentes.");
    const start = !prev ? new Date(input.startsAt) : mode === "junto" ? prev.start : new Date(prev.start.getTime() + prev.durationMin * 60_000);
    if (it.professionalId) {
      if (it.linkService && can(actor.perms, "config.editar")) {
        await tx.insert(professionalServices).values({ professionalId: it.professionalId, serviceId: s.id, source: "manual", createdBy: actor.id }).onConflictDoNothing();
      }
      await assertProfessionalFor(tx, it.professionalId, s.id);
    }
    out.push({ service: s, professionalId: it.professionalId, durationMin, start, mode });
  }
  if (out.some((p) => dayOf(p.start) !== dayOf(out[0].start))) throw new UserFacingError("Todos os serviços da visita precisam ser no mesmo dia.");
  return out;
}

/**
 * O que fazer com o resultado do motor: recusa (com o texto do conflito ou da regra), ou aceita. Conflito e fora do horário só passam
 * com "Encaixar mesmo assim" (`fitIn`) E a permissão `agenda.encaixar` (6.4, regra 3); quem acabou de ocupar aparece pelo nome.
 */
export function decideBooking(check: SlotCheckResult, ctx: { proName: string; serviceLabel: string; fitIn: boolean; actor: Actor; now: Date }): { fitIn: boolean; outsideHours: boolean } {
  if (!check.ok) {
    const c = check.conflicts[0];
    throw new UserFacingError(check.reason === "conflito" && c ? conflictText(ctx.proName, c) : refusalText(check.reason, { proName: ctx.proName, serviceLabel: ctx.serviceLabel, blockedBy: check.blockedBy }));
  }
  if ((check.fitIn || check.outsideHours) && !(ctx.fitIn && canFitIn(ctx.actor))) {
    const c = check.conflicts[0];
    if (c) {
      const age = c.createdAt ? ctx.now.getTime() - new Date(c.createdAt).getTime() : Infinity;
      throw new UserFacingError(c.createdByName && age >= 0 && age <= JUST_TAKEN_MS ? justTakenText(c) : conflictText(ctx.proName, c));
    }
    throw new UserFacingError(refusalText("fora_do_horario", { proName: ctx.proName, serviceLabel: ctx.serviceLabel }));
  }
  return { fitIn: check.fitIn, outsideHours: check.outsideHours };
}

/** 6.6, última linha: o mesmo horário (cliente, início e serviço) que já existe não é criado de novo. */
async function assertNotDuplicateTx(tx: Tx, contactId: string, items: Planned[]) {
  for (const p of items) {
    const same = await tx.select({ id: appointments.id, origin: appointments.origin, serviceId: appointments.serviceId, serviceLabel: appointments.serviceLabel, clientName: appointments.clientName, contactName: contacts.name })
      .from(appointments).leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId))
      .where(and(eq(appointments.contactId, contactId), eq(appointments.startsAt, p.start), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf), inArray(appointments.status, [...ACTIVE_STATUSES, "compareceu"])));
    const dup = same.find((a) => (a.serviceId ? a.serviceId === p.service.id : normText(a.serviceLabel) === normText(p.service.label)));
    if (dup) {
      const who = firstName(dup.contactName ?? dup.clientName) ?? "A cliente";
      throw new ExistingAppointmentError(`${who} já tem ${p.service.label} às ${hhmm(minuteOfDay(p.start))}${dup.origin === "avec" ? " (veio do Avec)" : ""}. Abrir o horário`, dup.id, dayOf(p.start));
    }
  }
}

/** "Qualquer uma": a primeira livre (sem encaixe, no horário de trabalho) na ordem da regra 9. `exclude` = a do serviço "ao mesmo tempo". */
async function pickAnyPro(tx: Tx, p: Planned, clientId: string, now: Date, cands: { id: string; name: string; position: number | null }[], exclude: string | null): Promise<string> {
  if (!cands.length) throw new UserFacingError(`Ninguém está marcado para fazer ${p.service.label}. Marque em Configurações › Profissionais.`);
  const counts = await proDayCounts(tx, cands.map((c) => c.id), dayOf(p.start));
  const scored = [];
  for (const c of cands.filter((x) => x.id !== exclude)) {
    const check = await checkSlotTx(tx, { professionalId: c.id, startsAt: p.start, durationMin: p.durationMin, bufferMin: p.service.bufferMin, clientId, now });
    const kind = check.ok ? (check.outsideHours ? "fora_do_horario" : check.fitIn ? "encaixe" : "livre") as const : null;
    scored.push({ ...c, dayCount: counts.get(c.id) ?? 0, kind });
  }
  const id = pickAny(scored); // a regra do 6A: só as livres; menos horários no dia, depois a ordem da agenda, depois o nome
  if (id) return id;
  throw new UserFacingError(`Ninguém que faz ${p.service.label} está livre às ${hhmm(minuteOfDay(p.start))}. Escolha a profissional para encaixar.`);
}

export async function createVisitTx(tx: Tx, raw: CreateVisitInput, actor: Actor, now: Date): Promise<CreateVisitResult & { dealIds: string[] }> {
  const input = createVisitSchema.parse(raw);
  await assertCanBook(tx);
  const client = await resolveClientTx(tx, input.client);
  if (input.dealId) {
    const [d] = await tx.select({ contactId: deals.contactId }).from(deals).where(eq(deals.id, input.dealId));
    if (d?.contactId !== client.id) throw new UserFacingError("Conversa não encontrada para essa cliente.");
  }
  const planned = await planItems(tx, input, actor);
  const day = dayOf(planned[0].start);
  const candidates = new Map<number, { id: string; name: string; position: number | null }[]>();
  for (const [k, p] of planned.entries()) if (!p.professionalId) candidates.set(k, await prosForService(tx, p.service.id));
  // 6.4, passos 2 e 3: trava (profissionais, candidatas do "Qualquer uma" e a cliente, no dia) e só então relê o banco.
  await lockAgendaTx(tx, { professionalIds: [...planned.map((p) => p.professionalId), ...[...candidates.values()].flat().map((c) => c.id)], contactId: client.id, days: [day] });
  const [again] = await tx.select({ id: visits.id }).from(visits).where(eq(visits.requestKey, input.requestKey));
  if (again) {
    const ids = await tx.select({ id: appointments.id }).from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.visitId, again.id)).orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt));
    return { visitId: again.id, appointmentIds: ids.map((r) => r.id), day, reused: true, dealIds: [] };
  }
  await assertNotDuplicateTx(tx, client.id, planned);
  const explicit = planned.map((p) => p.professionalId).filter((x): x is string => !!x);
  const names = new Map(explicit.length ? (await tx.select({ id: professionals.id, name: professionals.name }).from(professionals).where(inArray(professionals.id, explicit))).map((p) => [p.id, p.name]) : []);
  const decided: { professionalId: string; fitIn: boolean; outsideHours: boolean }[] = [];
  for (const [k, p] of planned.entries()) {
    if (!p.professionalId) {
      const exclude = p.mode === "junto" ? decided[k - 1]?.professionalId ?? null : null;
      decided.push({ professionalId: await pickAnyPro(tx, p, client.id, now, candidates.get(k) ?? [], exclude), fitIn: false, outsideHours: false });
      continue;
    }
    const check = await checkSlotTx(tx, { professionalId: p.professionalId, startsAt: p.start, durationMin: p.durationMin, bufferMin: p.service.bufferMin, clientId: client.id, now });
    decided.push({ professionalId: p.professionalId, ...decideBooking(check, { proName: names.get(p.professionalId) ?? "A profissional", serviceLabel: p.service.label, fitIn: !!input.fitIn, actor, now }) });
  }
  const visitId = randomUUID();
  await tx.insert(visits).values({ id: visitId, contactId: client.id, clientName: client.name, day, dealId: input.dealId ?? null, origin: "crm", requestKey: input.requestKey, createdBy: actor.id, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now });
  const appointmentIds: string[] = [];
  for (const [k, p] of planned.entries()) {
    const id = randomUUID();
    const d = decided[k];
    await tx.insert(appointments).values({
      id, externalKey: `crm:${id}`, origin: "crm", visitId, contactId: client.id, clientName: client.name, professionalId: d.professionalId,
      serviceId: p.service.id, serviceLabel: p.service.label, startsAt: p.start, durationMin: p.durationMin, bufferMin: p.service.bufferMin, priceCents: p.service.priceCents,
      status: input.walkIn ? "chegou" : "agendado", arrivedAt: input.walkIn ? now : null, notes: input.notes || null,
      fitIn: d.fitIn, fitInBy: d.fitIn ? actor.id : null, outsideHours: d.outsideHours, anyPro: !p.professionalId,
      createdBy: actor.id, updatedBy: actor.id, crmTouchedAt: now, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
    });
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actor.id, action: "agendamento_marcado", entity: "appointment", entityId: id, createdAt: now,
      data: { visitId, inicio: p.start.toISOString(), profissional: d.professionalId, servico: p.service.label, encaixe: d.fitIn, foraDoHorario: d.outsideHours, qualquerUma: !p.professionalId, chegou: !!input.walkIn } });
    appointmentIds.push(id);
  }
  const dealIds = await agendaFunnelTx(tx, [client.id], now);
  return { visitId, appointmentIds, day, reused: false, dealIds };
}

/** Marcar (6.1): uma visita com um ou mais serviços. Mandar o mesmo formulário duas vezes devolve a mesma visita. */
export async function createVisit(raw: CreateVisitInput, actor: Actor, now = new Date()): Promise<CreateVisitResult> {
  const r = await db.transaction((tx) => createVisitTx(tx, raw, actor, now));
  if (!r.reused) await afterAgendaChange([r.day], r.dealIds);
  return { visitId: r.visitId, appointmentIds: r.appointmentIds, day: r.day, reused: r.reused };
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: Acrescentar ao fim de `visits.ts`**

Acrescentar `gte, lt, or` ao import do drizzle e `checkCancel, CANCEL_REASONS` ao import de `@/lib/appointment-status`, e `instantOf` ao de `@/lib/agenda`. Depois:

```ts
/* ───────────── Remarcar ───────────── */

export const rescheduleSchema = z.object({
  startsAt: z.string().datetime({ offset: true, message: "Escolha o dia e a hora." }),
  professionalId: z.string().uuid().optional(),
  durationMin: z.number().int().nullable().optional(),
  fitIn: z.boolean().optional(),
});
export type RescheduleInput = z.input<typeof rescheduleSchema>;

/** A visita da cliente naquele dia (a primeira que existir), ou uma nova. */
export async function visitForDayTx(tx: Tx, ref: { contactId: string | null; clientName: string | null }, day: string, actorId: string | null, now: Date, origin: "crm" | "avec" = "crm"): Promise<string> {
  const same = ref.contactId ? eq(visits.contactId, ref.contactId) : and(isNull(visits.contactId), eq(visits.clientName, ref.clientName ?? ""));
  const [v] = await tx.select({ id: visits.id }).from(visits).where(and(same, eq(visits.day, day))).orderBy(asc(visits.createdAt)).limit(1);
  if (v) return v.id;
  const [n] = await tx.insert(visits).values({ contactId: ref.contactId, clientName: ref.clientName, day, origin, createdBy: actorId, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now }).returning({ id: visits.id });
  return n.id;
}

/**
 * Remarcar (6.4, regra 9): muda o mesmo registro (início, profissional, duração), volta para Agendado e apaga a confirmação. Recusado no
 * modo consulta. Horário do Avec remarcado aqui passa a ser do CRM (regra 16). Mudando de dia, sai da visita antiga.
 */
export async function rescheduleAppointment(id: string, raw: RescheduleInput, actor: Actor, now = new Date()): Promise<{ day: string; previousDay: string }> {
  const input = rescheduleSchema.parse(raw);
  const r = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    await assertCanBook(tx);
    const [a0] = await tx.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, z.string().uuid().parse(id)));
    if (!a0 || a0.avecTwinOf) throw new UserFacingError("Horário não encontrado.");
    const startsAt = new Date(input.startsAt);
    const proId = input.professionalId ?? a0.professionalId;
    if (!proId) throw new UserFacingError("Escolha a profissional.");
    const previousDay = dayOf(a0.startsAt), day = dayOf(startsAt);
    await lockAgendaTx(tx, { professionalIds: [a0.professionalId, proId], contactId: a0.contactId, days: [previousDay, day] });
    const [a] = await tx.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, a0.id)).for("update");
    if (!["agendado", "confirmado", "solicitado"].includes(a.status)) throw new UserFacingError("Só dá para remarcar um horário agendado ou confirmado.");
    if (a.serviceId) await assertProfessionalFor(tx, proId, a.serviceId);
    const [svc] = a.serviceId ? await tx.select({ durationMin: lists.durationMin, bufferMin: lists.bufferMin }).from(lists).where(eq(lists.id, a.serviceId)) : [];
    const durationMin = input.durationMin ?? a.durationMin ?? svc?.durationMin ?? null;
    if (durationMin == null) throw new UserFacingError(refusalText("sem_duracao", { proName: "", serviceLabel: a.serviceLabel }));
    if (durationMin < DURATION_STEP || durationMin > DURATION_MAX || durationMin % DURATION_STEP) throw new UserFacingError(refusalText("duracao_invalida", { proName: "", serviceLabel: a.serviceLabel }));
    const bufferMin = a.bufferMin ?? svc?.bufferMin ?? null;
    const [pro] = await tx.select({ name: professionals.name }).from(professionals).where(eq(professionals.id, proId));
    const check = await checkSlotTx(tx, { professionalId: proId, startsAt, durationMin, bufferMin, clientId: a.contactId, ignoreAppointmentId: a.id, now });
    const d = decideBooking(check, { proName: pro?.name ?? "A profissional", serviceLabel: a.serviceLabel, fitIn: !!input.fitIn, actor, now });
    const visitId = a.visitId && previousDay === day ? a.visitId : await visitForDayTx(tx, { contactId: a.contactId, clientName: a.clientName }, day, actor.id, now);
    await tx.update(appointments).set({
      startsAt, professionalId: proId, durationMin, bufferMin, status: "agendado", confirmedAt: null, confirmedVia: null, visitId,
      fitIn: d.fitIn, fitInBy: d.fitIn ? actor.id : null, outsideHours: d.outsideHours, anyPro: false, updatedBy: actor.id, updatedAt: now, crmTouchedAt: now,
    }).where(eq(appointments.id, a.id));
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actor.id, action: "agendamento_remarcado", entity: "appointment", entityId: a.id, createdAt: now, data: {
      de: { inicio: a.startsAt.toISOString(), profissional: a.professionalId, duracao: a.durationMin, status: a.status },
      para: { inicio: startsAt.toISOString(), profissional: proId, duracao: durationMin, status: "agendado" },
    } });
    const dealIds = await agendaFunnelTx(tx, [a.contactId], now);
    return { day, previousDay, dealIds };
  });
  await afterAgendaChange([r.day, r.previousDay], r.dealIds);
  return { day: r.day, previousDay: r.previousDay };
}

/* ───────────── Cancelar ───────────── */

export const cancelSchema = z.object({
  appointmentId: z.string().uuid(),
  scope: z.enum(["este", "visita"]).default("este"),
  reason: z.enum(CANCEL_REASONS, { message: "Escolha o motivo." }),
  note: z.string().trim().max(200, "Use até 200 caracteres no motivo.").nullable().optional(),
  via: z.enum(["recepcao", "link"]).default("recepcao"),
}).refine((v) => v.reason !== "outro" || !!v.note, { message: "Escreva o motivo.", path: ["note"] });
export type CancelInput = z.input<typeof cancelSchema>;

/**
 * Cancelar (6.4, regra 10): motivo, quem e por onde; nunca apaga. "A visita toda" cancela os horários ainda abertos da visita.
 * Vale em qualquer modo da agenda. O 6C acrescenta aqui a trava da visita já paga e a saída do item da comanda aberta.
 */
export async function cancelAppointmentsTx(tx: Tx, raw: CancelInput, actorId: string | null, now: Date) {
  const input = cancelSchema.parse(raw);
  const [a] = await tx.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, input.appointmentId)).for("update");
  if (!a || a.avecTwinOf) throw new UserFacingError("Horário não encontrado.");
  const day = dayOf(a.startsAt);
  const first = checkCancel(a.status);
  if (!first.ok) throw new UserFacingError(first.error);
  const targets = input.scope === "visita" && a.visitId
    ? await tx.select().from(appointments).where(and(eq(appointments.visitId, a.visitId), inArray(appointments.status, [...ACTIVE_STATUSES]), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf))).for("update")
    : [a];
  for (const t of targets) { const c = checkCancel(t.status); if (!c.ok) throw new UserFacingError(c.error); }
  for (const t of targets) {
    await tx.update(appointments).set({ status: "cancelado", cancelledAt: now, cancelReason: input.reason, cancelNote: input.note || null, cancelledBy: actorId, cancelledVia: input.via,
      updatedBy: actorId, updatedAt: now, crmTouchedAt: now }).where(eq(appointments.id, t.id));
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actorId, action: "agendamento_situacao", entity: "appointment", entityId: t.id, createdAt: now,
      data: { de: t.status, para: "cancelado", motivo: input.reason, nota: input.note || null, via: input.via } });
  }
  const dealIds = await agendaFunnelTx(tx, [a.contactId], now);
  return { cancelledIds: targets.map((t) => t.id), day, contactId: a.contactId, dealIds };
}

export async function cancelAppointments(raw: CancelInput, actor: Actor | null, now = new Date()): Promise<{ cancelled: number; day: string }> {
  const r = await db.transaction((tx) => cancelAppointmentsTx(tx, raw, actor?.id ?? null, now));
  await afterAgendaChange([r.day], r.dealIds);
  return { cancelled: r.cancelledIds.length, day: r.day };
}

/* ───────────── Visita sob demanda ───────────── */

/**
 * A visita do horário (4.1). Horário do Avec sem visita ganha uma agora, juntando os horários ativos (e finalizados) da mesma cliente
 * naquele dia. Não marca o horário como mexido no CRM: a visita é só a ligação. Usado pela comanda (6C) e pelo link da cliente (7B).
 */
export async function ensureVisitFor(appointmentId: string, q?: Tx): Promise<string> {
  const run = async (tx: Tx) => {
    const [a] = await tx.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, z.string().uuid().parse(appointmentId))).for("update");
    if (!a || a.avecTwinOf) throw new UserFacingError("Horário não encontrado.");
    if (a.visitId) return a.visitId;
    const day = dayOf(a.startsAt);
    const visitId = await visitForDayTx(tx, { contactId: a.contactId, clientName: a.clientName }, day, null, new Date(), a.origin === "avec" ? "avec" : "crm");
    const sameClient = a.contactId ? eq(appointments.contactId, a.contactId) : and(isNull(appointments.contactId), eq(appointments.clientName, a.clientName ?? ""));
    await tx.update(appointments).set({ visitId }).where(and(sameClient, isNull(appointments.visitId), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf),
      gte(appointments.startsAt, instantOf(day, 0)), lt(appointments.startsAt, instantOf(day, 1440)),
      or(eq(appointments.id, a.id), inArray(appointments.status, [...ACTIVE_STATUSES, "compareceu"]))));
    return visitId;
  };
  return q ? run(q) : db.transaction(run);
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: Desativar com horários futuros (`professionals.ts`, ação e tela)**

```ts
const DM_HM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });

// assinatura atual (6A, professionals.ts:128) ganha `force` e `now`:
export async function updateProfessional(id: string, patch: { name?: string; fullName?: string | null; role?: string | null; serviceIds?: string[]; active?: boolean; force?: boolean }, userId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<void> {
  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    // 6.4, regra 14: desativar com horários futuros é recusado com a lista; a gestão pode forçar, e fica registrado.
    if (patch.active === false) {
      const future = await tx.select({ startsAt: appointments.startsAt, clientName: appointments.clientName, contactName: contacts.name })
        .from(appointments).leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId))
        .where(and(eq(appointments.professionalId, id), gt(appointments.startsAt, now), inArray(appointments.status, ["agendado", "confirmado", "solicitado"]), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf)))
        .orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt));
      if (future.length && !patch.force) {
        const lista = future.slice(0, 5).map((f) => `${firstName(f.contactName ?? f.clientName) ?? "Cliente"} ${DM_HM.format(f.startsAt).replace(",", "")}`).join(", ");
        throw new UserFacingError(`Ela tem ${future.length} ${future.length === 1 ? "horário marcado" : "horários marcados"}: remarque antes. ${lista}${future.length > 5 ? ", …" : ""}`);
      }
      if (future.length) await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId, action: "profissional_desativada_com_horarios", entity: "professional", entityId: id, data: { horarios: future.length } });
    }
    const { force: _force, ...rest } = patch;
    // … o resto da função continua igual, usando `rest` no lugar de `patch` (inclusive no audit "editou_profissional")
```

`DM_HM.format` dá "16/10, 10:00" em pt-BR; o `replace(",", "")` deixa "16/10 10:00".

Em `src/app/(app)/config/actions.ts:69`, `proPatch` ganha `force: z.boolean().optional()`.

```ts
const pedido = (contactId: string, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
  ({ client: { contactId }, serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: null, day: "2026-10-16", window: "manha" as const, ...over });

it("pôr na lista: no dia pedido às 00:00 de Salvador, com a janela, quem pôs e a chave do CRM", async () => {
  const p = await cliente();
  const { id } = await addToWaitlist(pedido(p.contactId, { day: "2026-10-17", window: "tarde", notes: "prefere depois das 15h" }), s.recepcao, NOW);
  const w = await get(id);
  expect(w).toMatchObject({ status: "espera", waitWindow: "tarde", externalKey: `crm:esp:${id}`, origin: "crm", createdBy: s.cati.id, serviceLabel: "Corte", notes: "prefere depois das 15h" });
  expect(w.startsAt.toISOString()).toBe("2026-10-17T03:00:00.000Z");
  await expect(addToWaitlist(pedido(p.contactId, { day: "2026-10-14" }), s.recepcao, NOW)).rejects.toThrow("Escolha hoje ou um dia que ainda não passou.");
});

it("Encaixar: cria o horário e o pedido vira espera_encaixada, apontando o horário novo", async () => {

  expect(r.appointmentIds).toHaveLength(1);
  expect(await get(id)).toMatchObject({ status: "espera_encaixada", waitResultAppointmentId: r.appointmentIds[0] });
  expect(await get(r.appointmentIds[0])).toMatchObject({ status: "agendado", contactId: p.contactId, professionalId: s.bia.id });
});

});

});

});

  await addToWaitlist(pedido(r0.contactId, { day: "2026-10-17" }), s.recepcao, bahia(15, 9, 2));
  expect((await waitlistMatchesFor("2026-10-16")).map((m) => m.clientName)).toEqual(["Paula Souza", "Joana Lima"]);
  expect((await waitlistMatchesFor("2026-10-16", { professionalId: s.ana.id, serviceId: s.corte.id })).map((m) => m.clientName)).toEqual(["Paula Souza"]);
});
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `src/server/domain/agenda/waitlist.ts`**

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { and, asc, eq, gte, isNull, lt, or } from "drizzle-orm";
import { z } from "zod";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { appointments, auditLog, contacts, lists, professionals } from "../../db/schema";
import { UserFacingError } from "../errors";
import { assertProfessionalFor } from "../professionals";
import { dayOf, instantOf, todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { WAIT_WINDOWS } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { assertCanBook } from "./mode";
import { afterAgendaChange } from "./notify";
import { createVisitTx, resolveClientTx, type ClientRef, type CreateVisitInput } from "./visits";
import type { Actor } from "./actor";

/**
 * Lista de espera (6.4, regra 13): o pedido fica como `espera`, no dia desejado às 00:00 (como o importado do Avec). "Encaixar" cria o
 * horário pela porta de marcar e o pedido vira `espera_encaixada`; "Desistiu" vira `espera_desistiu`. Nada se apaga, e a reimportação não
 * devolve o pedido para a espera (ele fica como mexido no CRM).
 */
export const addWaitlistSchema = z.object({
  client: z.custom<ClientRef>((v) => !!v && typeof v === "object", "Escolha a cliente."),
  serviceId: z.string().uuid().nullable(),
  professionalId: z.string().uuid().nullable(),
  day: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, "Escolha o dia."),
  window: z.enum(WAIT_WINDOWS, { message: "Escolha manhã, tarde ou dia todo." }),
  notes: z.string().trim().max(500, "Use até 500 caracteres.").nullable().optional(),
});
export type AddWaitlistInput = z.input<typeof addWaitlistSchema>;
export type ResolveWaitlistInput = { result: "encaixada"; booking: Omit<CreateVisitInput, "client"> } | { result: "desistiu"; note?: string | null };
export type WaitlistMatch = {
  id: string; contactId: string | null; clientName: string; serviceId: string | null; serviceLabel: string;
  professionalId: string | null; professionalName: string | null; window: string | null; notes: string | null; origin: string;
};

export async function addToWaitlist(raw: AddWaitlistInput, actor: Actor, now = new Date()): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  const input = addWaitlistSchema.parse(raw);
  if (input.day < todayBahia(now)) throw new UserFacingError("Escolha hoje ou um dia que ainda não passou.");
  const id = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    await assertCanBook(tx);
    const client = await resolveClientTx(tx, input.client);
    const [svc] = input.serviceId ? await tx.select({ label: lists.label }).from(lists).where(and(eq(lists.id, input.serviceId), eq(lists.kind, "service"))) : [];
    if (input.serviceId && !svc) throw new UserFacingError("Esse serviço não está mais na lista. Escolha outro.");
    if (input.professionalId) await assertProfessionalFor(tx, input.professionalId, input.serviceId);
    const newId = randomUUID();
    await tx.insert(appointments).values({
      id: newId, externalKey: `crm:esp:${newId}`, origin: "crm", contactId: client.id, clientName: client.name, professionalId: input.professionalId,
      serviceId: input.serviceId, serviceLabel: svc?.label ?? "Não informado", startsAt: instantOf(input.day, 0), status: "espera", waitWindow: input.window,
      notes: input.notes || null, createdBy: actor.id, updatedBy: actor.id, crmTouchedAt: now, createdAt: now, updatedAt: now,
    });
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actor.id, action: "agendamento_espera", entity: "appointment", entityId: newId, createdAt: now, data: { dia: input.day, janela: input.window } });
    return newId;
  });
  await afterAgendaChange([input.day]);
  return { id };
}

export async function resolveWaitlist(id: string, input: ResolveWaitlistInput, actor: Actor, now = new Date()): Promise<{ appointmentIds: string[] }> {
  const r = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const [w] = await tx.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, z.string().uuid().parse(id))).for("update");
    if (!w || w.status !== "espera") throw new UserFacingError("Esse pedido já saiu da lista de espera.");
    const day = dayOf(w.startsAt);
    if (input.result === "desistiu") {
      const note = z.string().trim().max(200, "Use até 200 caracteres.").parse(input.note ?? "") || null;
      await tx.update(appointments).set({ status: "espera_desistiu", cancelNote: note, updatedBy: actor.id, updatedAt: now, crmTouchedAt: now }).where(eq(appointments.id, w.id));
      await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actor.id, action: "agendamento_espera_resolvida", entity: "appointment", entityId: w.id, createdAt: now, data: { de: "espera", para: "espera_desistiu" } });
      return { appointmentIds: [] as string[], days: [day], dealIds: [] as string[] };
    }
    if (!w.contactId) throw new UserFacingError("Esse pedido não tem a ficha da cliente: marque pelo botão Marcar.");
    const v = await createVisitTx(tx, { ...input.booking, client: { contactId: w.contactId } }, actor, now);
    await tx.update(appointments).set({ status: "espera_encaixada", waitResultAppointmentId: v.appointmentIds[0], updatedBy: actor.id, updatedAt: now, crmTouchedAt: now }).where(eq(appointments.id, w.id));
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: actor.id, action: "agendamento_espera_resolvida", entity: "appointment", entityId: w.id, createdAt: now,
      data: { de: "espera", para: "espera_encaixada", horario: v.appointmentIds[0] } });
    return { appointmentIds: v.appointmentIds, days: [day, v.day], dealIds: v.dealIds };
  });
  await afterAgendaChange(r.days, r.dealIds);
  return { appointmentIds: r.appointmentIds };
}

/** Quem está na espera para o dia: da profissional (ou qualquer uma) e do serviço (ou sem serviço), na ordem em que pediu. */
export async function waitlistMatchesFor(day: string, opts: { professionalId?: string | null; serviceId?: string | null } = {}): Promise<WaitlistMatch[]> {
  const rows = await db.select({
    id: appointments.id, contactId: appointments.contactId, clientName: appointments.clientName, contactName: contacts.name, serviceId: appointments.serviceId,
    serviceLabel: appointments.serviceLabel, professionalId: appointments.professionalId, professionalName: professionals.name, window: appointments.waitWindow,
    notes: appointments.notes, origin: appointments.origin,
  }).from(appointments).leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId)).leftJoin(professionals, eq(professionals.id, appointments.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(appointments.status, "espera"), gte(appointments.startsAt, instantOf(day, 0)), lt(appointments.startsAt, instantOf(day, 1440)),
      opts.professionalId ? or(isNull(appointments.professionalId), eq(appointments.professionalId, opts.professionalId)) : undefined,
      opts.serviceId ? or(isNull(appointments.serviceId), eq(appointments.serviceId, opts.serviceId)) : undefined))
    .orderBy(asc(appointments.createdAt));
  return rows.map(({ contactName, ...r }) => ({ ...r, clientName: contactName?.trim() || r.clientName?.trim() || "Sem nome" }));
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { asc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { appointments } from "../../db/schema";
import { resetDb } from "../../../../test/db";
import { bahia, cliente, NOW, setupAgenda } from "../../../../test/agenda-fixtures";
import { applyAvecFiles } from "../avec/apply";

import { setAppointmentStatus } from "../appointments";

import { agendaRange } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { checkSlotTx } from "./slots";

import { resolveWaitlist } from "./waitlist";
import { listAvecDivergences } from "./twins";

// Planilhas SINTÉTICAS, com os cabeçalhos dos formatos reais do Avec (os mesmos de avec/apply.test.ts).
const H_RES = ["Data Cadastro Reserva", "Data Reserva", "Hora", "Cliente", "Celular", "Data Cadastro Cliente", "E-mail", "Profissional", "Serviço", "Origem", "Status", "Observação", "Data Comanda", "Número", "Quem Cadastrou"];
const H_ESP = ["Data", "Cliente", "Telefone", "Profissional", "Serviço", "Obs", "Responsavel", "Responsavel última atualização", "Data Cadastro", "Última atualização"];
const reservas = async (rows: (string | number)[][]) => ({ fileName: "reservas.xlsx", rows: await readXlsx(await buildXlsx([H_RES, ...rows])) });
const linha = (o: { cliente: string; celular: string; hora?: string; pro?: string; servico?: string; status?: string }) =>
  ["14/10/2026 10:00", "16/10/2026", o.hora ?? "10:00", o.cliente, Number(o.celular), "", "", o.pro ?? "ANA", o.servico ?? "Corte", "Interno", o.status ?? "Agendado", "", "", "", "Recepção"];
const DIA = { from: bahia(16, 0), to: bahia(17, 0) };

let s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setupAgenda>>;
beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(); s = await setupAgenda(); });
const book = (contactId: string, over: Partial<CreateVisitInput> = {}): CreateVisitInput => ({
  requestKey: randomUUID(), client: { contactId }, startsAt: bahia(16, 10).toISOString(), items: [{ serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.ana.id }], ...over,
});
const get = async (id: string) => (await db.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.id, id)))[0];
const avecRows = () => db.select().from(appointments).where(eq(appointments.origin, "avec"));

describe("Review Focus 1: o mesmo horário no CRM e no Avec (modo dupla)", () => {
  it("a reserva do Avec vira gêmea: some da grade, não ocupa, e o do CRM não muda nem com 'Cancelado' lá; a diferença vai para Conferir", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    const crmId = (await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW)).appointmentIds[0];
    await applyAvecFiles([await reservas([linha({ cliente: "Paula Souza", celular: p.digits })])]);
    const [twin] = await avecRows();
    expect(twin.avecTwinOf).toBe(crmId);
    expect((await getAgenda(agendaRange({ d: "2026-10-16" }, NOW), NOW)).filter((i) => i.name === "Paula Souza")).toHaveLength(1);
    const q = await cliente("Joana Lima");
    const check = await checkSlotTx(db, { professionalId: s.ana.id, startsAt: bahia(16, 10), durationMin: 60, bufferMin: null, clientId: q.contactId, now: NOW });
    expect(check.ok && check.conflicts.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual([crmId]);
    await applyAvecFiles([await reservas([linha({ cliente: "Paula Souza", celular: p.digits, status: "Cancelado" })])]);
    expect((await get(crmId)).status).toBe("agendado");
    expect((await get(twin.id)).status).toBe("cancelado");
    expect((await listAvecDivergences(DIA)).map((d) => [d.kind, d.appointmentId, d.avecAppointmentId])).toEqual([["status_diferente", crmId, twin.id]]);
  });

    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW);

    const before = await db.select().from(appointments).orderBy(asc(appointments.id));
    await applyAvecFiles([file]);
    expect(await db.select().from(appointments).orderBy(asc(appointments.id))).toEqual(before);
  });

  it("serviços diferentes no mesmo horário (mão e pé) não são gêmeos", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW);
    await applyAvecFiles([await reservas([linha({ cliente: "Paula Souza", celular: p.digits, servico: "Hidratação", pro: "BIA" })])]);
    expect((await avecRows())[0].avecTwinOf).toBeNull();
  });

  it("remarcado só de um lado: horário diferente para a mesma cliente no mesmo dia vai para Conferir com o Avec", async () => {
    const p = await cliente("Paula Souza");
    const id = (await createVisit(book(p.contactId), s.recepcao, NOW)).appointmentIds[0];
    await rescheduleAppointment(id, { startsAt: bahia(16, 14).toISOString() }, s.recepcao, NOW);
    await applyAvecFiles([await reservas([linha({ cliente: "Paula Souza", celular: p.digits })])]);
    expect(await listAvecDivergences(DIA)).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({
      kind: "horario_diferente", appointmentId: id, crmStartsAt: bahia(16, 14).toISOString(), avecStartsAt: bahia(16, 10).toISOString(), clientName: "Paula Souza",
    })]);
  });
});

});
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `mergeAppointment` (regra 15)**

Em `src/server/domain/avec/import-agenda.ts`, trocar `mergeAppointment` por:

```ts
/**
 * Regra 15 (6.4): horário mexido no CRM (`crmTouchedAt`) não é tocado pela importação: nem status, nem observação, nem profissional.
 * Só guarda em `avecStatus` o que o Avec diz agora (a diferença aparece em "Conferir com o Avec"). Os outros seguem a regra de sempre:
 * completar o vazio e o status só sobe.
 */
function mergeAppointment(old: Record<string, unknown>, inc: NewAppointment): Partial<NewAppointment> {
  if (old.crmTouchedAt) {
    const avec = inc.avecStatus ?? inc.status;
    return old.avecStatus === avec ? {} : { avecStatus: avec };
  }
  const patch: Partial<NewAppointment> = fillEmpty(old, inc, FILL);
  const oldRank = RANK[old.status as AppointmentStatus] ?? 0, newRank = RANK[inc.status as AppointmentStatus] ?? 0;
  if (newRank > oldRank) { patch.status = inc.status; patch.avecStatus = inc.avecStatus ?? null; }
  return patch;
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/server/domain/agenda/twins.ts`**

```ts
import { and, eq, gte, isNotNull, isNull, lt, ne, notInArray, or, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db, type Tx } from "../../db";
import { appointments, auditLog, contacts, settings } from "../../db/schema";
import { normText } from "../avec/normalize";
import { dayOf } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { statusGroup } from "@/lib/appointment-status";

/** O relógio do banco: o `created_at` das linhas importadas vem dele, então "o que acabou de entrar" se mede por ele. */
export async function dbNow(q: Tx | typeof db = db): Promise<Date> {
  const r = await q.execute(sql`select now() as now`);
  return new Date((r.rows[0] as { now: string | Date }).now);
}

type Ref = { contactId: string | null; clientName: string | null; contactName?: string | null; serviceId: string | null; serviceLabel: string };
const sameClient = (a: Ref, b: Ref) => (a.contactId && b.contactId ? a.contactId === b.contactId : normText(a.contactName ?? a.clientName) === normText(b.contactName ?? b.clientName));
const sameService = (a: Ref, b: Ref) => (a.serviceId && b.serviceId ? a.serviceId === b.serviceId : normText(a.serviceLabel) === normText(b.serviceLabel));
const WAIT = ["espera", "espera_encaixada", "espera_desistiu"];

/**
 * Gêmeos (6.4, regra 11): cada reserva do Avec que acabou de entrar (criada a partir de `from`) e é o mesmo horário de um registro do CRM
 * (criado aqui, ou do Avec e mexido aqui) — mesma cliente, mesmo início, mesmo serviço — vira gêmea dele: some da grade, não ocupa,
 * não entra no funil e nunca muda o do CRM. Casa com o registro do CRM em qualquer status (menos lista de espera), preferindo um aberto:
 * uma reserva esquecida no Avec nunca reativa o horário nem o funil. Serviços diferentes no mesmo horário não são gêmeos.
 */
export async function pairAvecTwins(tx: Tx, opts: { from: Date }): Promise<{ paired: number }> {
  const fresh = await tx.select({ id: appointments.id, contactId: appointments.contactId, clientName: appointments.clientName, contactName: contacts.name,
    serviceId: appointments.serviceId, serviceLabel: appointments.serviceLabel, startsAt: appointments.startsAt })
    .from(appointments).leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId))
    .where(and(eq(appointments.origin, "avec"), gte(appointments.createdAt, opts.from), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf), isNull(appointments.crmTouchedAt), notInArray(appointments.status, WAIT)));
  let paired = 0;
  for (const f of fresh) {
    const cands = await tx.select({ id: appointments.id, contactId: appointments.contactId, clientName: appointments.clientName, contactName: contacts.name,
      serviceId: appointments.serviceId, serviceLabel: appointments.serviceLabel, status: appointments.status })
      .from(appointments).leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId))
      .where(and(eq(appointments.startsAt, f.startsAt), ne(appointments.id, f.id), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf), notInArray(appointments.status, WAIT),
        or(eq(appointments.origin, "crm"), isNotNull(appointments.crmTouchedAt))));
    const twin = cands.filter((c) => sameClient(f, c) && sameService(f, c))
      .sort((a, b) => Number(statusGroup(b.status) === "ativo") - Number(statusGroup(a.status) === "ativo"))[0];
    if (!twin) continue;
    await tx.update(appointments).set({ avecTwinOf: twin.id }).where(eq(appointments.id, f.id));
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId: null, action: "avec_gemeo", entity: "appointment", entityId: f.id, data: { de: null, para: twin.id } });
    paired++;
  }
  return { paired };
}

export type DivergenceKind = "horario_diferente" | "status_diferente" | "criado_depois_da_virada";
export const DIVERGENCE_LABEL: Record<DivergenceKind, string> = {
  horario_diferente: "Horário diferente nos dois sistemas", status_diferente: "Situação diferente no Avec", criado_depois_da_virada: "Criado no Avec depois da virada para só CRM",
};
export type AvecDivergence = {
  kind: DivergenceKind; appointmentId: string; avecAppointmentId: string | null; day: string; clientName: string; serviceLabel: string;
  crmStartsAt: string | null; avecStartsAt: string | null; crmStatus: string | null; avecStatus: string | null;
};

/** "Conferir com o Avec" (6.4, regra 12): só leitura. A recepção decide o que fazer; o sistema não corrige sozinho. */
export async function listAvecDivergences(range: { from: Date; to: Date }): Promise<AvecDivergence[]> {
  const rows = await db.select({
    id: appointments.id, contactId: appointments.contactId, clientName: appointments.clientName, contactName: contacts.name, serviceId: appointments.serviceId,
    serviceLabel: appointments.serviceLabel, startsAt: appointments.startsAt, status: appointments.status, avecStatus: appointments.avecStatus, origin: appointments.origin,
    crmTouchedAt: appointments.crmTouchedAt, avecTwinOf: appointments.avecTwinOf, createdAt: appointments.createdAt,
  }).from(appointments).leftJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, appointments.contactId))
    .where(and(gte(appointments.startsAt, range.from), lt(appointments.startsAt, range.to), notInArray(appointments.status, WAIT)));
  type Row = (typeof rows)[number];
  const byId = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const nameOf = (r: Row) => r.contactName?.trim() || r.clientName?.trim() || "Sem nome";
  const base = (x: Row, y: Row | null) => ({ appointmentId: x.id, avecAppointmentId: y?.id ?? null, day: dayOf(x.startsAt), clientName: nameOf(x), serviceLabel: x.serviceLabel,
    crmStartsAt: x.startsAt.toISOString(), avecStartsAt: (y ?? x).startsAt.toISOString(), crmStatus: x.status });
  const out: AvecDivergence[] = [];
  for (const y of rows.filter((r) => r.avecTwinOf)) {
    const x = byId.get(y.avecTwinOf!);
    if (x && statusGroup(y.status) !== statusGroup(x.status)) out.push({ kind: "status_diferente", ...base(x, y), avecStatus: y.status });
  }
  for (const x of rows.filter((r) => r.origin === "avec" && r.crmTouchedAt && !r.avecTwinOf && r.avecStatus)) {
    if (statusGroup(x.avecStatus) !== statusGroup(x.status)) out.push({ kind: "status_diferente", ...base(x, null), avecStatus: x.avecStatus });
  }
  const crmOwned = rows.filter((r) => !r.avecTwinOf && (r.origin === "crm" || r.crmTouchedAt) && statusGroup(r.status) === "ativo");
  const avecPlain = rows.filter((r) => r.origin === "avec" && !r.crmTouchedAt && !r.avecTwinOf && statusGroup(r.status) === "ativo");
  for (const x of crmOwned) for (const y of avecPlain) {
    if (dayOf(x.startsAt) === dayOf(y.startsAt) && x.startsAt.getTime() !== y.startsAt.getTime() && sameClient(x, y) && sameService(x, y)) {
      out.push({ kind: "horario_diferente", ...base(x, y), avecStatus: y.status });
    }
  }
  const [mode] = await db.select().from(settings).where(eq(settings.key, "agenda_modo"));
  if (mode?.value === "so_crm") {
    for (const y of rows.filter((r) => r.origin === "avec" && !r.avecTwinOf && r.createdAt > mode.updatedAt)) out.push({ kind: "criado_depois_da_virada", ...base(y, y), avecStatus: y.status });
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => a.day.localeCompare(b.day) || a.clientName.localeCompare(b.clientName, "pt-BR"));
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { db } from "../db";
import { professionals } from "../db/schema";
import { resetDb } from "../../../test/db";
import { ap, bahia, CASA_TER_SAB, cliente, NOW, setSetting, setupAgenda } from "../../../test/agenda-fixtures";
import { agendaRange } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { getAgendaWeekByPro } from "./agenda-week";

beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(); });

it("Review Focus 3: semana que atravessa o fim do mês, com clientes, horas e folgas por profissional", async () => {
  const s = await setupAgenda();
  await setSetting("business_hours", CASA_TER_SAB);
  await db.insert(professionals).values({ name: "Carla" }); // sem horário próprio: segue a Casa (ter–sáb)
  const p = await cliente("Paula Souza"), q = await cliente("Joana Lima");
  await ap({ startsAt: bahia(31, 10), contactId: p.contactId, professionalId: s.ana.id });
  await ap({ startsAt: bahia(31, 11), contactId: q.contactId, professionalId: s.ana.id, serviceId: s.hidratacao.id, serviceLabel: "Hidratação", durationMin: null });
  await ap({ startsAt: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 10, 1, 12)), contactId: p.contactId, professionalId: s.ana.id }); // 01/11, 09:00 em Salvador
  const crm = await ap({ startsAt: bahia(30, 10), contactId: p.contactId, professionalId: s.ana.id, status: "cancelado" });
  await ap({ startsAt: bahia(30, 10), contactId: p.contactId, professionalId: s.ana.id, avecTwinOf: crm.id });
  const rows = await getAgendaWeekByPro(agendaRange({ d: "2026-10-29", v: "semana" }, NOW));
  expect(rows.map((r) => r.name)).toEqual(["Ana", "Bia", "Carla"]);
  const ana = rows[0];
  expect(ana.cells.map((c) => c.day)).toEqual(["2026-10-26", "2026-10-27", "2026-10-28", "2026-10-29", "2026-10-30", "2026-10-31", "2026-11-01"]);
  expect(ana.cells.find((c) => c.day === "2026-10-31")).toEqual({ day: "2026-10-31", clients: 2, minutes: 105, off: false });
  expect(ana.cells.find((c) => c.day === "2026-11-01")).toMatchObject({ clients: 1, minutes: 60 });
  expect(ana.cells.find((c) => c.day === "2026-10-30")).toMatchObject({ clients: 0, minutes: 0 });
  expect(rows[2].cells.filter((c) => c.off).map((c) => c.day)).toEqual(["2026-10-26", "2026-11-01"]);
});
```

```ts
it("a agenda de uma cliente: próximos, anteriores e cancelados, sem gêmeos nem lista de espera", async () => {
  const [c] = await db.insert(contacts).values({ name: "Paula Souza", phoneE164: "+5571988000101" }).returning();
  const now = bahia(15, 9);
  const futuro = await ap({ startsAt: bahia(20, 10), contactId: c.id });
  await ap({ startsAt: bahia(20, 10), contactId: c.id, avecTwinOf: futuro.id });
  const passado = await ap({ startsAt: bahia(10, 10), contactId: c.id, status: "compareceu" });
  const cancelado = await ap({ startsAt: bahia(18, 10), contactId: c.id, status: "cancelado" });
  await ap({ startsAt: bahia(22, 0), contactId: c.id, status: "espera" });
  const r = await clientAgenda(c.id, now);
  expect([r.futuros.map((x) => x.appointmentId), r.passados.map((x) => x.appointmentId), r.cancelados.map((x) => x.appointmentId)]).toEqual([[futuro.id], [passado.id], [cancelado.id]]);
  expect(r.futuros[0]).toMatchObject({ day: "2026-10-20", serviceLabel: "Corte" });
});
```
