- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/server/queries/agenda.ts`**

Substituir o arquivo por:

```ts
import { and, asc, eq, gte, inArray, isNull, lt, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { alias } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { agendaStatus, appointmentAgendaStatus, dayOf, entryDuration, minuteOfDay, type AgendaRange, type AgendaStatus } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { db } from "../db";
import { appointments, contacts, deals, lists, professionals, salesItems, stages, users } from "../db/schema";
import { listAppointments } from "./avec";
import { teamNameCol } from "./team-name";

export type AgendaItem = {
  /** Chave única na tela: "lead:<id>" ou "ag:<id>". */ key: string;
  /** lead = conversa em Agendado sem horário na agenda; agendamento = horário da agenda (Avec ou CRM). */ source: "lead" | "agendamento";
  dealId: string | null; appointmentId: string | null; visitId: string | null;
  contactId: string | null; name: string; phone: string | null; hasAvatar: boolean;
  scheduledAt: string; /** Do horário, senão do serviço; sem nenhuma, 60 min estimados. */ durationMin: number; durationEstimated: boolean;
  /** Cancelamento do Avec que veio sem hora (gravado às 00:00): a tela mostra "sem hora". */ noTime: boolean;
  service: string | null; serviceId: string | null; ownerName: string | null; professionalId: string | null; professionalName: string | null;
  /** Cor da profissional (6A, `professionals.color`): o card ganha um toque dela (6.10). */ professionalColor: string | null;
  stageName: string | null; status: AgendaStatus; /** O status gravado (só agendamentos): o card escolhe o próximo passo por ele. */ dbStatus: string | null;
  origin: "avec" | "crm"; notes: string | null; /** Quem marcou (CRM) ou "Quem cadastrou" (Avec). */ bookedBy: string | null;
  /** Comanda fechada (6C): selo R$. */ paid: boolean; /** Nenhuma visita finalizada antes deste dia. */ firstVisit: boolean;
  anyPro: boolean; fitIn: boolean; outsideHours: boolean; confirmedVia: string | null; cancelReason: string | null;
};

const service = alias(lists, "service");

/** O primeiro dia (Salvador) em que cada cliente foi atendida: visita finalizada (CRM ou Avec) ou comanda do Avec. */
async function firstVisitDays(contactIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
  if (!contactIds.length) return new Map();
  const [aps, sales] = await Promise.all([
    db.select({ id: appointments.contactId, day: sql<string>`to_char(min(${appointments.startsAt}) at time zone 'America/Bahia', 'YYYY-MM-DD')` }).from(appointments)
      .where(and(inArray(appointments.contactId, contactIds), eq(appointments.status, "compareceu"), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf))).groupBy(appointments.contactId),
    db.select({ id: salesItems.contactId, day: sql<string>`min(${salesItems.soldOn})::text` }).from(salesItems).where(inArray(salesItems.contactId, contactIds)).groupBy(salesItems.contactId),
  ]);
  const out = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const r of [...aps, ...sales]) if (r.id && r.day && (!out.has(r.id) || r.day < out.get(r.id)!)) out.set(r.id, r.day);
  return out;
}

/**
 * A Agenda: os horários do período (um dia ou uma semana, em Salvador), em ordem. A agenda é da Casa: todo mundo que atende vê todos os
 * horários. Lead arquivado e contato ignorado ficam de fora; gêmeos do Avec e lista de espera também; cancelados entram.
 * Lote 6B: o horário ligado a um lead aparece **como horário** (e o lead some), porque é nele que a recepção marca Chegou, Finalizar e Faltou.
 */
export async function getAgenda(range: AgendaRange, now: Date = new Date(), opts: { professionalId?: string | null } = {}): Promise<AgendaItem[]> {
  const to = new Date(range.from.getTime() + range.days * 86_400_000);
  const [rows, aps] = await Promise.all([
    db.select({
      dealId: deals.id, contactId: contacts.id, name: contacts.name, phone: contacts.phoneE164, hasAvatar: sql<boolean>`(${contacts.avatarPath} is not null)`,
      scheduledAt: deals.scheduledAt, attendance: deals.attendance, serviceId: deals.serviceId, service: service.label, serviceDuration: service.durationMin, ownerName: teamNameCol(),
      professionalId: deals.professionalId, professionalName: professionals.name, professionalColor: professionals.color, stageName: stages.name, stageKind: stages.kind,
    })
      .from(deals).innerJoin(contacts, eq(contacts.id, deals.contactId)).innerJoin(stages, eq(stages.id, deals.stageId))
      .leftJoin(users, eq(users.id, deals.ownerId)).leftJoin(service, eq(service.id, deals.serviceId)).leftJoin(professionals, eq(professionals.id, deals.professionalId))
      .where(and(isNull(deals.archivedAt), eq(contacts.ignored, false), gte(deals.scheduledAt, range.from), lt(deals.scheduledAt, to),
        opts.professionalId ? eq(deals.professionalId, opts.professionalId) : undefined))
      .orderBy(asc(deals.scheduledAt)),
    listAppointments({ from: range.from, to }, { professionalId: opts.professionalId }),
  ]);

  const apptDeals = new Set(aps.map((a) => a.dealId).filter(Boolean));
  const apptSlots = new Set(aps.filter((a) => a.contactId).map((a) => `${a.contactId}|${a.startsAt.slice(0, 16)}`));
  const leadRows = rows.filter((r) => !apptDeals.has(r.dealId) && !apptSlots.has(`${r.contactId}|${new Date(r.scheduledAt!).toISOString().slice(0, 16)}`));

  const serviceIds = [...new Set(aps.map((a) => a.serviceId).filter((x): x is string => !!x))];
  const apContacts = [...new Set(aps.map((a) => a.contactId).filter((x): x is string => !!x))];
  const [durations, avatars, firstDay] = await Promise.all([
    serviceIds.length ? db.select({ id: lists.id, durationMin: lists.durationMin }).from(lists).where(inArray(lists.id, serviceIds)) : [],
    apContacts.length ? db.select({ id: contacts.id, ignored: contacts.ignored, hasAvatar: sql<boolean>`(${contacts.avatarPath} is not null)` }).from(contacts).where(inArray(contacts.id, apContacts)) : [],
    firstVisitDays([...new Set([...leadRows.map((r) => r.contactId), ...apContacts])]),
  ]);
  const durationOf = new Map(durations.map((d) => [d.id, d.durationMin]));
  const contactOf = new Map(avatars.map((c) => [c.id, c]));
  const isFirst = (contactId: string | null, at: string) => !!contactId && (!firstDay.has(contactId) || firstDay.get(contactId)! >= dayOf(at));

  const leads: AgendaItem[] = leadRows.map((r) => {
    const scheduledAt = new Date(r.scheduledAt!).toISOString();
    const d = entryDuration(null, r.serviceDuration);
    return {
      key: `lead:${r.dealId}`, source: "lead", dealId: r.dealId, appointmentId: null, visitId: null,
      contactId: r.contactId, name: r.name?.trim() || r.phone, phone: r.phone, hasAvatar: r.hasAvatar,
      scheduledAt, durationMin: d.minutes, durationEstimated: d.estimated, noTime: false,
      service: r.service, serviceId: r.serviceId, ownerName: r.ownerName, professionalId: r.professionalId, professionalName: r.professionalName, professionalColor: r.professionalColor, stageName: r.stageName,
      status: agendaStatus({ scheduledAt, attendance: r.attendance, stageKind: r.stageKind }, now), dbStatus: null, origin: "crm", notes: null, bookedBy: null,
      paid: false, firstVisit: isFirst(r.contactId, scheduledAt), anyPro: false, fitIn: false, outsideHours: false, confirmedVia: null, cancelReason: null,
    };
  });

  const booked: AgendaItem[] = aps.filter((a) => !(a.contactId && contactOf.get(a.contactId)?.ignored)).map((a) => {
    const d = entryDuration(a.durationMin, a.serviceId ? durationOf.get(a.serviceId) : null);
    return {
      key: `ag:${a.id}`, source: "agendamento", dealId: a.dealId, appointmentId: a.id, visitId: a.visitId,
      contactId: a.contactId, name: a.clientName, phone: a.phone, hasAvatar: !!(a.contactId && contactOf.get(a.contactId)?.hasAvatar),
      scheduledAt: a.startsAt, durationMin: d.minutes, durationEstimated: d.estimated,
      noTime: a.origin === "avec" && a.status === "cancelado" && minuteOfDay(a.startsAt) === 0,
      service: a.serviceLabel, serviceId: a.serviceId, ownerName: null, professionalId: a.professionalId, professionalName: a.professionalName, professionalColor: a.professionalColor, stageName: null,
      status: appointmentAgendaStatus(a, now), dbStatus: a.status, origin: a.origin === "crm" ? "crm" : "avec", notes: a.notes, bookedBy: a.bookedByName ?? a.createdByName,
      paid: false, firstVisit: isFirst(a.contactId, a.startsAt), anyPro: a.anyPro, fitIn: a.fitIn, outsideHours: a.outsideHours, confirmedVia: a.confirmedVia, cancelReason: a.cancelReason,
    };
  });

  return [...leads, ...booked].sort((a, b) => a.scheduledAt.localeCompare(b.scheduledAt) || (a.source === b.source ? 0 : a.source === "lead" ? -1 : 1));
}

export type BookableService = { id: string; label: string; durationMin: number | null; category: string | null };
/** Os serviços que dá para marcar (ativos), na ordem da lista. */
export async function listBookableServices(): Promise<BookableService[]> {
  return db.select({ id: lists.id, label: lists.label, durationMin: lists.durationMin, category: lists.category }).from(lists)
    .where(and(eq(lists.kind, "service"), eq(lists.active, true))).orderBy(asc(lists.position), asc(lists.label));
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 5: `src/server/queries/agenda-week.ts`**

```ts
import { and, asc, gte, inArray, isNull, lt, sql, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { appointments, lists, professionals } from "../db/schema";
import { weekDays, dayOf, type AgendaRange } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { OCCUPYING_STATUSES } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { effectiveRanges } from "@/lib/work-hours";
import { normText } from "../domain/avec/normalize";
import { getBusinessHours } from "../domain/hours";
import { getAgendaRules } from "../domain/agenda/mode";

export type WeekCell = { day: string; clients: number; minutes: number; off: boolean };
export type WeekRow = { professionalId: string; name: string; active: boolean; cells: WeekCell[] };

/**
 * Semana por profissional (6.1): uma linha por profissional (as ativas, mais quem tem horário na semana) e uma coluna por dia, com o
 * número de clientes e as horas marcadas, ou "Folga" quando ela não trabalha no dia (horário próprio, senão o da Casa).
 */
export async function getAgendaWeekByPro(range: AgendaRange): Promise<WeekRow[]> {
  const days = weekDays(range);
  const to = new Date(range.from.getTime() + range.days * 86_400_000);
  const [pros, casa, rows, step] = await Promise.all([
    db.select({ id: professionals.id, name: professionals.name, active: professionals.active, workHours: professionals.workHours })
      .from(professionals).orderBy(sql`${professionals.agendaPosition} asc nulls last`, asc(professionals.name)),
    getBusinessHours(),
    db.select({ pro: appointments.professionalId, contactId: appointments.contactId, clientName: appointments.clientName, startsAt: appointments.startsAt,
      durationMin: appointments.durationMin, serviceDuration: lists.durationMin })
      .from(appointments).leftJoin(lists, eq(lists.id, appointments.serviceId))
      .where(and(gte(appointments.startsAt, range.from), lt(appointments.startsAt, to), inArray(appointments.status, [...OCCUPYING_STATUSES]), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf))),
    getAgendaRules().then((r) => r.passoMin),
  ]);
  const busyPros = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.pro).filter(Boolean));
  return pros.filter((p) => p.active || busyPros.has(p.id)).map((p) => ({
    professionalId: p.id, name: p.name, active: p.active,
    cells: days.map((day) => {
      const mine = rows.filter((r) => r.pro === p.id && dayOf(r.startsAt) === day);
      return {
        day,
        clients: new Set(mine.map((r) => r.contactId ?? `nome:${normText(r.clientName)}`)).size,
        minutes: mine.reduce((a, r) => a + (r.durationMin ?? r.serviceDuration ?? step), 0),
        off: effectiveRanges(day, p.workHours, casa).length === 0,
      };
    }),
  }));
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 6: `src/server/queries/agenda-clients.ts`**

```ts
import { and, eq, isNull, notInArray, desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { appointments, professionals } from "../db/schema";
import { dayOf } from "@/lib/agenda";

export type AgendaClientHit = { id: string; name: string; phone: string | null; lastVisit: string | null; ignored: boolean };
const escapeLike = (s: string) => s.replace(/[\\%_]/g, "\\$&");

/**
 * Busca de cliente no Marcar e na Agenda: nome (começo do nome primeiro) ou telefone (4 dígitos ou mais), com a última visita (CRM ou Avec).
 * Quem está como "Não é cliente" aparece por último (marcar a traz de volta). Nunca devolve CPF nem e-mail.
 */
export async function searchAgendaClients(q: string, limit = 8): Promise<AgendaClientHit[]> {
  const term = q.trim().slice(0, 80);
  if (term.length < 2) return [];
  const digits = term.replace(/\D/g, "");
  const byPhone = digits.length >= 4 ? sql`c.phone_e164 like ${`%${digits}%`}` : sql`false`;
  const r = await db.execute(sql`
    select c.id, c.name, c.phone_e164 as phone, c.ignored,
      greatest(
        (select to_char(max(a.starts_at) at time zone 'America/Bahia', 'YYYY-MM-DD') from appointments a where a.contact_id = c.id and a.status = 'compareceu' and a.avec_twin_of is null),
        (select max(s.sold_on)::text from sales_items s where s.contact_id = c.id)
      ) as last_visit
    from contacts c
    where coalesce(c.name, '') ilike ${`%${escapeLike(term)}%`} or ${byPhone}
    order by c.ignored, (coalesce(c.name, '') ilike ${`${escapeLike(term)}%`}) desc, c.name nulls last
    limit ${limit}`);
  return (r.rows as { id: string; name: string | null; phone: string; ignored: boolean; last_visit: string | null }[]).map((x) => ({
    id: x.id, name: x.name?.trim() || "Sem nome", phone: x.phone.startsWith("lid:") ? null : x.phone, lastVisit: x.last_visit, ignored: x.ignored,
  }));
}

export type ClientAgendaEntry = { appointmentId: string; day: string; startsAt: string; serviceLabel: string; professionalName: string | null; status: string; origin: string };
export type ClientAgenda = { futuros: ClientAgendaEntry[]; passados: ClientAgendaEntry[]; cancelados: ClientAgendaEntry[] };

/** "Buscar cliente na Agenda": os horários dela (próximos, anteriores e cancelados), sem gêmeos nem pedidos da lista de espera. */
export async function clientAgenda(contactId: string, now = new Date(), limit = 30): Promise<ClientAgenda> {
  const rows = await db.select({ id: appointments.id, startsAt: appointments.startsAt, serviceLabel: appointments.serviceLabel, professionalName: professionals.name,
    status: appointments.status, origin: appointments.origin })
    .from(appointments).leftJoin(professionals, eq(professionals.id, appointments.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(appointments.contactId, contactId), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf), notInArray(appointments.status, ["espera", "espera_encaixada", "espera_desistiu"])))
    .orderBy(desc(appointments.startsAt)).limit(limit * 3);
  const entry = (r: (typeof rows)[number]): ClientAgendaEntry => ({ appointmentId: r.id, day: dayOf(r.startsAt), startsAt: r.startsAt.toISOString(), serviceLabel: r.serviceLabel,
    professionalName: r.professionalName, status: r.status, origin: r.origin });
  return {
    futuros: rows.filter((r) => r.status !== "cancelado" && r.startsAt >= now).reverse().slice(0, limit).map(entry),
    passados: rows.filter((r) => r.status !== "cancelado" && r.startsAt < now).slice(0, limit).map(entry),
    cancelados: rows.filter((r) => r.status === "cancelado").slice(0, limit).map(entry),
  };
}
```

```ts
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { z } from "zod";
import { assertPerm, type SessionUser } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { can, type Perm } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { UserFacingError } from "@/server/domain/errors";
import { APPOINTMENT_MARKS, setAppointmentStatus, undoAppointmentStatus, updateAppointmentNotes, type AppointmentMark } from "@/server/domain/appointments";
import { cancelAppointments, createVisit, ExistingAppointmentError, rescheduleAppointment, type CancelInput, type CreateVisitInput, type CreateVisitResult, type RescheduleInput } from "@/server/domain/agenda/visits";
import { addToWaitlist, resolveWaitlist, waitlistMatchesFor, type AddWaitlistInput, type ResolveWaitlistInput } from "@/server/domain/agenda/waitlist";
import { previewBooking, type FreeSlotsQuery } from "@/server/domain/agenda/slots";
import { clientAgenda, searchAgendaClients } from "@/server/queries/agenda-clients";
import { createBlock, deleteBlock, updateBlock } from "@/server/domain/agenda/blocks";
import { nextFreeSlots, type NextFree } from "@/server/domain/agenda/availability";
import { BLOCK_REASONS, type ClientInside } from "@/lib/agenda-blocks";
import { userErrorMessage } from "@/server/user-error";

export type ActionResult<T = undefined> = { ok: true; data?: T } | { ok: false; error: string };
type Refused = { ok: false; error: string; openAppointment?: { id: string; day: string } };
export type BookingActionResult = { ok: true; data: CreateVisitResult } | Refused;

/** Quem age é sempre a pessoa da sessão (6.7): a tela nunca manda o autor. */
const actorOf = (u: SessionUser) => ({ id: u.id, perms: u.perms });
const uuid = (v: string) => z.string().uuid().parse(v);

async function run<T>(perm: Perm, fn: (u: SessionUser) => Promise<T>, opts: { fallback?: string; revalidate?: boolean } = {}): Promise<ActionResult<T>> {
  try {
    const u = await assertPerm(perm);
    const data = await fn(u);
    if (opts.revalidate !== false) revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true, data };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", opts.fallback ?? "Não foi possível registrar. Tente de novo.") }; }
}

const refused = (e: unknown): Refused =>
  e instanceof ExistingAppointmentError
    ? { ok: false, error: e.message, openAppointment: { id: e.appointmentId, day: e.day } }
    : { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não foi possível marcar. Tente de novo.") };

/** Marcar (6.1). O servidor decide conflito, encaixe e o modo da agenda. */
export async function createVisitAction(input: CreateVisitInput): Promise<BookingActionResult> {
  try {
    const u = await assertPerm("agenda.marcar");
    const data = await createVisit(input, actorOf(u));
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true, data };
  } catch (e) { return refused(e); }
}

/** Encaixar um pedido da lista de espera (pela porta de marcar) ou marcar que desistiu. */
export async function resolveWaitlistAction(id: string, input: ResolveWaitlistInput): Promise<{ ok: true; data: { appointmentIds: string[] } } | Refused> {
  try {
    const u = await assertPerm("agenda.marcar");
    const data = await resolveWaitlist(uuid(id), input, actorOf(u));
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true, data };
  } catch (e) { return refused(e); }
}

export const rescheduleAction = async (id: string, input: RescheduleInput) => run("agenda.marcar", (u) => rescheduleAppointment(uuid(id), input, actorOf(u)));
export const cancelAction = async (input: CancelInput) => run("agenda.marcar", async (u) => {
  const r = await cancelAppointments(input, actorOf(u));
  return { ...r, waitlist: (await waitlistMatchesFor(r.day)).length };
});
/**
 * Confirmar, Chegou, Iniciar, Finalizar e Faltou: quem vê a agenda (como o Compareceu/Faltou de antes).
 * Provisório até a Tarefa 6B.13: "agendado" é o Desfazer da tela atual; cancelar exige motivo (folha Cancelar).
 */
export async function setAppointmentStatusAction(appointmentId: string, status: AppointmentMark | "cancelado"): Promise<ActionResult<{ changed: boolean }>> {
  try {
    const user = await assertPerm("atendimento.ver");
    const id = uuid(appointmentId);
    if (status === "cancelado") throw new UserFacingError("Para cancelar, escolha o motivo em Cancelar horário.");
    if (status === "agendado") {
      if (!can(user.perms, "agenda.marcar")) throw new UserFacingError("Você não tem permissão para isso.");
      await undoAppointmentStatus(id, actorOf(user));
      revalidatePath("/agenda");
      return { ok: true, data: { changed: true } };
    }
    const r = await setAppointmentStatus(id, z.enum(APPOINTMENT_MARKS).parse(status), user.id, { via: "recepcao" });
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true, data: r };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não foi possível registrar. Tente de novo.") }; }
}
export const undoStatusAction = async (id: string) => run("agenda.marcar", (u) => undoAppointmentStatus(uuid(id), actorOf(u)));
export const notesAction = async (id: string, notes: string | null) => run("agenda.marcar", (u) => updateAppointmentNotes(uuid(id), notes, u.id));
export const waitlistAction = async (input: AddWaitlistInput) => run("agenda.marcar", (u) => addToWaitlist(input, actorOf(u)));
/** Prévia do Marcar: só lê. Fica com quem marca (5.7). */
export const freeSlotsAction = async (q: FreeSlotsQuery) => run("agenda.marcar", () => previewBooking(q, new Date()), { fallback: "Não foi possível ver os horários livres.", revalidate: false });
/** Nome, telefone e última visita: só para quem atende (6.7). */
export const searchClientsAction = async (q: string) => run("atendimento.ver", () => searchAgendaClients(z.string().max(80).parse(q)), { revalidate: false });
export const clientAgendaAction = async (contactId: string) => run("atendimento.ver", () => clientAgenda(uuid(contactId)), { revalidate: false });

/* ───────────── Lote 6A: bloqueios e Procurar horário (quem marca: agenda.marcar) ───────────── */

const blockInput = z.object({
  professionalId: z.string().uuid("Escolha a profissional.").nullable(),
  startsOn: z.string().max(10), endsOn: z.string().max(10), startTime: z.string().max(5), endTime: z.string().max(5),
  weekdays: z.array(z.number().int()).max(7).nullable(),
  reason: z.enum(BLOCK_REASONS, "Escolha o motivo do bloqueio."),
  note: z.string().max(300).nullish(),
});

/** Cria (id null) ou edita um bloqueio. Devolve as clientes marcadas dentro (o bloqueio não mexe nelas). */
export async function saveBlockAction(id: string | null, input: z.input<typeof blockInput>): Promise<ActionResult<{ clientsInside: ClientInside[] }>> {
  try {
    const user = await assertPerm("agenda.marcar");
    const v = blockInput.parse(input);
    const r = id ? await updateBlock(z.string().uuid().parse(id), v, user.id) : await createBlock(v, user.id);
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true, data: { clientsInside: r.clientsInside } };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não foi possível salvar o bloqueio. Tente de novo.") }; }
}

/** Apaga um bloqueio (só marca a data e quem apagou; vale também para os do Avec). */
export async function deleteBlockAction(id: string): Promise<ActionResult> {
  try {
    const user = await assertPerm("agenda.marcar");
    await deleteBlock(z.string().uuid().parse(id), user.id);
    revalidatePath("/agenda");
    return { ok: true };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não foi possível apagar o bloqueio. Tente de novo.") }; }
}

/** Procurar horário: só lê, mas fica com quem marca. Nunca devolve o nome de outra cliente. */
export async function findFreeSlotsAction(input: { serviceId: string; professionalId?: string | null }): Promise<ActionResult<NextFree>> {
  try {
    await assertPerm("agenda.marcar");
    const v = z.object({ serviceId: z.string().uuid("Escolha o serviço."), professionalId: z.string().uuid().nullish() }).parse(input);
    return { ok: true, data: await nextFreeSlots(v) };
  } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: userErrorMessage(e, "agenda", "Não foi possível procurar agora. Tente de novo.") }; }
}
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterEach, beforeAll, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";

const freeSlotsAction = vi.fn();
const createVisitAction = vi.fn();

  searchClientsAction: (...a: unknown[]) => searchClientsAction(...a), rescheduleAction: vi.fn(), resolveWaitlistAction: vi.fn(),
}));
import { BookingSheet } from "./booking-sheet";

beforeAll(() => {
  // jsdom não tem matchMedia (a folha escolhe lateral × de baixo)
  window.matchMedia ??= ((q: string) => ({ matches: true, media: q, addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {}, addListener() {}, removeListener() {}, onchange: null, dispatchEvent: () => false })) as typeof window.matchMedia;
});
afterEach(() => { cleanup(); vi.clearAllMocks(); });

const PROS = [{ id: "p-ana", name: "Ana", active: true, serviceIds: ["s-corte", "s-mechas"] }, { id: "p-bia", name: "Bia", active: true, serviceIds: ["s-corte"] }];

  suggestions: [{ professionalId: "p-ana", name: "Ana", startsAt: ["2026-10-16T13:00:00.000Z", "2026-10-16T13:30:00.000Z"] }],
};
const encaixe = { ...livre, preview: { state: "encaixe", text: "Ana já tem Paula das 10:00 às 11:00", warnings: [], professionalId: "p-ana" } };
const PAULA = { contactId: "c-paula", name: "Paula Souza" };

  open: true, onClose: () => {}, services: SERVICES, professionals: PROS, canFitIn: true, canEditConfig: false, agendaMode: "so_crm" as const,
  initial: { day: "2026-10-16", time: "10:00", professionalId: "p-ana" }, onDone: vi.fn(), ...over,
});

it("marca: busca a cliente, escolhe o serviço, vê 'Livre' e manda a visita com a request key (hora lida em Salvador)", async () => {
  searchClientsAction.mockResolvedValue({ ok: true, data: [{ id: "c-paula", name: "Paula Souza", phone: "+5571988000101", lastVisit: "2026-08-12", ignored: false }] });

});

});

});
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `src/components/agenda/client-picker.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { searchClientsAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import type { AgendaClientHit } from "@/server/queries/agenda-clients";
import { formatPhoneClient } from "@/lib/phone-client";

export type PickedClient = { kind: "contato"; contactId: string; name: string } | { kind: "nova"; name: string; phone: string };
const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "UTC" });

/** Cliente do horário: busca por nome ou telefone (com a última visita) ou "Cliente nova" (nome e celular). Telefone que já existe reaproveita a ficha. */
export function ClientPicker({ value, onChange, locked = false }: { value: PickedClient | null; onChange: (c: PickedClient | null) => void; locked?: boolean }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [hits, setHits] = useState<AgendaClientHit[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (q.trim().length < 2) { setHits([]); return; }
    let alive = true;
    const t = setTimeout(async () => { const r = await searchClientsAction(q); if (alive) setHits(r.ok ? r.data ?? [] : []); }, 250);
    return () => { alive = false; clearTimeout(t); };
  }, [q]);

  if (value?.kind === "contato") return (
    <div className="flex min-h-11 items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line px-3 py-2">
      <span className="font-medium" data-testid="cliente-escolhida">{value.name}</span>
      {!locked && <button type="button" className="text-sm text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => onChange(null)}>Trocar</button>}
    </div>
  );
  if (value?.kind === "nova") return (
    <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
      <Input aria-label="Nome da cliente nova" value={value.name} maxLength={120} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, name: e.target.value })} className="h-11" />
      <Input aria-label="Celular da cliente nova" inputMode="tel" placeholder="71 99999-9999" value={value.phone} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...value, phone: e.target.value })} className="h-11" />
      <button type="button" className="justify-self-start text-sm text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => onChange(null)}>Buscar uma cliente que já existe</button>
    </div>
  );
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <Input type="search" aria-label="Buscar cliente por nome ou telefone" placeholder="Nome ou telefone" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} className="h-11" />
      {hits.length > 0 && (
        <ul aria-label="Clientes encontradas" className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line">
          {hits.map((h) => (
            <li key={h.id} className="border-t border-line/70 first:border-t-0">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => onChange({ kind: "contato", contactId: h.id, name: h.name })} className="flex w-full flex-col items-start px-3 py-2 text-left hover:bg-paper-2 pointer-coarse:min-h-11">
                <span className="font-medium">{h.name}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted">
                  {[h.phone ? formatPhoneClient(h.phone) : null, h.lastVisit ? `última visita ${DM.format(new Date(`${h.lastVisit}T12:00:00Z`))}` : "sem visitas", h.ignored ? "estava como Não é cliente" : null].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
                </span>
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline"
        onClick={() => onChange({ kind: "nova", name: q.replace(/[\d()+\-.]/g, "").trim(), phone: q.replace(/\D/g, "") })}>Cliente nova</button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/components/agenda/booking-sheet.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input, Select, Textarea } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/segmented";
import { ActionSheet, FieldError, SheetLabel } from "@/components/queue/action-sheet";
import { createVisitAction, freeSlotsAction, rescheduleAction, resolveWaitlistAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import type { FreeSlotsResult } from "@/server/domain/agenda/slots";
import type { CreateVisitInput } from "@/server/domain/agenda/visits";
import { durationLabel } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { DUPLA_NOTE } from "@/lib/agenda-text";
import { slotMs } from "@/lib/schedule";
import { firstName } from "@/lib/authorship";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { ClientPicker, type PickedClient } from "./client-picker";

import type { AgendaMode } from "@/lib/agenda-settings";
export type BookingService = { id: string; label: string; durationMin: number | null };
export type BookingPro = { id: string; name: string; active: boolean; serviceIds: string[] };
export type BookingInitial = {
  day: string; time: string; professionalId?: string | null; serviceId?: string | null;
  client?: { contactId: string; name: string } | null; dealId?: string | null; walkIn?: boolean; waitlistId?: string | null;
};
export type RescheduleTarget = { appointmentId: string; serviceId: string | null; serviceLabel: string; professionalId: string | null; durationMin: number | null; clientName: string; contactId: string | null };

type Item = { serviceId: string; professionalId: string; durationText: string; saveDuration: boolean; linkService: boolean; mode: "depois" | "junto" };
const newItem = (serviceId = "", professionalId = ""): Item => ({ serviceId, professionalId, durationText: "", saveDuration: false, linkService: false, mode: "depois" });
const HORA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });
const DIA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "short", day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });
const MAX_ITEMS = 4;

/**
 * Marcar e Remarcar (6.1): cliente (busca ou nova), um ou mais serviços (profissional ou "Qualquer uma", "logo depois" ou "ao mesmo
 * tempo"), dia e hora com os próximos livres e, na hora, "Livre" ou o conflito exato. A prévia só ajuda: quem decide é o servidor.
 * "Encaixar mesmo assim" aparece só para quem tem `agenda.encaixar`. Nasce nova a cada abertura (a request key é dela).
 */
export function BookingSheet({ open, onClose, services, professionals, initial, reschedule = null, canFitIn, canEditConfig, agendaMode, onDone }: {
  open: boolean; onClose: () => void; services: BookingService[]; professionals: BookingPro[]; initial: BookingInitial; reschedule?: RescheduleTarget | null;
  canFitIn: boolean; canEditConfig: boolean; agendaMode: AgendaMode; onDone: (r: { day: string; message: string }) => void;
}) {
  const [requestKey] = useState(() => crypto.randomUUID());
  const [client, setClient] = useState<PickedClient | null>(() =>
    initial.client ? { kind: "contato", contactId: initial.client.contactId, name: initial.client.name }
      : reschedule?.contactId ? { kind: "contato", contactId: reschedule.contactId, name: reschedule.clientName } : null);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<Item[]>(() => [newItem(reschedule?.serviceId ?? initial.serviceId ?? "", reschedule?.professionalId ?? initial.professionalId ?? "")]);
  const [day, setDay] = useState(initial.day);
  const [time, setTime] = useState(initial.time);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<FreeSlotsResult | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [existing, setExisting] = useState<{ id: string; day: string } | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();

  const active = professionals.filter((p) => p.active);
  const svcOf = (id: string) => services.find((s) => s.id === id) ?? null;
  const prosFor = (serviceId: string) => (serviceId ? active.filter((p) => p.serviceIds.includes(serviceId)) : active);
  const typed = (it: Item) => (svcOf(it.serviceId)?.durationMin == null ? Number(it.durationText) || null : null);
  const needsLink = (it: Item) => !!it.professionalId && !!it.serviceId && !active.find((p) => p.id === it.professionalId)?.serviceIds.includes(it.serviceId);
  const first = items[0];
  const firstDuration = typed(first);
  const clientId = client?.kind === "contato" ? client.contactId : null;

  const setItem = (k: number, patch: Partial<Item>) => { setItems((l) => l.map((it, j) => (j === k ? { ...it, ...patch } : it))); setError(null); };
  const pickService = (k: number, serviceId: string) => {
    const pro = items[k].professionalId;
    const keep = !pro || !serviceId || canEditConfig || !!active.find((p) => p.id === pro)?.serviceIds.includes(serviceId);
    setItem(k, { serviceId, professionalId: keep ? pro : "", durationText: "", saveDuration: false, linkService: false });
  };

  // Prévia do servidor para o 1º serviço (livre, conflito, próximos livres); espera parar de digitar.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || !first.serviceId || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(day)) { setPreview(null); return; }
    let alive = true;
    const t = setTimeout(async () => {
      const r = await freeSlotsAction({ serviceId: first.serviceId, professionalId: first.professionalId || null, day, time: /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(time) ? time : null,
        durationMin: firstDuration, clientId, ignoreAppointmentId: reschedule?.appointmentId ?? null, linkService: first.linkService });
      if (alive) setPreview(r.ok ? r.data ?? null : null);
    }, 250);
    return () => { alive = false; clearTimeout(t); };
  }, [open, first.serviceId, first.professionalId, first.linkService, day, time, firstDuration, clientId, reschedule?.appointmentId]);

  const state = preview?.preview?.state;
  const needsFit = state === "encaixe" || state === "fora_do_horario";
  const anyPro = !first.professionalId;

  const submit = (fitIn: boolean) => {
    setError(null); setExisting(null);
    const ms = slotMs(day, time);
    if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) { setError("Escolha o dia e a hora."); return; }
    if (!client) { setError("Escolha a cliente."); return; }
    if (items.some((i) => !i.serviceId)) { setError("Escolha o serviço."); return; }
    if (items.some((i) => needsLink(i) && !i.linkService)) { setError("Essa profissional não faz esse serviço. Marque \"Ela faz esse serviço\" ou escolha outra."); return; }
    const startsAt = new Date(ms).toISOString();
    const when = `${DIA.format(new Date(ms)).replace(".", "")} às ${HORA.format(new Date(ms))}`;
    start(async () => {
      if (reschedule) {
        const r = await rescheduleAction(reschedule.appointmentId, { startsAt, professionalId: first.professionalId || undefined, durationMin: firstDuration ?? undefined, fitIn });
        if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
        onDone({ day: r.data!.day, message: `Remarcado: ${firstName(reschedule.clientName) ?? "cliente"}, ${when}` });
        return;
      }
      const booking: Omit<CreateVisitInput, "client"> = {
        requestKey, startsAt, dealId: initial.dealId ?? null, notes: notes.trim() || null, fitIn, walkIn: !!initial.walkIn,
        items: items.map((it, k) => ({ serviceId: it.serviceId, professionalId: it.professionalId || null, durationMin: typed(it), saveDuration: it.saveDuration, linkService: it.linkService, mode: k ? it.mode : undefined })),
      };
      const r = initial.waitlistId
        ? await resolveWaitlistAction(initial.waitlistId, { result: "encaixada", booking })
        : await createVisitAction({ ...booking, client: client.kind === "contato" ? { contactId: client.contactId } : { name: client.name, phone: client.phone } });
      if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); setExisting(r.openAppointment ?? null); return; }
      onDone({ day, message: `Horário marcado: ${firstName(client.name) ?? "cliente"}, ${when}` });
    });
  };

  const title = reschedule ? "Remarcar horário" : initial.waitlistId ? "Encaixar da lista de espera" : initial.walkIn ? "Encaixe agora" : "Marcar horário";
  return (
    <ActionSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title}
      footer={<>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="lg" onClick={onClose} disabled={pending}>Voltar</Button>
        {needsFit && canFitIn
          ? <Button size="lg" variant="gold" loading={pending} onClick={() => submit(true)}>Encaixar mesmo assim</Button>
          : <Button size="lg" loading={pending} disabled={needsFit} onClick={() => submit(false)}>{reschedule ? "Remarcar" : "Marcar"}</Button>}
      </>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        {agendaMode === "dupla" && <p role="note" className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-gold-soft px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-gold-ink">{DUPLA_NOTE}</p>}
        <section aria-label="Cliente">
          <SheetLabel>Cliente</SheetLabel>
          <ClientPicker value={client} onChange={(c) => { setClient(c); setError(null); }} locked={!!reschedule || !!initial.client} />
        </section>
        {items.map((it, k) => {
          const svc = svcOf(it.serviceId);
          return (
            <fieldset key={k} className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line p-3">
              <legend className="px-1 text-sm font-medium text-graphite">Serviço {k + 1}{svc?.durationMin ? ` · ${durationLabel(svc.durationMin)}` : ""}</legend>
              {k > 0 && <Segmented label={`Quando é o serviço ${k + 1}`} value={it.mode} onChange={(v) => setItem(k, { mode: v })}
                options={[{ value: "depois", label: "Logo depois" }, { value: "junto", label: "Ao mesmo tempo, com outra profissional" }]} />}
              <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <Select aria-label={`Serviço ${k + 1}`} value={it.serviceId} disabled={!!reschedule} onChange={(e) => pickService(k, e.target.value)} className="h-12">
                  <option value="">Escolher serviço</option>
                  {services.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}
                </Select>
                <Select aria-label={`Profissional ${k + 1}`} value={it.professionalId} onChange={(e) => setItem(k, { professionalId: e.target.value, linkService: false })} className="h-12">
                  <option value="">{prosFor(it.serviceId).length ? "Qualquer uma" : "Ninguém faz esse serviço"}</option>
                  {(canEditConfig ? active : prosFor(it.serviceId)).map((p) => (
                    <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}{it.serviceId && !p.serviceIds.includes(it.serviceId) ? " (não faz)" : ""}</option>
                  ))}
                </Select>
              </div>
              {svc && svc.durationMin == null && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <label className="text-sm text-graphite" htmlFor={`marcar-duracao-${k}`}>Quanto tempo leva desta vez? (min)</label>
                  <Input id={`marcar-duracao-${k}`} inputMode="numeric" value={it.durationText} className="h-11 w-32"
                    onChange={(e) => setItem(k, { durationText: e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 3) })} />
                  {canEditConfig && (
                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={it.saveDuration} onChange={(e) => setItem(k, { saveDuration: e.target.checked })} />Salvar como duração do serviço</label>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
              {canEditConfig && needsLink(it) && (
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={it.linkService} onChange={(e) => setItem(k, { linkService: e.target.checked })} />Ela faz esse serviço (gravar no cadastro)</label>
              )}
              {k > 0 && <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm text-alert hover:underline" onClick={() => setItems((l) => l.filter((_, j) => j !== k))}>Tirar este serviço</button>}
            </fieldset>
          );
        })}
        {!reschedule && items.length < MAX_ITEMS && (
          <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => setItems((l) => [...l, newItem()])}>+ Outro serviço na mesma visita</button>
        )}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div>
            <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="marcar-dia">Dia</label></SheetLabel>
            <Input id="marcar-dia" type="date" value={day} className="h-12" onChange={(e) => { setDay(e.target.value); setError(null); }} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="marcar-hora">Hora</label></SheetLabel>
            <Input id="marcar-hora" type="time" step={300} value={time} className="h-12" onChange={(e) => { setTime(e.target.value); setError(null); }} />
          </div>
        </div>
        {preview && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {preview.needsDuration && firstDuration == null && <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{preview.serviceLabel} não tem duração cadastrada. Diga quanto tempo leva desta vez para ver os horários livres.</p>}
            {preview.preview && (
              <p role="status" data-testid="booking-preview" className={cn("rounded-[var(--radius-control)] px-3 py-2 text-sm", state === "livre" ? "bg-ok-soft text-ok" : "bg-alert-soft text-alert")}>{preview.preview.text}</p>
            )}
            {preview.preview?.warnings.map((w) => <p key={w} className="text-sm text-gold-ink">{w}</p>)}
            {preview.suggestions.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <p className="mb-1.5 text-xs text-muted">Próximos livres</p>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {preview.suggestions.flatMap((sug) => sug.startsAt.map((iso) => (
                    <button key={`${sug.professionalId}${iso}`} type="button" className="inline-flex h-10 items-center rounded-full border border-line-strong px-3 text-sm tabular hover:bg-paper-2"
                      onClick={() => { setTime(HORA.format(new Date(iso))); if (anyPro) setItem(0, { professionalId: sug.professionalId }); }}>
                      {HORA.format(new Date(iso))}{anyPro ? ` · ${sug.name}` : ""}
                    </button>
                  )))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        {!reschedule && (
          <div>
            <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="marcar-obs">Observação deste horário</label></SheetLabel>
            <Textarea id="marcar-obs" value={notes} maxLength={1000} onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        )}
        <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
        {existing && <Link href={`/agenda?d=${existing.day}&destaque=${existing.id}`} className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink underline-offset-4 hover:underline">Abrir o horário</Link>}
      </div>
    </ActionSheet>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { expect, it } from "vitest";
import { nowGroups } from "./agenda-now";

const at = (h: number, m = 0) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 16, h + 3, m)).toISOString();
const row = (dbStatus: string, scheduledAt: string, over: Partial<{ paid: boolean; noTime: boolean }> = {}) =>
  ({ source: "agendamento" as const, dbStatus, scheduledAt, paid: false, noTime: false, ...over });
const NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 16, 13, 0); // 10:00 em Salvador

it("chegam em 30 min, atrasadas há 10 min sem Chegou, na cadeira e falta fechar (só com o caixa no CRM)", () => {
  const items = [
    row("agendado", at(10, 20)), row("confirmado", at(10, 40)), row("agendado", at(9, 50)), row("agendado", at(9, 55)),
    row("chegou", at(10)), row("em_atendimento", at(9)), row("compareceu", at(8)), row("compareceu", at(8, 30), { paid: true }), row("cancelado", at(0), { noTime: true }),
  ];
  const g = nowGroups(items, NOW, { caixaOn: true });
  expect(g.chegando.map((i) => i.scheduledAt)).toEqual([at(10, 20)]);
  expect(g.atrasadas.map((i) => i.scheduledAt)).toEqual([at(9, 50)]);
  expect(g.naCadeira.map((i) => i.dbStatus)).toEqual(["chegou", "em_atendimento"]);
  expect(g.faltaFechar.map((i) => i.scheduledAt)).toEqual([at(8)]);
  expect(nowGroups(items, NOW, { caixaOn: false }).faltaFechar).toEqual([]);
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterEach, beforeAll, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import type { AgendaItem } from "@/server/queries/agenda";

const cancelAction = vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true as const, data: { cancelled: 2, day: "2026-10-16", waitlist: 1 } }));
```

`src/lib/agenda-now.ts`:

```ts
/** A faixa "Agora" da Agenda de hoje (6.1): quem chega, quem está atrasada, quem está na cadeira e quem falta fechar (6C). Pura. */
export type NowGroupKey = "chegando" | "atrasadas" | "naCadeira" | "faltaFechar";
export const NOW_LABEL: Record<NowGroupKey, string> = { chegando: "Chegam em 30 min", atrasadas: "Atrasadas, sem Chegou", naCadeira: "Na cadeira", faltaFechar: "Falta fechar" };
type NowItem = { source: string; dbStatus: string | null; scheduledAt: string; paid: boolean; noTime: boolean };
const PLANNED = new Set(["agendado", "confirmado"]);

export function nowGroups<T extends NowItem>(items: T[], nowMs: number, opts: { caixaOn: boolean }): Record<NowGroupKey, T[]> {
  const out: Record<NowGroupKey, T[]> = { chegando: [], atrasadas: [], naCadeira: [], faltaFechar: [] };
  for (const i of items) {
    if (i.source !== "agendamento" || i.noTime || !i.dbStatus) continue;
    const t = new Date(i.scheduledAt).getTime();
    if (PLANNED.has(i.dbStatus) && t > nowMs && t <= nowMs + 30 * 60_000) out.chegando.push(i);
    else if (PLANNED.has(i.dbStatus) && t <= nowMs - 10 * 60_000) out.atrasadas.push(i);
    else if (i.dbStatus === "chegou" || i.dbStatus === "em_atendimento") out.naCadeira.push(i);
    else if (opts.caixaOn && i.dbStatus === "compareceu" && !i.paid) out.faltaFechar.push(i);
  }
  return out;
}
```

Substituir `src/components/agenda/use-agenda-actions.ts` por:

```ts
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import type { AgendaItem } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { appointmentAgendaStatus, STATUS_LABEL, type AgendaStatus } from "@/lib/agenda";
import type { AppointmentMark } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { setAttendanceAction } from "@/app/(app)/funil/actions";
import { notesAction, setAppointmentStatusAction, undoStatusAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import { attendanceToast } from "@/components/queue/use-lead-actions";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { formatPhoneClient } from "@/lib/phone-client";

/** O nome que a tela mostra: o da ficha; sem nome, o telefone formatado. */
export const shownName = (i: AgendaItem) => (i.phone && i.name === i.phone ? formatPhoneClient(i.phone) : i.name);

const DONE: Record<AppointmentMark, (n: string) => string> = {
  confirmado: (n) => `Horário de ${n} confirmado`, chegou: (n) => `${n} chegou`, em_atendimento: (n) => `${n} em atendimento`,
  compareceu: (n) => `${n}: atendimento finalizado`, faltou: (n) => `${n} faltou`, agendado: (n) => `Pedido de ${n} aceito`,
};

export type AgendaActions = {
  pending: boolean;
  mark: (i: AgendaItem, status: AppointmentMark, after?: () => void) => void;
  undo: (i: AgendaItem, after?: () => void) => void;
  saveNotes: (i: AgendaItem, notes: string, after?: () => void) => void;
  apply: (items: AgendaItem[]) => AgendaItem[];
};

/**
 * As ações do card na Agenda. Lead (sem horário na agenda): o mesmo Compareceu/Faltou da Fila. Horário: a porta única de status do 6B.
 * A tela mostra a situação nova antes do recarregar.
 */
export function useAgendaActions(nowMs: number): AgendaActions {
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const [marked, setMarked] = useState<Record<string, { status: AgendaStatus; dbStatus: string | null }>>({});
  const apply = (items: AgendaItem[]) => items.map((i) => (marked[i.key] ? { ...i, ...marked[i.key] } : i));
  const shown = (i: AgendaItem, status: string) => ({ status: appointmentAgendaStatus({ startsAt: i.scheduledAt, status }, new Date(nowMs)), dbStatus: status });

  const mark = (i: AgendaItem, status: AppointmentMark, after?: () => void) => start(async () => {
    const name = shownName(i);
    if (i.source === "lead" && i.dealId) {
      if (status !== "compareceu" && status !== "faltou") return;
      const r = await setAttendanceAction(i.dealId, status);
      if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
      setMarked((m) => ({ ...m, [i.key]: { status, dbStatus: null } }));
      toast(attendanceToast(name, status, r.data), r.data?.changed ? "ok" : "info");
    } else if (i.appointmentId) {
      const r = await setAppointmentStatusAction(i.appointmentId, status);
      if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
      setMarked((m) => ({ ...m, [i.key]: shown(i, status) }));
      toast(r.data?.changed ? DONE[status](name) : "Já estava registrado", r.data?.changed ? "ok" : "info");
    } else return;
    after?.();
    router.refresh();
  });

  const undo = (i: AgendaItem, after?: () => void) => start(async () => {
    if (!i.appointmentId) return;
    const r = await undoStatusAction(i.appointmentId);
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    const back = shown(i, r.data!.to);
    setMarked((m) => ({ ...m, [i.key]: back }));
    toast(`Desfeito: ${shownName(i)} voltou para ${STATUS_LABEL[back.status]}`);
    after?.();
    router.refresh();
  });

  const saveNotes = (i: AgendaItem, notes: string, after?: () => void) => start(async () => {
    if (!i.appointmentId) return;
    const r = await notesAction(i.appointmentId, notes);
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    toast(r.data?.changed ? "Observação salva" : "Nada mudou", r.data?.changed ? "ok" : "info");
    after?.();
    router.refresh();
  });

  return { pending, mark, undo, saveNotes, apply };
}
```

`src/components/agenda/agenda-sheets.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import type { AgendaItem } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { dayOf, minuteOfDay } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { hhmm } from "@/lib/free-slots";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import type { AgendaMode } from "@/lib/agenda-settings";
import { BookingSheet, type BookingInitial, type BookingPro, type BookingService, type RescheduleTarget } from "./booking-sheet";
import { CancelSheet } from "./cancel-sheet";

export type AgendaCatalog = { services: BookingService[]; professionals: BookingPro[]; canMark: boolean; canFitIn: boolean; canEditConfig: boolean; agendaMode: AgendaMode };
type Ctx = { catalog: AgendaCatalog | null; book: (i: BookingInitial) => void; reschedule: (item: AgendaItem) => void; cancel: (item: AgendaItem, visitCount?: number) => void };
const AgendaSheetsCtx = createContext<Ctx>({ catalog: null, book: () => {}, reschedule: () => {}, cancel: () => {} });
export const useAgendaSheets = () => useContext(AgendaSheetsCtx);
/** Marcar, remarcar e lista de espera: com `agenda.marcar` e fora do modo consulta. */
export const canBookIn = (c: AgendaCatalog | null) => !!c && c.canMark && c.agendaMode !== "consulta";

/** As folhas da Agenda (Marcar/Remarcar e Cancelar) num lugar só: qualquer card, a grade e a faixa "Agora" abrem a mesma folha. */
export function AgendaSheetsProvider({ catalog, children }: { catalog: AgendaCatalog; children: ReactNode }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [booking, setBooking] = useState<{ n: number; open: boolean; initial: BookingInitial; target: RescheduleTarget | null } | null>(null);
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState<{ n: number; open: boolean; item: AgendaItem; visitCount: number } | null>(null);
  const book = useCallback((initial: BookingInitial) => setBooking((b) => ({ n: (b?.n ?? 0) + 1, open: true, initial, target: null })), []);
  const reschedule = useCallback((item: AgendaItem) => setBooking((b) => ({
    n: (b?.n ?? 0) + 1, open: true,
    initial: { day: dayOf(item.scheduledAt), time: hhmm(minuteOfDay(item.scheduledAt)), professionalId: item.professionalId, serviceId: item.serviceId },
    target: { appointmentId: item.appointmentId!, serviceId: item.serviceId, serviceLabel: item.service ?? "Serviço", professionalId: item.professionalId,
      durationMin: item.durationEstimated ? null : item.durationMin, clientName: item.name, contactId: item.contactId },
  })), []);
  const cancel = useCallback((item: AgendaItem, visitCount = 1) => setCancelling((c) => ({ n: (c?.n ?? 0) + 1, open: true, item, visitCount })), []);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ catalog, book, reschedule, cancel }), [catalog, book, reschedule, cancel]);
  return (
    <AgendaSheetsCtx.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
      {booking && (
        <BookingSheet key={`b${booking.n}`} open={booking.open} onClose={() => setBooking((b) => b && { ...b, open: false })}
          services={catalog.services} professionals={catalog.professionals} initial={booking.initial} reschedule={booking.target}
          canFitIn={catalog.canFitIn} canEditConfig={catalog.canEditConfig} agendaMode={catalog.agendaMode}
          onDone={(r) => { setBooking((b) => b && { ...b, open: false }); toast(r.message); router.refresh(); }} />
      )}
      {cancelling && (
        <CancelSheet key={`c${cancelling.n}`} open={cancelling.open} onClose={() => setCancelling((c) => c && { ...c, open: false })}
          item={cancelling.item} visitCount={cancelling.visitCount} agendaMode={catalog.agendaMode}
          onDone={(r) => {
            setCancelling((c) => c && { ...c, open: false });
            const many = r.cancelled > 1 ? `${r.cancelled} horários cancelados` : "Horário cancelado";
            if (r.waitlist) toast(`${many} · ${r.waitlist} na espera querem este dia`, "ok", { label: "Ver", onClick: () => router.push("/agenda?aba=espera") });
            else toast(many);
            router.refresh();
          }} />
      )}
    </AgendaSheetsCtx.Provider>
  );
}
```

`src/components/agenda/cancel-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { AgendaItem } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/segmented";
import { ActionSheet, FieldError, SheetLabel } from "@/components/queue/action-sheet";
import { cancelAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import { CANCEL_REASON_LABEL, RECEPTION_CANCEL_REASONS, type CancelReason } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { cancelNoticeText } from "@/lib/agenda-text";
import { leadHref } from "@/lib/lead-href";
import type { AgendaMode } from "@/lib/agenda-settings";

/**
 * Cancelar (6.1): motivo obrigatório ("Outro" pede o texto); visita com mais de um serviço pergunta "Só este serviço" ou "A visita toda".
 * "Avisar a cliente" copia o texto pronto e oferece abrir a conversa: nada é enviado sozinho. Nada é apagado.
 */
export function CancelSheet({ open, onClose, item, visitCount, agendaMode, onDone }: {
  open: boolean; onClose: () => void; item: AgendaItem; visitCount: number; agendaMode: AgendaMode;
  onDone: (r: { cancelled: number; waitlist: number }) => void;
}) {
  const [reason, setReason] = useState<CancelReason | null>(null);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const [scope, setScope] = useState<"este" | "visita">("este");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const notice = cancelNoticeText({ clientName: item.name, startsAt: item.scheduledAt, services: [item.service ?? "seu serviço"] });

  const confirm = () => {
    if (!reason) { setError("Escolha o motivo."); return; }
    if (reason === "outro" && !note.trim()) { setError("Escreva o motivo."); return; }
    setError(null);
    start(async () => {
      const r = await cancelAction({ appointmentId: item.appointmentId!, scope, reason, note: note.trim() || null });
      if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
      onDone({ cancelled: r.data!.cancelled, waitlist: r.data!.waitlist });
    });
  };
  const copy = async () => {
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(notice); setCopied(true); } catch { setCopied(false); setError("Não deu para copiar. Selecione o texto e copie."); }
  };

  return (
    <ActionSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Cancelar horário"
      footer={<>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="lg" onClick={onClose} disabled={pending}>Voltar</Button>
        <Button variant="danger" size="lg" loading={pending} onClick={confirm}>Cancelar horário</Button>
      </>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        {agendaMode !== "so_crm" && <p role="note" className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-gold-soft px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-gold-ink">Enquanto o Avec estiver ligado, cancele também lá.</p>}
        <div>
          <SheetLabel id="cancelar-motivo">Motivo</SheetLabel>
          <Segmented labelledBy="cancelar-motivo" value={reason} onChange={(v) => { setReason(v); setError(null); }}
            options={RECEPTION_CANCEL_REASONS.map((r) => ({ value: r, label: CANCEL_REASON_LABEL[r] }))} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="cancelar-nota">{reason === "outro" ? "Qual o motivo?" : "Observação (opcional)"}</label></SheetLabel>
          <Textarea id="cancelar-nota" value={note} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => { setNote(e.target.value); setError(null); }} />
        </div>
        {visitCount > 1 && (
          <div>
            <SheetLabel id="cancelar-escopo">O que cancelar</SheetLabel>
            <Segmented labelledBy="cancelar-escopo" value={scope} onChange={setScope} options={[{ value: "este", label: "Só este serviço" }, { value: "visita", label: "A visita toda" }]} />
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line px-3 py-3">
          <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{notice}</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={copy}>Avisar a cliente</Button>
            {item.dealId && <Link href={leadHref(item.dealId)} className="text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink underline-offset-4 hover:underline">Abrir conversa</Link>}
          </div>
          {copied && <p role="status" className="text-sm text-ok">Texto copiado: cole na conversa da cliente.</p>}
        </div>
        <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
      </div>
    </ActionSheet>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 5: O card (`entry-sheet.tsx`) e a faixa "Agora" (`now-strip.tsx`)**

Substituir `src/components/agenda/entry-sheet.tsx` por:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import type { AgendaItem } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { durationLabel, STATUS_LABEL, timeRange } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { ACTIVE_STATUSES, CANCEL_REASON_LABEL, canMarkNoShow, checkCancel, nextStep, STEP_LABEL, type CancelReason } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { leadHref } from "@/lib/lead-href";
import { formatPhoneClient } from "@/lib/phone-client";
import { Sheet } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { IconCheck, IconClose } from "@/components/icons";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { firstName } from "@/lib/authorship";
import { shownName, type AgendaActions } from "./use-agenda-actions";
import { StatusChip } from "./status-chip";
import { canBookIn, useAgendaSheets } from "./agenda-sheets";

const DIA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });
const cap = (s: string) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
const UNDOABLE = new Set(["confirmado", "chegou", "em_atendimento", "compareceu", "faltou", "cancelado"]);

/** De onde veio o horário, em palavras da recepção. */
export const originLabel = (i: AgendaItem) => (i.source === "lead" ? "Lead do Atendimento" : i.origin === "avec" ? "Veio do Avec" : "Marcado na Casa");

/**
 * Um horário aberto (6.1): o próximo passo em destaque (Confirmar, Chegou, Iniciar, Finalizar), Faltou depois do início, Remarcar,
 * Cancelar, Desfazer, Observação, e abrir a conversa ou a ficha. Lead sem horário na agenda continua com Compareceu/Faltou.
 */
export function EntrySheet({ item, onClose, actions, nowMs, visitCount = 1 }: { item: AgendaItem | null; onClose: () => void; actions: AgendaActions; nowMs: number; visitCount?: number }) {
  const { catalog, reschedule, cancel } = useAgendaSheets();
  const [notesFor, setNotesFor] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const i = item;
  const now = new Date(nowMs);
  const isAppt = !!i && i.source === "agendamento" && !!i.appointmentId;
  const db = i?.dbStatus ?? null;
  const step = isAppt && db && !i!.noTime ? nextStep({ status: db, startsAt: new Date(i!.scheduledAt) }, now) : null;
  const canMark = !!catalog?.canMark;
  const open = i ? (i.dealId ? { href: leadHref(i.dealId), label: "Abrir conversa" } : i.contactId ? { href: `/contatos/${i.contactId}`, label: "Abrir ficha da cliente" } : null) : null;
  const editingNotes = !!i && notesFor === i.key;

  return (
    <Sheet open={!!i} onClose={onClose} title={i ? shownName(i) : ""}
      footer={i && (
        <div className="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2">
          {open && <Link href={open.href} className="mr-auto inline-flex h-10 items-center text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink underline-offset-4 hover:underline pointer-coarse:min-h-11">{open.label}</Link>}
          {!isAppt && i.status === "conferir" && (["compareceu", "faltou"] as const).map((s) => (
            <Button key={s} variant="secondary" disabled={actions.pending} onClick={() => actions.mark(i, s, onClose)}
              className={s === "compareceu" ? "border-ok/40 text-ok hover:bg-ok-soft" : "border-alert/40 text-alert hover:bg-alert-soft"}>
              {s === "compareceu" ? <IconCheck size={14} aria-hidden /> : <IconClose size={14} aria-hidden />}{s === "compareceu" ? "Compareceu" : "Faltou"}
            </Button>
          ))}
          {isAppt && db && (
            <>
              {canMark && UNDOABLE.has(db) && <Button variant="ghost" disabled={actions.pending} onClick={() => actions.undo(i, onClose)}>Desfazer “{STATUS_LABEL[i.status]}”</Button>}
              {canMark && checkCancel(db).ok && <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { cancel(i, visitCount); onClose(); }}>Cancelar horário</Button>}
              {canBookIn(catalog) && (db === "agendado" || db === "confirmado" || db === "solicitado") && <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { reschedule(i); onClose(); }}>Remarcar</Button>}
              {canMarkNoShow({ status: db, startsAt: new Date(i.scheduledAt) }, now) && (
                <Button variant="secondary" className="border-alert/40 text-alert hover:bg-alert-soft" disabled={actions.pending} onClick={() => actions.mark(i, "faltou", onClose)}>Faltou</Button>
              )}
              {step === "compareceu" && (db === "agendado" || db === "confirmado") && <Button variant="secondary" disabled={actions.pending} onClick={() => actions.mark(i, "chegou")}>Chegou</Button>}
              {step && (step !== "agendado" || canBookIn(catalog)) && <Button disabled={actions.pending} onClick={() => actions.mark(i, step, step === "compareceu" ? onClose : undefined)}>{STEP_LABEL[step]}</Button>}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}>
      {i && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{cap(DIA.format(new Date(i.scheduledAt)))}</p>
            <p className={cn("mt-0.5 text-2xl font-semibold tabular", i.status === "cancelado" && "text-muted line-through")}>{i.noTime ? "Sem hora" : timeRange(i.scheduledAt, i.durationMin)}</p>
            {!i.noTime && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{durationLabel(i.durationMin)}{i.durationEstimated ? " · duração estimada (o serviço não tem duração cadastrada)" : ""}</p>}
            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <StatusChip status={i.status} />
              {i.firstVisit && <Tag>1ª vez</Tag>}
              {i.fitIn && <Tag tone="alert">Encaixe</Tag>}
              {i.outsideHours && <Tag tone="alert">Fora do horário</Tag>}
              {i.anyPro && <Tag>Qualquer uma</Tag>}
              {i.confirmedVia === "link" && <Tag tone="ok">Confirmou pelo link</Tag>}
              {i.paid && <Tag tone="ok">R$ pago</Tag>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <dl className="grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr] gap-x-4 gap-y-2.5 text-sm">
            <Row k="Serviço" v={i.service || "Não informado"} muted={!i.service} />
            <Row k="Profissional" v={i.professionalName || "Não informada"} muted={!i.professionalName} />
            {i.phone && <Row k="Telefone" v={formatPhoneClient(i.phone)} />}
            {i.source === "lead" && <Row k="Conversa com" v={firstName(i.ownerName) ?? "Ninguém (sem dono)"} muted={!i.ownerName} />}
            {i.bookedBy && <Row k="Marcado por" v={i.bookedBy} />}
            <Row k="Origem" v={originLabel(i)} />
            {i.cancelReason && <Row k="Motivo" v={CANCEL_REASON_LABEL[i.cancelReason as CancelReason] ?? i.cancelReason} />}
            {i.notes && !editingNotes && <Row k="Observação" v={i.notes} />}
          </dl>
          {isAppt && canMark && (editingNotes ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <label htmlFor="obs-horario" className="text-sm font-medium text-graphite">Observação deste horário</label>
              <Textarea id="obs-horario" value={notes} maxLength={1000} onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)} />
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Button size="sm" disabled={actions.pending} onClick={() => actions.saveNotes(i, notes, () => setNotesFor(null))}>Salvar observação</Button>
                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setNotesFor(null)}>Voltar</Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => { setNotes(i.notes ?? ""); setNotesFor(i.key); }}>
              {i.notes ? "Editar observação" : "Observação"}
            </button>
          ))}
          {isAppt && !i.contactId && <p className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-paper-2 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-graphite">Essa cliente ainda não tem ficha no CRM (o Avec não mandou o telefone).</p>}
          {isAppt && ACTIVE_STATUSES.includes(db ?? "") && catalog?.agendaMode === "dupla" && <p className="text-xs text-muted">Enquanto o Avec estiver ligado, mude também lá.</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function Tag({ children, tone = "neutral" }: { children: ReactNode; tone?: "neutral" | "ok" | "alert" }) {
  return <span className={cn("inline-flex h-6 items-center rounded-full px-2 text-xs font-semibold", tone === "ok" ? "bg-ok-soft text-ok" : tone === "alert" ? "bg-alert-soft text-alert" : "bg-paper-3 text-graphite")}>{children}</span>;
}

function Row({ k, v, muted = false }: { k: string; v: string; muted?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <>
      <dt className="text-muted">{k}</dt>
      <dd className={cn("min-w-0 break-words", muted ? "text-muted" : "text-ink")}>{v}</dd>
    </>
  );
}
```

`src/components/agenda/now-strip.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import type { AgendaItem } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { NOW_LABEL, nowGroups, type NowGroupKey } from "@/lib/agenda-now";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { shownName, useAgendaActions } from "./use-agenda-actions";

const HORA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });

/** A faixa "Agora" (Agenda de hoje): cada cliente com o próximo botão. "Falta fechar" e "Abrir comanda" chegam com o caixa no CRM (6C). */
export function NowStrip({ items, nowMs, caixaOn = false, onOpenComanda }: { items: AgendaItem[]; nowMs: number; caixaOn?: boolean; onOpenComanda?: (i: AgendaItem) => void }) {
  const actions = useAgendaActions(nowMs);
  const groups = nowGroups(actions.apply(items), nowMs, { caixaOn });
  const keys = (Object.keys(NOW_LABEL) as NowGroupKey[]).filter((k) => groups[k].length);
  if (!keys.length) return null;
  const next = (k: NowGroupKey, i: AgendaItem): { label: string; run: () => void } | null => {
    if (k === "chegando" || k === "atrasadas") return { label: "Chegou", run: () => actions.mark(i, "chegou") };
    if (k === "naCadeira") return i.dbStatus === "chegou" ? { label: "Iniciar", run: () => actions.mark(i, "em_atendimento") } : { label: "Finalizar", run: () => actions.mark(i, "compareceu") };
    return onOpenComanda ? { label: "Abrir comanda", run: () => onOpenComanda(i) } : null;
  };
  return (
    <section aria-label="Agora" className="mb-5 grid gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-gold/40 bg-gold-soft/30 p-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      {keys.map((k) => (
        <div key={k}>
          <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-gold-ink">{NOW_LABEL[k]} · {groups[k].length}</h3>
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
            {groups[k].map((i) => {
              const b = next(k, i);
              return (
                <li key={i.key} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-paper px-2.5 py-2">
                  <span className="w-12 shrink-0 text-sm font-semibold tabular">{HORA.format(new Date(i.scheduledAt))}</span>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm">{shownName(i)}{i.professionalName ? <span className="text-muted"> · {i.professionalName}</span> : null}</span>
                  {b && <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={actions.pending} onClick={b.run} aria-label={`${b.label}: ${shownName(i)}`}>{b.label}</Button>}
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  );
}
```

```ts
});
```

```tsx
const searchClientsAction = vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true as const, data: [{ id: "c1", name: "Lia Souza", phone: null, lastVisit: null, ignored: false }] }));
vi.mock("@/app/(app)/agenda/actions", () => ({
  waitlistAction: (...a: unknown[]) => (waitlistAction as (...x: unknown[]) => unknown)(...a),
  searchClientsAction: (...a: unknown[]) => (searchClientsAction as (...x: unknown[]) => unknown)(...a),
}));
import { WaitlistSheet } from "./waitlist-sheet";

beforeAll(() => {
  window.matchMedia ??= ((q: string) => ({ matches: true, media: q, addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {}, addListener() {}, removeListener() {}, onchange: null, dispatchEvent: () => false })) as typeof window.matchMedia;
});
afterEach(cleanup);

it("pede a cliente; manda serviço, profissional (ou qualquer uma), dia e período", async () => {

  render(<WaitlistSheet open onClose={() => {}} today="2026-10-16" onDone={onDone}
    services={[{ id: "s1", label: "Corte", durationMin: 60 }]} professionals={[{ id: "p1", name: "Ana", active: true, serviceIds: ["s1"] }]} />);

  expect(onDone).toHaveBeenCalled();
});
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `defaultBookingTime` e `BookButton`**

Em `src/lib/agenda-now.ts`, acrescentar `import { minuteOfDay, todayBahia } from "./agenda";` e `import { hhmm } from "./free-slots";` e:

```ts
/** Hora inicial do Marcar: hoje, a próxima meia hora (no máximo 23:30); outro dia, 09:00. */
export function defaultBookingTime(day: string, nowMs: number): string {
  if (day !== todayBahia(new Date(nowMs))) return "09:00";
  return hhmm(Math.min(23 * 60 + 30, Math.ceil((minuteOfDay(new Date(nowMs)) + 1) / 30) * 30));
}
```

`src/components/agenda/book-button.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { IconPlus } from "@/components/icons";
import { minuteOfDay, todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { hhmm } from "@/lib/free-slots";
import { defaultBookingTime } from "@/lib/agenda-now";
import { canBookIn, useAgendaSheets } from "./agenda-sheets";

/** "Marcar horário" no alto da Agenda e, hoje, "Encaixe agora" (a cliente chegou sem hora: nasce como Chegou). Some no modo consulta. */
export function BookButton({ day, nowMs }: { day: string; nowMs: number }) {
  const { catalog, book } = useAgendaSheets();
  if (!canBookIn(catalog)) return null;
  const isToday = day === todayBahia(new Date(nowMs));
  return (
    <>
      {isToday && <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => book({ day, time: hhmm(Math.floor(minuteOfDay(new Date(nowMs)) / 5) * 5), walkIn: true })}>Encaixe agora</Button>}
      <Button onClick={() => book({ day, time: defaultBookingTime(day, nowMs) })}><IconPlus size={16} aria-hidden />Marcar horário</Button>
    </>
  );
}
```
