`src/components/agenda/waitlist-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input, Select, Textarea } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/segmented";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { ActionSheet, FieldError, SheetLabel } from "@/components/queue/action-sheet";
import { waitlistAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import { WAIT_WINDOW_LABEL, WAIT_WINDOWS, type WaitWindow } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { ClientPicker, type PickedClient } from "./client-picker";
import type { BookingPro, BookingService } from "./booking-sheet";
import { canBookIn, useAgendaSheets } from "./agenda-sheets";

/** "+ Lista de espera" (6.1): cliente, serviço, profissional preferida ou qualquer uma, dia desejado, manhã/tarde/dia todo e observação. */
export function WaitlistSheet({ open, onClose, services, professionals, today, onDone }: {
  open: boolean; onClose: () => void; services: BookingService[]; professionals: BookingPro[]; today: string; onDone: () => void;
}) {
  const [client, setClient] = useState<PickedClient | null>(null);
  const [serviceId, setServiceId] = useState("");
  const [pro, setPro] = useState("");
  const [day, setDay] = useState(today);
  const [win, setWin] = useState<WaitWindow>("dia");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const pros = professionals.filter((p) => p.active && (!serviceId || p.serviceIds.includes(serviceId)));
  const save = () => {
    if (!client) { setError("Escolha a cliente."); return; }
    setError(null);
    start(async () => {
      const r = await waitlistAction({
        client: client.kind === "contato" ? { contactId: client.contactId } : { name: client.name, phone: client.phone },
        serviceId: serviceId || null, professionalId: pro || null, day, window: win, notes: notes.trim() || null,
      });
      if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
      onDone();
    });
  };
  return (
    <ActionSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Lista de espera"
      footer={<>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="lg" onClick={onClose} disabled={pending}>Voltar</Button>
        <Button size="lg" loading={pending} onClick={save}>Pôr na lista de espera</Button>
      </>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
        <section aria-label="Cliente"><SheetLabel>Cliente</SheetLabel><ClientPicker value={client} onChange={(c) => { setClient(c); setError(null); }} /></section>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Select aria-label="Serviço desejado" value={serviceId} className="h-12" onChange={(e) => { setServiceId(e.target.value); setPro(""); }}>
            <option value="">Qualquer serviço</option>
            {services.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}
          </Select>
          <Select aria-label="Profissional preferida" value={pro} className="h-12" onChange={(e) => setPro(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">Qualquer uma</option>
            {pros.map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>)}
          </Select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="espera-dia">Dia desejado</label></SheetLabel>
          <Input id="espera-dia" type="date" min={today} value={day} className="h-12" onChange={(e) => setDay(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <SheetLabel id="espera-periodo">Período</SheetLabel>
          <Segmented labelledBy="espera-periodo" value={win} onChange={setWin} options={WAIT_WINDOWS.map((w) => ({ value: w, label: WAIT_WINDOW_LABEL[w] }))} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="espera-obs">Observação</label></SheetLabel>
          <Textarea id="espera-obs" value={notes} maxLength={500} onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <FieldError>{error}</FieldError>
      </div>
    </ActionSheet>
  );
}

/** O botão da aba Lista de espera (some no modo consulta e para quem não marca). */
export function WaitlistAdd({ today }: { today: string }) {
  const { catalog } = useAgendaSheets();
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<{ n: number; open: boolean } | null>(null);
  if (!canBookIn(catalog) || !catalog) return null;
  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSheet((s) => ({ n: (s?.n ?? 0) + 1, open: true }))}>+ Lista de espera</Button>
      {sheet && (
        <WaitlistSheet key={sheet.n} open={sheet.open} onClose={() => setSheet((s) => s && { ...s, open: false })} today={today}
          services={catalog.services} professionals={catalog.professionals}
          onDone={() => { setSheet((s) => s && { ...s, open: false }); toast("Pedido na lista de espera"); router.refresh(); }} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

Substituir `src/components/agenda/waitlist.tsx` por:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import type { AppointmentItem } from "@/server/queries/avec";
import { dayOf } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { WAIT_WINDOW_LABEL, type WaitWindow } from "@/lib/appointment-status";
import { formatPhoneClient } from "@/lib/phone-client";
import { initials } from "@/lib/format";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { resolveWaitlistAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { AvecTag } from "./status-chip";
import { canBookIn, useAgendaSheets } from "./agenda-sheets";

const DIA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long", timeZone: "UTC" });
const cap = (s: string) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
const WINDOW_TIME: Record<WaitWindow, string> = { manha: "09:00", tarde: "14:00", dia: "09:00" };

/**
 * Aba Lista de espera: quem pediu horário e ainda não tem, pelo dia pedido. "Encaixar" abre o Marcar já preenchido (cliente, serviço,
 * profissional e período) e o pedido sai da lista; "Desistiu" tira sem apagar. Dia que já passou fica marcado.
 */
export function Waitlist({ items, today }: { items: AppointmentItem[]; today: string }) {
  const { catalog, book } = useAgendaSheets();
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  if (!items.length) return (
    <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper">
      <Empty title="Ninguém na lista de espera">Quem pedir horário sem vaga aparece aqui, pelo dia pedido.</Empty>
    </div>
  );
  const giveUp = (i: AppointmentItem) => start(async () => {
    const r = await resolveWaitlistAction(i.id, { result: "desistiu" });
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    toast(`${i.clientName} saiu da lista de espera`);
    router.refresh();
  });
  const groups = new Map<string, AppointmentItem[]>();
  for (const i of items) { const d = dayOf(i.startsAt); groups.set(d, [...(groups.get(d) ?? []), i]); }
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      {[...groups].map(([d, its]) => {
        const past = d < today;
        return (
          <section key={d} aria-label={`Pedidos para ${d}`}>
            <h3 className="mb-2 flex items-baseline gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
              <span className={past ? "text-muted" : "text-ink"}>{cap(DIA.format(new Date(`${d}T12:00:00Z`)))}</span>
              {d === today && <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gold-ink">hoje</span>}
              {past && <span className="text-xs font-medium text-alert">dia já passou</span>}
              <span className="ml-auto text-xs font-normal text-muted tabular">{its.length} {its.length === 1 ? "pedido" : "pedidos"}</span>
            </h3>
            <ul className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper">
              {its.map((i) => {
                const win = (i.waitWindow ?? "dia") as WaitWindow;
                const who = (
                  <>
                    <span aria-hidden className="grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-gold-soft text-sm font-semibold text-gold-ink">{initials(i.clientName)}</span>
                    <span className="min-w-0">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><span className="truncate text-base font-medium">{i.clientName}</span>{i.origin === "avec" && <AvecTag />}</span>
                      <span className="block truncate text-sm text-graphite">
                        {[i.serviceLabel || "Serviço não informado", i.professionalName ? `com ${i.professionalName.split(" ")[0]}` : "qualquer profissional", WAIT_WINDOW_LABEL[win] ?? null].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                  </>
                );
                return (
                  <li key={i.id} className={cn("flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5 border-t border-line/70 px-4 py-3 first:border-t-0 sm:px-5", past && "bg-paper-2/40")}>
                    {i.contactId
                      ? <Link href={`/contatos/${i.contactId}`} className="flex min-h-11 min-w-0 flex-1 basis-56 items-center gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] hover:text-gold-ink">{who}</Link>
                      : <span className="flex min-h-11 min-w-0 flex-1 basis-56 items-center gap-3">{who}</span>}
                    {i.phone && <span className="text-sm text-graphite tabular">{formatPhoneClient(i.phone)}</span>}
                    <span className="flex gap-2">
                      {canBookIn(catalog) && i.contactId && (
                        <Button size="sm" onClick={() => book({ day: past ? today : d, time: WINDOW_TIME[win] ?? "09:00", serviceId: i.serviceId, professionalId: i.professionalId,
                          client: { contactId: i.contactId!, name: i.clientName }, waitlistId: i.id })}>Encaixar</Button>
                      )}
                      {catalog?.canMark && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" disabled={pending} onClick={() => giveUp(i)}>Desistiu</Button>}
                    </span>
                    {i.notes && <p className="w-full pl-[52px] text-sm text-muted">“{i.notes}”</p>}
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </section>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/agenda/week-by-pro.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import type { WeekRow } from "@/server/queries/agenda-week";
import { durationLabel } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

const SEMANA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "short", timeZone: "UTC" });
const clientsText = (n: number) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? "cliente" : "clientes"}`;

/** Semana por profissional (6.1): uma linha por profissional, uma coluna por dia; tocar abre o dia dela na visão Profissionais. */
export function WeekByPro({ rows, days, today }: { rows: WeekRow[]; days: string[]; today: string }) {
  if (!rows.length) return (
    <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper"><Empty title="Nenhuma profissional cadastrada">Cadastre em Configurações › Profissionais.</Empty></div>
  );
  return (
    <div className="scroll-thin overflow-x-auto rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper">
      <table className="w-full min-w-[760px] border-collapse text-sm">
        <caption className="sr-only">Semana por profissional</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col" className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-paper px-3 py-2.5 text-left font-medium text-graphite">Profissional</th>
            {days.map((d) => (
              <th key={d} scope="col" className={cn("px-2 py-2.5 text-center font-medium capitalize", d === today ? "text-gold-ink" : "text-graphite")}>
                {SEMANA.format(new Date(`${d}T12:00:00Z`)).replace(".", "")} {Number(d.slice(8))}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.professionalId} className="border-t border-line/70">
              <th scope="row" className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-paper px-3 py-2 text-left font-medium">{r.name}{!r.active && <span className="ml-1 text-xs text-muted">(desativada)</span>}</th>
              {r.cells.map((c) => (
                <td key={c.day} className="px-1 py-1 text-center">
                  <Link href={`/agenda?d=${c.day}&v=profissionais&p=${r.professionalId}`}
                    aria-label={`${r.name}, ${c.day}: ${c.clients ? `${clientsText(c.clients)}, ${durationLabel(c.minutes)}` : c.off ? "folga" : "livre"}`}
                    className={cn("flex min-h-11 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-[var(--radius-control)] hover:bg-paper-2", c.off && !c.clients && "bg-paper-2/60 text-muted")}>
                    {c.clients
                      ? <><span className="font-semibold tabular">{clientsText(c.clients)}</span><span className="text-xs text-muted tabular">{durationLabel(c.minutes)}</span></>
                      : <span>{c.off ? "Folga" : "livre"}</span>}
                  </Link>
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/agenda/agenda-search.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { clientAgendaAction, searchClientsAction } from "@/app/(app)/agenda/actions";
import type { AgendaClientHit, ClientAgenda } from "@/server/queries/agenda-clients";

const WHEN = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "short", day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });
const GROUPS: [keyof ClientAgenda, string][] = [["futuros", "Próximos"], ["passados", "Anteriores"], ["cancelados", "Cancelados"]];

/** "Buscar cliente na Agenda" (6.1): os horários dela (próximos, anteriores e cancelados); um toque leva ao dia com o horário destacado. */
export function AgendaSearch() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [hits, setHits] = useState<AgendaClientHit[]>([]);
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<{ name: string; agenda: ClientAgenda } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (picked || q.trim().length < 2) { setHits([]); return; }
    let alive = true;
    const t = setTimeout(async () => { const r = await searchClientsAction(q); if (alive) setHits(r.ok ? r.data ?? [] : []); }, 250);
    return () => { alive = false; clearTimeout(t); };
  }, [q, picked]);
  const pick = async (h: AgendaClientHit) => {
    const r = await clientAgendaAction(h.id);
    if (r.ok && r.data) { setPicked({ name: h.name, agenda: r.data }); setHits([]); }
  };
  return (
    <div className="relative w-full sm:w-72">
      <Input type="search" aria-label="Buscar cliente na Agenda" placeholder="Buscar cliente" value={q} className="h-11 sm:h-10"
        onChange={(e) => { setQ(e.target.value); setPicked(null); }} />
      {hits.length > 0 && (
        <ul aria-label="Clientes encontradas" className="absolute inset-x-0 top-full z-40 mt-1 overflow-hidden rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line bg-paper shadow-[var(--shadow-card)]">
          {hits.map((h) => (
            <li key={h.id}><button type="button" onClick={() => pick(h)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-paper-2 pointer-coarse:min-h-11">{h.name}</button></li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      {picked && (
        <section aria-label={`Horários de ${picked.name}`} className="absolute inset-x-0 top-full z-40 mt-1 flex max-h-[60vh] flex-col gap-3 overflow-auto rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line bg-paper p-3 shadow-[var(--shadow-card)]">
          {GROUPS.map(([k, label]) => picked.agenda[k].length > 0 && (
            <div key={k}>
              <h3 className="mb-1 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted">{label}</h3>
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                {picked.agenda[k].map((e) => (
                  <li key={e.appointmentId}>
                    <Link href={`/agenda?d=${e.day}&destaque=${e.appointmentId}`} className="block rounded-[6px] px-2 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-paper-2">
                      {WHEN.format(new Date(e.startsAt)).replace(".", "")} · {e.serviceLabel}{e.professionalName ? ` com ${e.professionalName}` : ""}
                    </Link>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          ))}
          {GROUPS.every(([k]) => !picked.agenda[k].length) && <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nenhum horário na agenda.</p>}
          <button type="button" className="self-end text-sm text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => { setPicked(null); setQ(""); }}>Fechar</button>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/agenda/avec-divergences.tsx` (componente de servidor):

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { DIVERGENCE_LABEL, type AvecDivergence } from "@/server/domain/agenda/twins";
import { DUPLA_NOTE, hourOf } from "@/lib/agenda-text";
import { STATUS_LABEL, type AgendaStatus } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";

const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "UTC" });
const label = (s: string | null) => (s ? STATUS_LABEL[s as AgendaStatus] ?? s : "sem informação");
const describe = (d: AvecDivergence) =>
  d.kind === "horario_diferente" ? `No CRM às ${hourOf(d.crmStartsAt!)}; no Avec às ${hourOf(d.avecStartsAt!)}.`
    : d.kind === "status_diferente" ? `No CRM: ${label(d.crmStatus)}; no Avec: ${label(d.avecStatus)}.`
      : `Marcado no Avec às ${hourOf(d.avecStartsAt!)}, depois da virada para só CRM.`;

/** "Conferir com o Avec" (6.4, regra 12): só leitura. A recepção decide o que fazer; o sistema não corrige sozinho. */
export function AvecDivergences({ items }: { items: AvecDivergence[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <p role="note" className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-gold-soft px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-gold-ink">{DUPLA_NOTE}</p>
      {!items.length ? (
        <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper"><Empty title="Nada para conferir">Os horários do CRM e do Avec batem.</Empty></div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper">
          {items.map((d) => (
            <li key={`${d.kind}:${d.appointmentId}:${d.avecAppointmentId ?? ""}`} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 border-t border-line/70 px-4 py-3 first:border-t-0 sm:px-5">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="font-medium">{d.clientName} · {d.serviceLabel} · {DM.format(new Date(`${d.day}T12:00:00Z`))}</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{DIVERGENCE_LABEL[d.kind]}. {describe(d)}</p>
              </div>
              <Link href={`/agenda?d=${d.day}&destaque=${d.appointmentId}`} className="text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink underline-offset-4 hover:underline">Abrir o horário</Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { dayColumns, matchesProSearch, orderProList, parseProSelection, proNowLine, selectionParam, toggleProSelection, type ProDayInfo } from "./agenda-pros";

const at = (h: number, m = 0) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 15, h + 3, m)).toISOString(); // 15/10/2026 em Salvador
const NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 15, 12, 0); // 09:00 em Salvador
const DAY = "2026-10-15";
const WORK: [number, number][] = [[9 * 60, 18 * 60]];

describe("escolha de profissionais (até 4 lado a lado, na URL)", () => {
  it("lê ?p=a,b: sem repetição, só ids que existem, as 4 mais recentes", () => {
    expect(parseProSelection("a,b,a,x", ["a", "b", "c"])).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(parseProSelection(undefined, ["a"])).toEqual([]);
    expect(parseProSelection(["b,a", "c"], ["a", "b", "c"])).toEqual(["b", "a"]);
    expect(parseProSelection("a,b,c,d,e", ["a", "b", "c", "d", "e"])).toEqual(["b", "c", "d", "e"]);
  });

  it("tocar acrescenta ou tira; a quinta troca a mais antiga; vazio = Casa Fiaes", () => {
    expect(toggleProSelection([], "a")).toEqual(["a"]);
    expect(toggleProSelection(["a", "b"], "a")).toEqual(["b"]);
    expect(toggleProSelection(["a", "b", "c", "d"], "e")).toEqual(["b", "c", "d", "e"]);
    expect(selectionParam(["a", "b"])).toBe("a,b");
    expect(selectionParam([])).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("o que ela está fazendo agora", () => {
  it("Atendendo, Livre até o próximo, Livre até o fim do expediente, Folga hoje e Fora do horário", () => {
    const julia = { name: "Júlia Souza", scheduledAt: at(8, 30), durationMin: 120, status: "em_atendimento" };
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: WORK, off: false }, [julia], DAY, DAY, NOW)).toBe("Atendendo Júlia até 10:30");
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: WORK, off: false }, [{ name: "Paula", scheduledAt: at(10), durationMin: 60, status: "agendado" }], DAY, DAY, NOW)).toBe("Livre até 10:00");
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: WORK, off: false }, [], DAY, DAY, NOW)).toBe("Livre até 18:00");
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: [], off: true }, [], DAY, DAY, NOW)).toBe("Folga hoje");
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: [[14 * 60, 18 * 60]], off: false }, [], DAY, DAY, NOW)).toBe("Fora do horário");
  });

  it("cancelado e faltou não contam como atendendo; outro dia mostra o expediente", () => {
    const cancelado = { name: "Júlia", scheduledAt: at(8, 30), durationMin: 120, status: "cancelado" };
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: WORK, off: false }, [cancelado], DAY, DAY, NOW)).toBe("Livre até 18:00");
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: WORK, off: false }, [], "2026-10-16", DAY, NOW)).toBe("Trabalha das 9h às 18h");
    expect(proNowLine({ ranges: [], off: true }, [], "2026-10-16", DAY, NOW)).toBe("Folga");
  });
});

const info = (id: string, name: string, over: Partial<ProDayInfo> = {}): ProDayInfo =>
  ({ id, name, color: null, photoUrl: null, ranges: WORK, off: false, count: 0, line: "", ...over });

describe("colunas e lista", () => {
  it("Review Focus 2: folga não vira coluna; fica no fim da lista; escolhida, vira coluna", () => {
    const pros = [info("c", "Carla", { off: true, ranges: [] }), info("a", "Ana"), info("b", "Bia")];
    expect(dayColumns(pros, [], []).map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(["Ana", "Bia"]);
    expect(orderProList(pros).map((p) => p.name)).toEqual(["Ana", "Bia", "Carla"]);
    expect(dayColumns(pros, ["c"], []).map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(["Carla"]);
  });

  it("de folga mas com horário marcado (encaixe) aparece; horário sem profissional abre 'Sem profissional'; desativada com horário entra no fim", () => {
    const pros = [info("c", "Carla", { off: true, ranges: [], count: 1 }), info("a", "Ana")];
    const items = [
      { professionalId: "c", professionalName: "Carla", status: "agendado" },
      { professionalId: null, professionalName: null, status: "agendado" },
      { professionalId: "x", professionalName: "Antiga", status: "agendado" },
    ];
    expect(dayColumns(pros, [], items).map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(["Carla", "Ana", "Antiga", "Sem profissional"]);
  });

  it("ninguém trabalha no dia: nenhuma coluna (a página explica)", () => {
    expect(dayColumns([info("a", "Ana", { off: true, ranges: [] })], [], [])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("busca por nome sem acento e sem maiúscula", () => {
    expect(matchesProSearch("Júlia", "jul")).toBe(true);
    expect(matchesProSearch("Ana", "bi")).toBe(false);
    expect(matchesProSearch("Ana", "  ")).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```tsx
});
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/lib/agenda-pros.ts`**

```ts
import { hhmm } from "./free-slots";
import { minuteOfDay, type ProColumn } from "./agenda";
import { firstName } from "./authorship";
import { hourLabel } from "./slot-text";

/** Modelo B (6.10): até 4 profissionais lado a lado; a escolha fica na URL (`?p=ana,bia`), na ordem em que foram tocadas. */
export const MAX_SIDE_BY_SIDE = 4;

export function parseProSelection(raw: string | string[] | undefined, validIds: readonly string[]): string[] {
  const text = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
  if (!text) return [];
  const valid = new Set(validIds);
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const id of text.split(",").map((s) => s.trim())) if (valid.has(id) && !out.includes(id)) out.push(id);
  return out.slice(-MAX_SIDE_BY_SIDE);
}

/** Tocar: tira se já estava; senão acrescenta no fim e, passando de 4, sai a mais antiga. */
export function toggleProSelection(sel: readonly string[], id: string): string[] {
  if (sel.includes(id)) return sel.filter((x) => x !== id);
  return [...sel, id].slice(-MAX_SIDE_BY_SIDE);
}

export const selectionParam = (sel: readonly string[]): string | null => (sel.length ? sel.join(",") : null);

export type ProDayInfo = {
  id: string; name: string; color: string | null; photoUrl: string | null;
  /** Faixas de trabalho do dia (próprio, senão o da Casa). */ ranges: [number, number][];
  /** Folga: sem faixa, ou tudo bloqueado. */ off: boolean;
  /** Horários (sem os cancelados) no período. */ count: number;
  /** "Atendendo Júlia até 10:30", "Livre até 10:00", "Folga hoje", "Fora do horário"… */ line: string;
};
export type NowLineItem = { name: string; scheduledAt: string; durationMin: number; status: string };

const NOT_BUSY = new Set(["cancelado", "faltou", "remarcou", "perdido", "espera"]);

/** A linha "o que está fazendo agora" da lista (6.10). Em outro dia, o expediente. */
export function proNowLine(p: { ranges: [number, number][]; off: boolean }, items: NowLineItem[], day: string, today: string, nowMs: number): string {
  if (day !== today) {
    if (p.off || !p.ranges.length) return "Folga";
    const first = Math.min(...p.ranges.map((r) => r[0])), last = Math.max(...p.ranges.map((r) => r[1]));
    return `Trabalha das ${hourLabel(first)} às ${hourLabel(last)}`;
  }
  if (p.off) return "Folga hoje";
  const now = minuteOfDay(new Date(nowMs));
  const live = items.filter((i) => !NOT_BUSY.has(i.status)).map((i) => ({ i, start: minuteOfDay(i.scheduledAt) }))
    .map((x) => ({ ...x, end: x.start + x.i.durationMin })).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  const current = live.find((x) => x.start <= now && now < x.end);
  if (current) return `Atendendo ${firstName(current.i.name) ?? "cliente"} até ${hhmm(Math.min(current.end, 1440))}`;
  const range = p.ranges.find(([a, b]) => a <= now && now < b);
  if (!range) return "Fora do horário";
  const next = live.find((x) => x.start > now && x.start < range[1]);
  return `Livre até ${hhmm(next ? next.start : range[1])}`;
}

/** Quem trabalha primeiro (na ordem da agenda); quem está de folga vai para o fim. */
export const orderProList = <T extends { off: boolean }>(pros: T[]): T[] => [...pros.filter((p) => !p.off), ...pros.filter((p) => p.off)];

const fold = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{M}/gu, "").toLowerCase().trim();
export const matchesProSearch = (name: string, q: string) => !fold(q) || fold(name).includes(fold(q));

/**
 * As colunas da grade do dia. Com escolha: as escolhidas, na ordem tocada (mesmo de folga). "Casa Fiaes" (sem escolha): quem trabalha no
 * dia e quem tem horário nele (encaixe na folga, desativada); "Sem profissional" se algum horário não tem profissional. Folga sem horário
 * não vira coluna.
 */
export function dayColumns(pros: ProDayInfo[], sel: readonly string[], items: { professionalId: string | null; professionalName: string | null; status: string }[]): ProColumn[] {
  const col = (p: ProDayInfo): ProColumn => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name, color: p.color, photoUrl: p.photoUrl });
  if (sel.length) return sel.flatMap((id) => { const p = pros.find((x) => x.id === id); return p ? [col(p)] : []; });
  const live = items.filter((i) => i.status !== "cancelado");
  const busy = new Set(live.map((i) => i.professionalId).filter(Boolean));
  const cols = pros.filter((p) => !p.off || p.count > 0 || busy.has(p.id)).map(col);
  for (const i of live) if (i.professionalId && !cols.some((c) => c.id === i.professionalId)) cols.push({ id: i.professionalId, name: i.professionalName ?? "Profissional" });
  if (live.some((i) => !i.professionalId)) cols.push({ id: null, name: "Sem profissional" });
  return cols;
}
```

`src/components/agenda/pro-avatar.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState } from "react";
import { initials } from "@/lib/format";
import { paletteHex, textOn } from "@/lib/palette";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

/** Foto da profissional (6B.16) ou as iniciais num círculo com a cor dela (6A). */
export function ProAvatar({ name, color, photoUrl, size = 32, className }: { name: string; color: string | null; photoUrl: string | null; size?: number; className?: string }) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const style = { width: size, height: size, fontSize: Math.round(size * 0.38) };
  if (photoUrl && !failed) {
    return <img src={photoUrl} alt="" width={size} height={size} loading="lazy" onError={() => setFailed(true)} style={style} className={cn("shrink-0 rounded-full bg-paper-3 object-cover", className)} />;
  }
  const hex = paletteHex(color);
  return (
    <span aria-hidden data-avatar="iniciais" style={{ ...style, ...(hex ? { background: hex, color: textOn(hex) } : {}) }}
      className={cn("grid shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full font-semibold", !hex && "bg-paper-3 text-graphite", className)}>{initials(name)}</span>
  );
}
```

`src/components/agenda/pro-list.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { matchesProSearch, type ProDayInfo } from "@/lib/agenda-pros";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { ProAvatar } from "./pro-avatar";

type Item = ProDayInfo & { href: string };

/**
 * A lista "Profissionais" ao lado da grade (modelo B, 6.10). "Casa Fiaes" primeiro (todas que trabalham no dia); tocar numa profissional
 * acrescenta ou tira da escolha (até 4); quem está de folga fica no fim, apagada. No celular vira uma faixa de fotos rolável.
 */
export function ProList({ pros, selected, casaHref, casaCount }: { pros: Item[]; selected: string[]; casaHref: string; casaCount: number }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const shown = pros.filter((p) => matchesProSearch(p.name, q));
  const row = "flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] px-2 py-2 text-left transition-colors lg:w-full";
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Profissionais" className="-mx-5 mb-4 px-5 lg:mx-0 lg:mb-0 lg:px-0">
      <input type="search" aria-label="Buscar profissional" placeholder="Buscar profissional" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        className="mb-2 hidden h-10 w-full rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong bg-paper px-3 text-sm lg:block" />
      <ul className="scroll-thin flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto pb-1 lg:flex-col lg:overflow-visible">
        <li>
          <Link href={casaHref} aria-label="Casa Fiaes" aria-current={selected.length === 0 ? "true" : undefined}
            className={cn(row, "flex-col lg:flex-row", selected.length === 0 ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "hover:bg-paper-2")}>
            <span aria-hidden className="grid size-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-gold-soft text-sm font-semibold text-gold-ink lg:size-9">CF</span>
            <span className="min-w-0 text-center lg:flex-1 lg:text-left">
              <span className="block truncate text-xs font-medium lg:text-sm">Casa Fiaes</span>
              <span className={cn("hidden text-xs lg:block", selected.length === 0 ? "text-paper/75" : "text-muted")}>{casaCount === 1 ? "1 trabalhando" : `${casaCount} trabalhando`}</span>
            </span>
          </Link>
        </li>
        {shown.map((p) => {
          const on = selected.includes(p.id);
          return (
            <li key={p.id}>
              <Link href={p.href} aria-label={p.name} aria-current={on ? "true" : undefined} data-folga={p.off || undefined}
                className={cn(row, "flex-col lg:flex-row", on ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "hover:bg-paper-2", p.off && !on && "opacity-55")}>
                <ProAvatar name={p.name} color={p.color} photoUrl={p.photoUrl} size={40} className={cn(on && "ring-2 ring-gold")} />
                <span className="min-w-0 text-center lg:flex-1 lg:text-left">
                  <span className="block max-w-[4.5rem] truncate text-xs font-medium lg:max-w-none lg:text-sm">{p.name}</span>
                  <span className={cn("hidden truncate text-xs lg:block", on ? "text-paper/75" : "text-graphite")}>{p.line}</span>
                </span>
                {p.count > 0 && <span className={cn("hidden rounded-full px-1.5 text-xs tabular lg:inline", on ? "bg-paper/20" : "bg-paper-2 text-muted")}>{p.count}</span>}
              </Link>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
      {shown.length === 0 && <p className="px-2 py-3 text-sm text-muted">Ninguém com esse nome.</p>}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
      const editBlocks = canBook ? {
        mode, pros: pros.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name })),
        blocks: rawBlocks.filter((b) => blocks.some((x) => x.id === b.id)).map(({ professionalName: _n, ...b }) => b),
      } : undefined;
      view = columns.length
        ? <ProGrid items={items} columns={columns} blocks={blocks} day={range.day} today={today} nowMs={now.getTime()} hours={hours} editBlocks={editBlocks} highlight={highlight} />
        : <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper"><Empty title={pros.length ? "Ninguém trabalha neste dia" : "Nenhuma profissional cadastrada"}>{pros.length ? "Toque numa profissional na lista para ver a agenda dela e marcar mesmo assim." : "Cadastre as profissionais em Configurações › Profissionais para ver a agenda de cada uma."}</Empty></div>;
    } else if (range.view === "semana" && sel.length !== 1) {
      // Semana da Casa (ou de várias): a contagem por dia e por profissional.
      const rows = await getAgendaWeekByPro(range);
      view = <WeekByPro rows={sel.length ? rows.filter((r) => sel.includes(r.professionalId)) : rows} days={days} today={today} />;
    } else {
      // Semana de uma profissional, ou a Lista (por horário).
      view = <AgendaBoard items={items} view={range.view === "semana" ? "semana" : "lista"} days={days} today={today} nowMs={now.getTime()} pro={sel.length === 1 ? sel[0] : null} highlight={highlight} />;
    }
```

```ts
  const { default: sharp } = await import("sharp");
  return sharp({ create: { width: w, height: h, channels: 3, background: "#8e4a7e" } })[format]().toBuffer();
};

  await resetDb();
  await rm(path.join(mediaDir(), "profissionais"), { recursive: true, force: true });
  proId = (await db.insert(professionals).values({ name: "Ana" }).returning())[0].id;
  userId = (await db.insert(users).values({ name: "Gestora", email: "g@c.com", role: "gestor", passwordHash: "x" }).returning())[0].id;
});

it("guarda a original e a miniatura quadrada de 256 px em WEBP, sem expor o caminho; registra quem trocou", async () => {
  const { photoVersion } = await saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, await image("png"), userId, new Date(1_760_000_000_000));
  expect(photoVersion).toBe("1760000000000");
  const [p] = await db.select().from(professionals).where(eq(professionals.id, proId));
  expect(p.photoPath).toBe(`profissionais/${proId}/1760000000000.png`);
  const q = await readProfessionalPhoto(proId, "q");
  expect(q?.mime).toBe("image/webp");
  const { default: sharp } = await import("sharp");
  expect(await sharp(q!.data).metadata()).toMatchObject({ width: 256, height: 256, format: "webp" });
  expect((await readProfessionalPhoto(proId, "full"))?.mime).toBe("image/png");
  expect(await db.select().from(auditLog).where(eq(auditLog.action, "profissional_foto"))).toHaveLength(1);
});

it("recusa acima de 5 MB, vazio e o que não é JPG, PNG ou WEBP (pelo conteúdo, não pelo nome)", async () => {
  const big = Buffer.concat([await image("png"), Buffer.alloc(5 * 1024 * 1024)]);
  await expect(saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, big, userId)).rejects.toThrow("A foto passa de 5 MB. Escolha uma menor.");
  await expect(saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, new Uint8Array(), userId)).rejects.toThrow("Escolha uma foto.");
  await expect(saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, Buffer.from("GIF89a……"), userId)).rejects.toThrow("Use uma foto JPG, PNG ou WEBP.");
  await expect(saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, Buffer.from('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"/>'), userId)).rejects.toThrow("Use uma foto JPG, PNG ou WEBP.");
  await expect(saveProfessionalPhoto("00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000", await image("jpeg"), userId)).rejects.toThrow("Profissional não encontrada.");
  expect((await db.select().from(professionals).where(eq(professionals.id, proId)))[0].photoPath).toBeNull();
});

it("trocar a foto apaga a anterior do disco; sem miniatura (sharp ausente), a leitura pequena devolve a original", async () => {
  await saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, await image("jpeg"), userId, new Date(1000));
  const first = path.join(mediaDir(), "profissionais", proId, "1000.jpg");
  expect(existsSync(first)).toBe(true);
  await saveProfessionalPhoto(proId, await image("webp"), userId, new Date(2000));
  expect(existsSync(first)).toBe(false);
  expect(existsSync(path.join(mediaDir(), "profissionais", proId, "1000-q.webp"))).toBe(false);
  await rm(path.join(mediaDir(), "profissionais", proId, "2000-q.webp"));
  expect((await readProfessionalPhoto(proId, "q"))?.name).toBe("2000.webp");
});
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "@/server/db";
import { professionals, profiles, users } from "@/server/db/schema";
import { insertSession } from "@/server/auth/sessions-store";
import { resetDb } from "../../../../../../test/db";
import { GET, POST } from "./route";

const png = async () => { const { default: sharp } = await import("sharp"); return sharp({ create: { width: 64, height: 64, channels: 3, background: "#3f7d4f" } }).png().toBuffer(); };
async function session(perms: string[] | "atendente") {
  const [profile] = perms === "atendente"
    ? await db.select().from(profiles).where(eq(profiles.key, "atendente"))
    : await db.insert(profiles).values({ name: `Teste ${perms.join(" ")}`, permissions: perms }).returning();
  const [u] = await db.insert(users).values({ name: "Pessoa", email: `${Math.random()}@c.com`, passwordHash: "x", profileId: profile.id }).returning();
  return `cf_session=${(await insertSession(u.id)).token}`;
}
const ctx = (id: string) => ({ params: Promise.resolve({ id }) });
async function post(id: string, cookie: string | null, data?: Uint8Array) {
  const fd = new FormData();
  if (data) fd.append("foto", new File([new Uint8Array(data)], "minha-foto.png"));
  return POST(new NextRequest(`http://localhost/api/profissionais/${id}/foto`, { method: "POST", body: fd, headers: cookie ? { cookie } : {} }), ctx(id));
}
const get = (id: string, cookie: string, q = "") => GET(new NextRequest(`http://localhost/api/profissionais/${id}/foto${q}`, { headers: { cookie } }), ctx(id));

let proId: string;
beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(); proId = (await db.insert(professionals).values({ name: "Ana" }).returning())[0].id; });

it("envio: sem sessão 401; sem config.editar nem agenda.marcar 403; a recepção (agenda.marcar) envia", async () => {
  expect((await post(proId, null, await png())).status).toBe(401);
  expect((await post(proId, await session(["atendimento.ver"]), await png())).status).toBe(403);
  const res = await post(proId, await session("atendente"), await png());
  expect(res.status).toBe(200);
  expect((await res.json()).photoVersion).toMatch(/^\d+$/);
});

it("envio sem arquivo ou de outro tipo: 400 com o texto para a tela; nada de caminho do disco na resposta", async () => {
  const c = await session("atendente");
  expect(await (await post(proId, c)).json()).toEqual({ error: "Escolha uma foto." });
  const r = await post(proId, c, new TextEncoder().encode("<html>oi</html>"));
  expect(r.status).toBe(400);
  expect(await r.json()).toEqual({ error: "Use uma foto JPG, PNG ou WEBP." });
});

it("leitura: quem vê o atendimento recebe a miniatura em cache longo; sem foto 404; sem permissão 403", async () => {
  const c = await session("atendente");
  const { photoVersion } = await (await post(proId, c, await png())).json();
  const res = await get(proId, c, `?v=${photoVersion}`);
  expect(res.status).toBe(200);
  expect(res.headers.get("content-type")).toBe("image/webp");
  expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("private, max-age=31536000, immutable");
  expect((await get(proId, c, `?v=${photoVersion}&tam=grande`)).headers.get("content-type")).toBe("image/png");
  const other = (await db.insert(professionals).values({ name: "Bia" }).returning())[0].id;
  expect((await get(other, c)).status).toBe(404);
  expect((await get(proId, await session(["relatorios.ver"]))).status).toBe(403);
});
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `src/lib/pro-photo.ts`**

```ts
/** Foto da profissional (6.10): o limite e a URL servem à tela e ao servidor. O caminho no disco nunca sai do servidor. */
export const PHOTO_MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

/** "profissionais/<id>/1760000000000.png" → "1760000000000" (a versão muda a cada troca e quebra o cache). */
export const photoVersionOf = (photoPath: string | null): string | null => (photoPath ? (photoPath.split("/").pop() ?? "").replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, "") || null : null);

export const proPhotoUrl = (id: string, version: string | null, size: "q" | "full" = "q"): string | null =>
  version ? `/api/profissionais/${id}/foto?v=${encodeURIComponent(version)}${size === "full" ? "&tam=grande" : ""}` : null;
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/server/domain/professional-photo.ts`**

```ts
import path from "node:path";
import { mkdir, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { auditLog, professionals } from "../db/schema";
import { isUuid } from "@/lib/uuid";
import { PHOTO_MAX_BYTES } from "@/lib/pro-photo";
import { mediaDir, readMediaFile } from "../whatsapp/media";
import { sniffMime, type SafeMime } from "../security/sniff";
import { UserFacingError } from "./errors";

const EXT: Partial<Record<SafeMime, string>> = { "image/jpeg": "jpg", "image/png": "png", "image/webp": "webp" };
const THUMB = "-q.webp";

/** Miniatura quadrada de 256 px (lista e cabeçalho da Agenda). Usa o `sharp` que vem com o Next; sem ele, null (a tela recorta a original). */
export async function makeThumb(data: Uint8Array): Promise<Buffer | null> {
  try {
    const { default: sharp } = await import("sharp");
    return await sharp(data).rotate().resize(256, 256, { fit: "cover" }).webp({ quality: 80 }).toBuffer();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[foto da profissional] miniatura:", e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);
    return null;
  }
}

/** Grava a foto no MEDIA_DIR (fora da pasta da versão), a miniatura ao lado, troca no cadastro e apaga a anterior. Tipo pelo conteúdo. */
export async function saveProfessionalPhoto(id: string, data: Uint8Array, userId: string, now = new Date()): Promise<{ photoVersion: string }> {
  if (!isUuid(id)) throw new UserFacingError("Profissional não encontrada.");
  if (!data.length) throw new UserFacingError("Escolha uma foto.");
  if (data.length > PHOTO_MAX_BYTES) throw new UserFacingError("A foto passa de 5 MB. Escolha uma menor.");
  const mime = sniffMime(data.subarray(0, 32));
  const ext = mime ? EXT[mime] : undefined;
  if (!mime || !ext) throw new UserFacingError("Use uma foto JPG, PNG ou WEBP.");
  const [p] = await db.select({ photoPath: professionals.photoPath }).from(professionals).where(eq(professionals.id, id));
  if (!p) throw new UserFacingError("Profissional não encontrada.");

  const version = String(now.getTime());
  const rel = `profissionais/${id}/${version}.${ext}`;
  const dir = path.join(mediaDir(), "profissionais", id);
  await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(path.join(dir, `${version}.${ext}`), data);
  const thumb = await makeThumb(data);
  if (thumb) await writeFile(path.join(dir, `${version}${THUMB}`), thumb);
  await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    await tx.update(professionals).set({ photoPath: rel }).where(eq(professionals.id, id));
    await tx.insert(auditLog).values({ userId, action: "profissional_foto", entity: "professional", entityId: id, data: { tipo: mime, tamanho: data.length } });
  });
  if (p.photoPath && p.photoPath !== rel) {
    const old = path.join(mediaDir(), p.photoPath);
    await rm(old, { force: true }).catch(() => {});
    await rm(old.replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, THUMB), { force: true }).catch(() => {});
  }
  return { photoVersion: version };
}

/** A foto para servir: "q" = miniatura (ou a original, se não houver); "full" = a original. */
export async function readProfessionalPhoto(id: string, size: "q" | "full"): Promise<{ data: Buffer; mime: SafeMime | null; name: string } | null> {
  if (!isUuid(id)) return null;
  const [p] = await db.select({ photoPath: professionals.photoPath }).from(professionals).where(eq(professionals.id, id));
  if (!p?.photoPath) return null;
  const original = path.join(mediaDir(), p.photoPath);
  const candidates = size === "q" ? [original.replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, THUMB), original] : [original];
  for (const file of candidates) {
    const data = await readMediaFile(file).catch(() => null);
    if (data) return { data, mime: sniffMime(data.subarray(0, 32)), name: path.basename(file) };
  }
  return null;
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 5: A rota `src/app/api/profissionais/[id]/foto/route.ts`**

```ts
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { PHOTO_MAX_BYTES } from "@/lib/pro-photo";
import { findSessionUser } from "@/server/auth/sessions-store";
import { readProfessionalPhoto, saveProfessionalPhoto } from "@/server/domain/professional-photo";
import { UserFacingError } from "@/server/domain/errors";
import { mediaHeaders } from "@/server/security/sniff";

const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) => Response.json(body, { status });

/** Enviar a foto: a gestão (Configurações) ou quem marca na Agenda. Nunca devolve o caminho do disco. */
export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: RouteContext<"/api/profissionais/[id]/foto">) {
  const user = await findSessionUser(req.cookies.get("cf_session")?.value);
  if (!user) return json({ error: "Entre de novo para enviar a foto." }, 401);
  if (!can(user.perms, "config.editar") && !can(user.perms, "agenda.marcar")) return json({ error: "Você não tem permissão para isso." }, 403);
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const form = await req.formData().catch(() => null);
  const file = form?.get("foto");
  if (!(file instanceof File) || file.size === 0) return json({ error: "Escolha uma foto." }, 400);
  if (file.size > PHOTO_MAX_BYTES) return json({ error: "A foto passa de 5 MB. Escolha uma menor." }, 413);
  try {
    return json(await saveProfessionalPhoto(id, new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()), user.id));
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof UserFacingError) return json({ error: e.message }, 400);
    console.error("[foto da profissional]", e instanceof Error ? e.message : e);
    return json({ error: "Não foi possível enviar a foto. Tente de novo." }, 500);
  }
}

/** Ler a foto: quem vê a Agenda (atendimento.ver) ou a gestão. A URL tem a versão, então o cache pode ser longo. */
export async function GET(req: NextRequest, ctx: RouteContext<"/api/profissionais/[id]/foto">) {
  const user = await findSessionUser(req.cookies.get("cf_session")?.value);
  if (!user) return new Response("não autorizado", { status: 401 });
  if (!can(user.perms, "atendimento.ver") && !can(user.perms, "config.editar")) return new Response("sem permissão", { status: 403 });
  const { id } = await ctx.params;
  const photo = await readProfessionalPhoto(id, req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("tam") === "grande" ? "full" : "q");
  if (!photo) return new Response("sem foto", { status: 404 });
  const headers = mediaHeaders(photo.mime?.startsWith("image/") ? photo.mime : null, photo.name);
  return new Response(new Uint8Array(photo.data), { headers: { ...headers, "cache-control": "private, max-age=31536000, immutable", "content-length": String(photo.data.length) } });
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 7: `src/components/config/photo-field.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { ProAvatar } from "@/components/agenda/pro-avatar";
import { PHOTO_MAX_BYTES, proPhotoUrl } from "@/lib/pro-photo";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

const PICK = "inline-flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong px-3.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-paper-2 focus-within:ring-2 focus-within:ring-gold lg:min-h-9";

/** Foto grande com "Enviar foto" e, no celular, "Tirar foto" (abre a câmera da frente). Envia na hora; a Agenda recarrega com a nova. */
export function PhotoField({ professionalId, name, color, photoUrl }: { professionalId: string; name: string; color: string | null; photoUrl: string | null }) {
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const send = async (file: File | undefined) => {
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.size > PHOTO_MAX_BYTES) { toast("A foto passa de 5 MB. Escolha uma menor.", "error"); return; }
    setPending(true);
    try {
      const body = new FormData();
      body.append("foto", file);
      const res = await fetch(`/api/profissionais/${professionalId}/foto`, { method: "POST", body });
      const r = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { photoVersion?: string; error?: string };
      if (!res.ok || !r.photoVersion) { toast(r.error ?? "Não foi possível enviar a foto. Tente de novo.", "error"); return; }
      setShown(proPhotoUrl(professionalId, r.photoVersion, "full"));
      toast("Foto salva");
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      toast("Sem conexão: a foto não foi enviada. Tente de novo.", "error");
    } finally { setPending(false); }
  };
  const input = (label: string, extra: Record<string, string>) => (
    <label className={cn(PICK, pending && "pointer-events-none opacity-60")}>
      {label}
      <input type="file" aria-label={label} accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" className="sr-only" disabled={pending} {...extra}
        onChange={(e) => { void send(e.target.files?.[0]); e.target.value = ""; }} />
    </label>
  );
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
      <ProAvatar name={name} color={color} photoUrl={shown ?? photoUrl} size={96} />
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {input(pending ? "Enviando…" : "Enviar foto", {})}
          <span className="lg:hidden">{input("Tirar foto", { capture: "user", accept: "image/*" })}</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">JPG, PNG ou WEBP, até 5 MB.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

(Enquanto envia, o rótulo vira "Enviando…"; o teste procura "Enviar foto" antes do envio.)

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { act, cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import type { BusinessHours } from "@/lib/business-hours";

const actions = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  updateProfessionalAction: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })), setAppearanceAction: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })),
  setProfessionalServiceAction: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })), acceptSuggestionsAction: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })),
  setWorkHoursAction: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })), createProfessionalAction: vi.fn(async () => ({ ok: true })),
}));
const toast = vi.fn();
vi.mock("@/app/(app)/config/actions", () => actions);
vi.mock("@/components/ui/toast", () => ({ useToast: () => toast }));
vi.mock("next/navigation", () => ({ useRouter: () => ({ refresh: vi.fn(), push: vi.fn() }), usePathname: () => "/config/profissionais" }));
import { ProfessionalPanel } from "./professional-sheet";

afterEach(() => { cleanup(); toast.mockReset(); Object.values(actions).forEach((f) => f.mockClear()); });
const CASA: BusinessHours = { "0": null, "1": null, "2": ["09:00", "18:00"], "3": ["09:00", "18:00"], "4": ["09:00", "18:00"], "5": ["09:00", "18:00"], "6": ["09:00", "18:00"] };
const PRO = { id: "p1", name: "Ana", fullName: null, role: "Cabeleireira", active: true, serviceIds: ["s1"], workHours: null, color: null, agendaPosition: null, photoVersion: null };
const SERVICES = [
  { id: "s1", label: "Corte", category: "Cabelo" }, { id: "s2", label: "Hidratação", category: "Cabelo" },
  { id: "s3", label: "Manicure", category: "Unhas" }, { id: "s4", label: "Café", category: null },
];
const SUG = [{ professionalId: "p1", professionalName: "Ana", serviceId: "s2", serviceLabel: "Hidratação", count: 7 }, { professionalId: "p1", professionalName: "Ana", serviceId: "s3", serviceLabel: "Manicure", count: 3 }];
const panel = () => render(<ProfessionalPanel pro={PRO} services={SERVICES} casa={CASA} suggestions={SUG} />);

it("ficha em blocos, na ordem da 6.10; sem horário próprio, diz que segue a Casa", () => {
  panel();
  const ficha = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Ficha de Ana" });
  expect(within(ficha).getAllByRole("heading", { level: 2 }).map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual(["Foto", "Dados", "Dias e horário de trabalho", "Serviços que faz"]);
  expect(within(ficha).getByText(/Segue o horário da Casa: ter–sáb, 9h–18h/)).toBeTruthy();
  expect(within(ficha).getByLabelText("Enviar foto")).toBeTruthy();
});

it("serviços por categoria, com busca e caixa de marcar; marcar grava na hora", async () => {
  panel();
  const bloco = screen.getByRole("region", { name: "Serviços que faz" });
  expect(within(bloco).getAllByRole("group").map((g) => g.getAttribute("aria-label"))).toEqual(["Cabelo", "Unhas", "Outros"]);
  fireEvent.change(within(bloco).getByRole("searchbox", { name: "Buscar serviço" }), { target: { value: "hidra" } });
  expect(within(bloco).getAllByRole("checkbox").map((c) => c.getAttribute("aria-label"))).toEqual(["Hidratação"]);
  await act(async () => { fireEvent.click(within(bloco).getByRole("checkbox", { name: "Hidratação" })); });
  expect(actions.setProfessionalServiceAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith("p1", "s2", true);
});

it("o aviso do Avec: 'Ver e marcar' mostra o que ela fez e marca tudo de uma vez", async () => {
  panel();
  expect(screen.getByText("O Avec mostra que ela fez mais 2 serviços este ano")).toBeTruthy();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Ver e marcar" }));
  expect(screen.getByText("fez 7 vezes")).toBeTruthy();
  await act(async () => { fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Marcar todos" })); });
  expect(actions.acceptSuggestionsAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ professionalId: "p1" });
});

it("Salvar só com mudança; Desativar com horários marcados oferece 'Desativar mesmo assim' (força e fica registrado)", async () => {
  panel();
  const salvar = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Salvar" });
  expect((salvar as HTMLButtonElement).disabled).toBe(true);
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Nome na agenda"), { target: { value: "Aninha" } });
  await act(async () => { fireEvent.click(salvar); });
  expect(actions.updateProfessionalAction).toHaveBeenCalledWith("p1", { name: "Aninha", fullName: "", role: "Cabeleireira" });

  actions.updateProfessionalAction.mockResolvedValueOnce({ ok: false, error: "Ela tem 1 horário marcado: remarque antes. Paula 16/10 10:00" } as never);
  await act(async () => { fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Desativar" })); });
  expect(toast).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("Ela tem 1 horário marcado: remarque antes. Paula 16/10 10:00", "error", expect.objectContaining({ label: "Desativar mesmo assim" }));
  const force = toast.mock.calls.at(-1)![2] as { onClick: () => void };
  await act(async () => { force.onClick(); });
  expect(actions.updateProfessionalAction).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("p1", { active: false, force: true });
});
```

```tsx
});
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import type { WhoSuggestion } from "@/server/domain/agenda/who-does-what";
import type { Service } from "./professional-sheet";

const fold = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{M}/gu, "").toLowerCase().trim();
const OTHERS = "Outros";

/** Serviços que ela faz, agrupados por categoria (Listas › Serviços), com busca e caixa de marcar; o aviso das sugestões do Avec (6A). */
export function ServicesByCategory({ services, value, onToggle, suggestions, onAcceptAll, pending }: {
  services: Service[]; value: string[]; onToggle: (id: string) => void; suggestions: WhoSuggestion[]; onAcceptAll: () => void; pending: boolean;
}) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [showSug, setShowSug] = useState(false);
  const open = suggestions.filter((s) => !value.includes(s.serviceId));
  const sugIds = new Set(open.map((s) => s.serviceId));
  const shown = services.filter((s) => !fold(q) || fold(s.label).includes(fold(q)));
  const groups = new Map<string, Service[]>();
  for (const s of shown) { const k = s.category?.trim() || OTHERS; groups.set(k, [...(groups.get(k) ?? []), s]); }
  const names = [...groups.keys()].sort((a, b) => (a === OTHERS ? 1 : b === OTHERS ? -1 : a.localeCompare(b, "pt-BR")));
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {open.length > 0 && (
        <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-gold/40 bg-gold-soft/60 px-4 py-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <span className="font-medium text-gold-ink">{`O Avec mostra que ela fez mais ${open.length} ${open.length === 1 ? "serviço" : "serviços"} este ano`}</span>
            <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" aria-expanded={showSug} onClick={() => setShowSug((v) => !v)}>Ver e marcar</Button>
          </div>
          {showSug && (
            <>
              <ul className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
                {open.map((s) => <li key={s.serviceId}><span className="font-medium">{s.serviceLabel}</span> · <span className="text-graphite">{`fez ${s.count} ${s.count === 1 ? "vez" : "vezes"}`}</span></li>)}
              </ul>
              <Button size="sm" variant="gold" className="mt-3" loading={pending} onClick={onAcceptAll}>Marcar todos</Button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      <input type="search" aria-label="Buscar serviço" placeholder="Buscar serviço" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        className="h-11 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong bg-paper px-3 text-sm lg:h-10" />
      {names.map((cat) => (
        <fieldset key={cat} role="group" aria-label={cat} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <legend className="mb-1 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted">{cat}</legend>
          {groups.get(cat)!.map((s) => (
            <label key={s.id} className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] px-2 hover:bg-paper-2 lg:min-h-9">
              <input type="checkbox" aria-label={s.label} className="size-4 accent-[var(--color-ink)]" checked={value.includes(s.id)} onChange={() => onToggle(s.id)} />
              <span className="flex-1 text-sm">{s.label}</span>
              {sugIds.has(s.id) && <span className="rounded-full bg-gold-soft px-2 text-xs text-gold-ink">sugestão do Avec</span>}
            </label>
          ))}
        </fieldset>
      ))}
      {shown.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nenhum serviço com esse nome.</p>}
      {value.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-gold-ink">Sem serviço marcado: ela não aparece ao marcar nem ao procurar horário.</p>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/components/config/professional-sheet.tsx` (reescrito)**

O arquivo inteiro:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { acceptSuggestionsAction, setAppearanceAction, setProfessionalServiceAction, updateProfessionalAction } from "@/app/(app)/config/actions";
import type { WhoSuggestion } from "@/server/domain/agenda/who-does-what";
import type { BusinessHours } from "@/lib/business-hours";
import type { WorkHours } from "@/lib/work-hours";
import { PALETTE, textOn } from "@/lib/palette";
import { proPhotoUrl } from "@/lib/pro-photo";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { WorkHoursEditor } from "./work-hours-editor";
import { PhotoField } from "./photo-field";
import { ServicesByCategory } from "./services-by-category";

export type Pro = {
  id: string; name: string; fullName: string | null; role: string | null; active: boolean; serviceIds: string[];
  workHours: WorkHours | null; color: string | null; agendaPosition: number | null; photoVersion: string | null;
};
export type Service = { id: string; label: string; category?: string | null };

/** Marca/desmarca serviços em botões (o "Adicionar profissional" e a matriz do 6A usam). */
export function ServicePicker({ services, value, onToggle, disabled }: { services: Service[]; value: string[]; onToggle: (id: string) => void; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
      {services.map((s) => {
        const on = value.includes(s.id);
        return (
          <button key={s.id} type="button" aria-pressed={on} disabled={disabled} onClick={() => onToggle(s.id)}
            className={cn("min-h-11 rounded-full border px-3 text-sm transition-colors disabled:opacity-60 lg:min-h-8",
              on ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper" : "border-line-strong text-graphite hover:bg-paper-2")}>{s.label}</button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function Block({ id, title, children }: { id: string; title: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-4 lg:p-5">
      <h2 id={id} className="mb-3 text-base font-semibold">{title}</h2>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

const same = (a: string, b: string | null) => a.trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ") === (b ?? "");

/**
 * A ficha da profissional (6.10), em blocos: Foto; Dados (nome na agenda, cargo, nome completo, cor e ordem); Dias e horário de trabalho
 * (sem horário próprio, segue o da Casa, e a tela diz); Serviços que faz (por categoria, com as sugestões do Avec); Salvar e Desativar.
 * Foto, horário e serviços gravam na hora; "Salvar" grava os Dados. O e-mail para o app dela entra com o 7A.
 */
export function ProfessionalPanel({ pro, services, casa, suggestions }: { pro: Pro; services: Service[]; casa: BusinessHours; suggestions: WhoSuggestion[] }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(pro.name);
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(pro.fullName ?? "");
  const [role, setRole] = useState(pro.role ?? "");
  const [color, setColor] = useState<string | null>(pro.color);
  const [position, setPosition] = useState(pro.agendaPosition ? String(pro.agendaPosition) : "");
  const [ids, setIds] = useState(pro.serviceIds);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const pos = position.trim() === "" ? null : Number(position);
  const dirtyData = !same(name, pro.name) || !same(fullName, pro.fullName) || !same(role, pro.role);
  const dirtyLook = color !== pro.color || pos !== pro.agendaPosition;

  const save = () => start(async () => {
    if (pos !== null && (!Number.isInteger(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 999)) { toast("Use um número de 1 a 999 na ordem.", "error"); return; }
    if (dirtyData) { const r = await updateProfessionalAction(pro.id, { name, fullName, role }); if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; } }
    if (dirtyLook) { const r = await setAppearanceAction(pro.id, { color, agendaPosition: pos }); if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; } }
    toast("Dados salvos");
    router.refresh();
  });
  const setActive = (active: boolean) => start(async () => {
    const r = await updateProfessionalAction(pro.id, { active });
    if (r.ok) { toast(active ? `${pro.name} reativada` : `${pro.name} desativada: não aparece mais ao marcar`); router.refresh(); return; }
    if (active || !r.error.includes("remarque antes")) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    // 6.4, regra 14: com horários marcados o servidor recusa com a lista; forçar fica registrado.
    toast(r.error, "error", { label: "Desativar mesmo assim", onClick: () => start(async () => {
      const f = await updateProfessionalAction(pro.id, { active: false, force: true });
      toast(f.ok ? `${pro.name} desativada (os horários dela continuam na agenda)` : f.error, f.ok ? "ok" : "error");
      router.refresh();
    }) });
  });
  const toggle = (id: string) => {
    const on = !ids.includes(id);
    setIds((x) => (on ? [...x, id] : x.filter((s) => s !== id)));
    start(async () => {
      const r = await setProfessionalServiceAction(pro.id, id, on);
      if (!r.ok) { setIds((x) => (on ? x.filter((s) => s !== id) : [...x, id])); toast(r.error, "error"); }
    });
  };
  const acceptAll = () => start(async () => {
    const r = await acceptSuggestionsAction({ professionalId: pro.id });
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    setIds((x) => [...new Set([...x, ...suggestions.map((s) => s.serviceId)])]);
    toast("Serviços marcados");
  });
  const label = "flex flex-col gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-graphite";

  return (
    <section aria-label={`Ficha de ${pro.name}`} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <Block id={`foto-${pro.id}`} title="Foto">
        <PhotoField professionalId={pro.id} name={pro.name} color={color} photoUrl={proPhotoUrl(pro.id, pro.photoVersion, "full")} />
      </Block>
      <Block id={`dados-${pro.id}`} title="Dados">
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <label className={label}>Nome na agenda<Input value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} maxLength={60} /></label>
          <label className={label}>Cargo<Input value={role} onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)} placeholder="não informado" maxLength={60} /></label>
          <label className={cn(label, "sm:col-span-2")}>Nome completo
            <Input value={fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} placeholder="não informado" maxLength={120} />
            <span className="font-normal text-muted">Liga com as vendas que vêm do Avec.</span>
          </label>
        </div>
        <fieldset className="mt-4">
          <legend className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-graphite">Cor na agenda</legend>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Cor na agenda">
            {PALETTE.map((c) => (
              <button key={c.key} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={color === c.key} aria-label={c.label} title={c.label} onClick={() => setColor(c.key)}
                className={cn("grid size-11 place-items-center rounded-full border-2 transition-transform lg:size-9", color === c.key ? "scale-110 border-ink" : "border-transparent")}
                style={{ background: c.hex, color: textOn(c.hex) }}>{color === c.key ? "✓" : ""}</button>
            ))}
            <button type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={color === null} onClick={() => setColor(null)}
              className={cn("h-11 rounded-full border px-3 text-sm lg:h-9", color === null ? "border-ink bg-paper-2" : "border-line-strong text-graphite")}>Sem cor</button>
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        <label className={cn(label, "mt-4")}>Ordem na agenda
          <Input type="number" inputMode="numeric" min={1} max={999} value={position} onChange={(e) => setPosition(e.target.value)} placeholder="pelo nome" className="w-32" />
        </label>
      </Block>
      <Block id={`horario-${pro.id}`} title="Dias e horário de trabalho">
        <WorkHoursEditor professionalId={pro.id} name={pro.name} value={pro.workHours} casa={casa} />
      </Block>
      <Block id={`servicos-${pro.id}`} title="Serviços que faz">
        <ServicesByCategory services={services} value={ids} onToggle={toggle} suggestions={suggestions} onAcceptAll={acceptAll} pending={pending} />
      </Block>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <Button variant={pro.active ? "danger" : "secondary"} loading={pending} onClick={() => setActive(!pro.active)}>{pro.active ? "Desativar" : "Reativar"}</Button>
        <Button loading={pending} disabled={!dirtyData && !dirtyLook} onClick={save}>Salvar</Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

(O `WorkHoursEditor` do 6A já escreve "Segue o horário da Casa: …" quando a profissional não tem horário próprio; o teste confere essa frase.)

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { createProfessionalAction } from "@/app/(app)/config/actions";
import type { WhoSuggestion } from "@/server/domain/agenda/who-does-what";
import type { BusinessHours } from "@/lib/business-hours";
import { proPhotoUrl } from "@/lib/pro-photo";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import { ProAvatar } from "@/components/agenda/pro-avatar";
import { ProfessionalPanel, ServicePicker, type Pro, type Service } from "./professional-sheet";
import { WhoDoesWhat } from "./who-does-what";

const plural = (n: number, um: string, varios: string) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? um : varios}`;
const BASE = "/config/profissionais";

/**
 * Profissionais (6.10): à esquerda as ativas (foto, nome, "Cargo · N serviços") e "Desativadas · N" recolhido; à direita a ficha da
 * escolhida (`?id=`), em blocos. No celular, uma coisa de cada vez: a lista, ou a ficha com "‹ Profissionais". A aba "Quem faz o quê" (6A)
 * continua igual.
 */
export function ProfessionalsEditor({ tab, items, services, casa, suggestions, selectedId }: {
  tab: "lista" | "matriz"; items: Pro[]; services: Service[]; casa: BusinessHours; suggestions: WhoSuggestion[]; selectedId: string | null;
}) {
  const opened = items.find((p) => p.id === selectedId) ?? null;
  const active = items.filter((p) => p.active), inactive = items.filter((p) => !p.active);
  const suggestionsOf = (id: string) => suggestions.filter((s) => s.professionalId === id);

  if (!services.length) return (
    <p className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper px-4 py-6 text-sm text-graphite">
      Cadastre primeiro os serviços da Casa em <Link className="font-medium text-gold-ink underline underline-offset-4" href="/config/listas?aba=service">Listas e respostas › Serviços</Link>.
    </p>
  );
  const row = (p: Pro) => (
    <li key={p.id}>
      <Link href={`${BASE}?id=${p.id}`} aria-label={`Abrir ${p.name}`} aria-current={p.id === selectedId ? "page" : undefined}
        className={cn("flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 transition-colors", p.id === selectedId ? "bg-paper-2" : "hover:bg-paper-2/70")}>
        <ProAvatar name={p.name} color={p.color} photoUrl={proPhotoUrl(p.id, p.photoVersion)} size={40} />
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span className="block truncate font-medium">{p.name}</span>
          <span className="block truncate text-sm text-graphite">{[p.role, plural(p.serviceIds.length, "serviço", "serviços")].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}</span>
        </span>
      </Link>
    </li>
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <nav aria-label="Profissionais e serviços" className="mb-4 inline-flex rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong p-1">
        {([["lista", "Profissionais", BASE], ["matriz", "Quem faz o quê", `${BASE}?aba=matriz`]] as const).map(([v, label, href]) => (
          <Link key={v} href={href} aria-current={tab === v ? "page" : undefined}
            className={cn("inline-flex h-11 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[calc(var(--radius-control)-4px)] px-3.5 text-sm font-medium transition-colors sm:h-8",
              tab === v ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "text-graphite hover:text-ink")}>
            {label}{v === "matriz" && suggestions.length > 0 && <span className={cn("rounded-full px-1.5 text-xs tabular", tab === v ? "bg-paper/20" : "bg-gold-soft text-gold-ink")}>{suggestions.length}</span>}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      {tab === "matriz" ? <WhoDoesWhat pros={active} services={services} suggestions={suggestions} /> : (
        <div className="grid items-start gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[300px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <aside className={cn("flex flex-col gap-3", selectedId && "hidden lg:flex")}>
            <Link href={`${BASE}?id=nova`} aria-label="+ Adicionar profissional"
              className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-dashed border-line-strong text-sm font-medium hover:bg-paper-2 lg:min-h-10">+ Adicionar profissional</Link>
            <ul aria-label="Profissionais ativas" className="divide-y divide-line/70 overflow-hidden rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper">
              {active.map(row)}
              {active.length === 0 && <li className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">Nenhuma profissional ativa.</li>}
            </ul>
            {inactive.length > 0 && (
              <details className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper">
                <summary className="flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer items-center px-4 text-sm font-medium text-graphite">{`Desativadas · ${inactive.length}`}</summary>
                <ul aria-label="Profissionais desativadas" className="divide-y divide-line/70 border-t border-line opacity-80">{inactive.map(row)}</ul>
              </details>
            )}
          </aside>
          <div className={cn("min-w-0", !selectedId && "hidden lg:block")}>
            {selectedId && <Link href={BASE} className="mb-3 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-sm font-medium text-graphite lg:hidden">‹ Profissionais</Link>}
            {selectedId === "nova" ? <AddForm services={services} />
              : opened ? <ProfessionalPanel key={opened.id} pro={opened} services={services} casa={casa} suggestions={suggestionsOf(opened.id)} />
              : <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper"><Empty title="Escolha uma profissional na lista">A ficha dela abre aqui: foto, dados, horário de trabalho e serviços.</Empty></div>}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

(O `AddForm` continua usando `useState`, `useTransition`, `Input`, `Button`, `useToast`, `createProfessionalAction` e `ServicePicker`: por isso os imports ficam.)

```tsx
import { requirePerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { listProfessionals } from "@/server/domain/professionals";
import { getBusinessHours } from "@/server/domain/hours";
import { suggestProfessionalServices } from "@/server/domain/agenda/who-does-what";
import { listBookableServices } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { ProfessionalsEditor } from "@/components/config/professionals";

export const metadata = { title: "Profissionais" };

/**
 * Profissionais (6.10): lista à esquerda e a ficha da escolhida (`?id=`) à direita; `?id=nova` abre "Adicionar profissional".
 * `?aba=matriz` abre a matriz Quem faz o quê (6A). Os serviços vêm de Listas › Serviços, com a categoria.
 */
export default async function ProfissionaisPage({ searchParams }: PageProps<"/config/profissionais">) {
  await requirePerm("config.editar");
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const tab = sp.aba === "matriz" ? "matriz" : "lista";
  const [items, services, casa, who] = await Promise.all([listProfessionals({ all: true }), listBookableServices(), getBusinessHours(), suggestProfessionalServices()]);
  const id = typeof sp.id === "string" ? sp.id : null;
  const selectedId = id === "nova" || items.some((p) => p.id === id) ? id : null;
  return <ProfessionalsEditor tab={tab} items={items} services={services} casa={casa} suggestions={who.suggestions} selectedId={selectedId} />;
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { appointments, deals, visits } from "../../db/schema";
import { resetDb } from "../../../../test/db";
import { ap, bahia, cliente, NOW, setupAgenda } from "../../../../test/agenda-fixtures";
import { applyScheduledMigration, planScheduledMigration, undoScheduledMigration } from "./migrate-scheduled";

let s: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof setupAgenda>>;

/** Um lead em Agendado com o horário só no lead (como antes do 6B). */
async function leadAgendado(name: string, startsAt: Date | null, over: Partial<typeof deals.$inferInsert> = {}) {
  const c = await cliente(name, { stage: "agendado" });
  await db.update(deals).set({ scheduledAt: startsAt, serviceId: s.corte.id, professionalId: s.ana.id, ...over }).where(eq(deals.id, c.dealId!));
  return c;
}

it("ensaio: o futuro entra; o que já passou e o que já está na agenda ficam de fora, com o motivo; arquivado e sem horário nem aparecem", async () => {
  const futuro = await leadAgendado("Paula Souza", bahia(16, 10), { attendance: "confirmado", attendanceAt: bahia(15, 8) });
  await leadAgendado("Rita Lima", bahia(14, 10));
  const naAgenda = await leadAgendado("Lia Souza", bahia(17, 10));
  await ap({ startsAt: bahia(17, 10), contactId: naAgenda.contactId, professionalId: s.ana.id });
  await leadAgendado("Arquivada", bahia(18, 10), { archivedAt: NOW });
  await leadAgendado("Sem Hora", null);
  const plan = await planScheduledMigration(NOW);
  expect(plan.items).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({

    professionalId: s.ana.id, durationMin: 60, status: "confirmado", confirmedAt: bahia(15, 8).toISOString(),
  })]);

  expect(await db.select().from(appointments)).toHaveLength(1); // o ensaio não grava
});

  const paula = await leadAgendado("Paula Souza", bahia(16, 10));

  expect(a).toMatchObject({ origin: "crm", dealId: paula.dealId, contactId: paula.contactId, status: "agendado", fitIn: true, externalKey: `crm:${a.id}` });
  expect((await db.select().from(visits).where(eq(visits.id, a.visitId!)))[0]).toMatchObject({ day: "2026-10-16", contactId: paula.contactId });
  const again = await planScheduledMigration(NOW);
  expect(again.items).toEqual([]);
  expect((await applyScheduledMigration({ ...again, items: (await planScheduledMigration(NOW)).items }, NOW)).createdIds).toEqual([]);
});

it("desfazer tira só o que ninguém mexeu; o mexido fica e é contado", async () => {

});
```
