- [ ] **Passo 3: `src/server/domain/caixa/paid.ts`**

```ts
import { and, asc, eq, gt, gte, inArray, isNotNull, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../../db";
import { appointments, clientCreditEntries, clientPackages, comandaItems, comandas, contacts, salesPayments } from "../../db/schema";
import { dayOf, shiftDay, todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";

/**
 * Agenda › "Já pagaram" (7.9): quem já pagou e ainda não fez o serviço. Uma linha por cliente, com o que falta usar (pacote com
 * sessão e dentro da validade; crédito com saldo no CRM; serviço pago antes do horário; crédito no Avec sem extrato, só para conferir),
 * o próximo horário e o vencimento mais perto. Nunca inventa saldo.
 */
export type PaidKind = "pacote" | "credito" | "servico_pago" | "credito_avec";
export type PaidEntry = { kind: PaidKind; label: string; valueCents: number | null; paidOn: string | null; validUntil: string | null };
export type PaidRow = { contactId: string; name: string; phone: string | null; entries: PaidEntry[]; nextAppointmentAt: string | null; soonestExpiry: string | null };
export type PaidFilter = { semHorario?: boolean; venceEm30?: boolean; tipo?: "pacote" | "credito" | null; withValues?: boolean };

const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "UTC" });
const dm = (day: string) => DM.format(new Date(`${day}T12:00:00Z`));
const DMH = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });
const ORDER: PaidKind[] = ["pacote", "credito", "servico_pago", "credito_avec"];

async function collect(now: Date): Promise<Map<string, PaidEntry[]>> {
  const today = todayBahia(now);
  const out = new Map<string, PaidEntry[]>();
  const push = (id: string, e: PaidEntry) => out.set(id, [...(out.get(id) ?? []), e]);

  const pkgs = await db.select().from(clientPackages).where(and(isNotNull(clientPackages.contactId), isNull(clientPackages.cancelledAt),
    sql`${clientPackages.totalSessions} > ${clientPackages.usedSessions}`, sql`(${clientPackages.validUntil} is null or ${clientPackages.validUntil} >= ${today})`))
    .orderBy(sql`${clientPackages.validUntil} asc nulls last`, asc(clientPackages.packageName));
  for (const p of pkgs) push(p.contactId!, {
    kind: "pacote", label: `${p.packageName} · faltam ${p.totalSessions - p.usedSessions} de ${p.totalSessions}${p.validUntil ? ` · vence ${dm(p.validUntil)}` : ""}`,
    valueCents: p.priceCents, paidOn: p.purchasedOn, validUntil: p.validUntil,
  });

  const credits = await db.select({ id: clientCreditEntries.contactId, balance: sql<number>`sum(${clientCreditEntries.deltaCents})::int`, last: sql<string>`to_char(max(${clientCreditEntries.createdAt}) at time zone 'America/Bahia', 'YYYY-MM-DD')` })
    .from(clientCreditEntries).groupBy(clientCreditEntries.contactId).having(sql`sum(${clientCreditEntries.deltaCents}) > 0`);
  for (const c of credits) push(c.id, { kind: "credito", label: "Crédito da cliente", valueCents: c.balance, paidOn: c.last, validUntil: null });

  const prepaid = await db.select({ contactId: appointments.contactId, label: appointments.serviceLabel, startsAt: appointments.startsAt, total: comandaItems.totalCents, day: comandas.day })
    .from(comandaItems).innerJoin(comandas, eq(comandas.id, comandaItems.comandaId)).innerJoin(appointments, eq(appointments.id, comandaItems.appointmentId))
    .where(and(eq(comandas.status, "fechada"), isNull(comandaItems.cancelledAt), gt(appointments.startsAt, now), inArray(appointments.status, ["agendado", "confirmado"]), isNotNull(appointments.contactId)))
    .orderBy(asc(appointments.startsAt));
  for (const a of prepaid) push(a.contactId!, { kind: "servico_pago", label: `${a.label} pago antes do horário (${DMH.format(a.startsAt).replace(",", "")})`, valueCents: a.total, paidOn: a.day, validUntil: null });

  const avec = await db.selectDistinct({ id: salesPayments.contactId }).from(salesPayments)
    .where(and(isNotNull(salesPayments.contactId), sql`translate(lower(${salesPayments.method}), 'áéíóúâêôãõç', 'aeiouaeoaoc') like '%credito cliente%'`));
  for (const a of avec) if (!(out.get(a.id!) ?? []).some((e) => e.kind === "credito")) push(a.id!, { kind: "credito_avec", label: "Crédito no Avec (conferir)", valueCents: null, paidOn: null, validUntil: null });
  return out;
}

export async function listPaidNotUsed(f: PaidFilter, now = new Date()): Promise<PaidRow[]> {
  const byContact = await collect(now);
  const ids = [...byContact.keys()];
  if (!ids.length) return [];
  const people = await db.select({ id: contacts.id, name: contacts.name, phone: contacts.phoneE164 }).from(contacts).where(inArray(contacts.id, ids));
  const next = await db.select({ id: appointments.contactId, at: sql<Date>`min(${appointments.startsAt})` }).from(appointments)
    .where(and(inArray(appointments.contactId, ids), gte(appointments.startsAt, now), inArray(appointments.status, ["agendado", "confirmado", "solicitado"]), isNull(appointments.avecTwinOf)))
    .groupBy(appointments.contactId);
  const limit = shiftDay(dayOf(now), 30);
  let rows: PaidRow[] = people.map((p) => {
    const entries = [...byContact.get(p.id)!].sort((a, b) => ORDER.indexOf(a.kind) - ORDER.indexOf(b.kind)).map((e) => (f.withValues ? e : { ...e, valueCents: null }));
    const expiries = entries.map((e) => e.validUntil).filter((d): d is string => !!d).sort();
    const at = next.find((n) => n.id === p.id)?.at;
    return { contactId: p.id, name: p.name?.trim() || "Cliente", phone: p.phone, entries, nextAppointmentAt: at ? new Date(at).toISOString() : null, soonestExpiry: expiries[0] ?? null };
  });
  if (f.semHorario) rows = rows.filter((r) => !r.nextAppointmentAt);
  if (f.venceEm30) rows = rows.filter((r) => r.soonestExpiry && r.soonestExpiry <= limit);
  if (f.tipo === "pacote") rows = rows.filter((r) => r.entries.some((e) => e.kind === "pacote"));
  if (f.tipo === "credito") rows = rows.filter((r) => r.entries.some((e) => e.kind === "credito" || e.kind === "credito_avec"));
  return rows.sort((a, b) => (a.soonestExpiry ?? "9999").localeCompare(b.soonestExpiry ?? "9999") || a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "pt-BR"));
}

/** A contagem no título da aba. */
export async function countPaidNotUsed(now = new Date()): Promise<number> {
  return (await collect(now)).size;
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/server/queries/caixa.ts`**

```ts
import { and, asc, desc, eq, inArray, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { cashSessions, clientPackages, comandaItems, comandaPayments, comandas, professionals } from "../db/schema";
import { comandaTotals, itemTotal } from "@/lib/comanda-math";
import { todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { isUuid } from "@/lib/uuid";
import { teamNameOf } from "./team-name";
import { cashSummary, dayTotals, getOpenCashTx, type CashSummary } from "../domain/caixa/cash";
import { creditBalance } from "../domain/caixa/credits";
import { getCaixaMode, getCaixaRules } from "../domain/caixa/mode";
import type { CaixaMode, CaixaRules } from "@/lib/caixa-settings";

export type ComandaDetail = {
  id: string; number: number; day: string; status: "aberta" | "fechada" | "cancelada"; contactId: string | null; clientName: string; visitId: string | null;
  discountCents: number; items: { id: string; kind: string; label: string; qty: number; unitCents: number | null; discountCents: number; totalCents: number | null;
    professionalId: string | null; professionalName: string | null; appointmentId: string | null; clientPackageId: string | null; commissionCents: number | null }[];
  payments: { id: string; method: string; valueCents: number; installments: number | null }[];
  totals: { subtotalCents: number; discountCents: number; totalCents: number; missingPrice: string[] }; paidCents: number; remainingCents: number;
  creditBalance: number | null; closedByName: string | null; closedAt: string | null; changeCents: number | null; cancelReason: string | null;
  cashOpen: boolean; exigeCaixaAberto: boolean;
};

export async function getComandaDetail(id: string): Promise<ComandaDetail | null> {
  if (!isUuid(id)) return null;
  const [c] = await db.select({ c: comandas, closedByName: teamNameOf(comandas.closedBy) }).from(comandas).where(eq(comandas.id, id));
  if (!c) return null;
  const items = await db.select({ i: comandaItems, pro: professionals.name }).from(comandaItems).leftJoin(professionals, eq(professionals.id, comandaItems.professionalId))
    .where(and(eq(comandaItems.comandaId, id), isNull(comandaItems.cancelledAt))).orderBy(asc(comandaItems.createdAt));
  const pays = await db.select().from(comandaPayments).where(and(eq(comandaPayments.comandaId, id), isNull(comandaPayments.cancelledAt))).orderBy(asc(comandaPayments.createdAt));
  const math = items.map(({ i }) => ({ id: i.id, label: i.label, qty: Number(i.qty), unitCents: i.unitCents, discountCents: i.discountCents }));
  const t = comandaTotals(math, c.c.discountCents);
  const paidCents = pays.reduce((a, p) => a + p.valueCents, 0);
  const [open, rules] = await Promise.all([getOpenCashTx(db), getCaixaRules()]);
  return {
    id: c.c.id, number: c.c.number, day: c.c.day, status: c.c.status as ComandaDetail["status"], contactId: c.c.contactId, clientName: c.c.clientName ?? "Cliente de balcão", visitId: c.c.visitId,
    discountCents: c.c.discountCents,
    items: items.map(({ i, pro }, k) => ({ id: i.id, kind: i.kind, label: i.label, qty: Number(i.qty), unitCents: i.unitCents, discountCents: i.discountCents, totalCents: itemTotal(math[k]),
      professionalId: i.professionalId, professionalName: pro, appointmentId: i.appointmentId, clientPackageId: i.clientPackageId, commissionCents: i.commissionCents })),
    payments: pays.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, method: p.method, valueCents: p.valueCents, installments: p.installments })),
    totals: { subtotalCents: t.subtotalCents, discountCents: t.discountCents, totalCents: t.totalCents, missingPrice: t.missingPrice },
    paidCents, remainingCents: Math.max(0, t.totalCents - paidCents), creditBalance: c.c.contactId ? await creditBalance(c.c.contactId) : null,
    closedByName: c.closedByName, closedAt: c.c.closedAt?.toISOString() ?? null, changeCents: c.c.changeCents, cancelReason: c.c.cancelReason,
    cashOpen: !!open, exigeCaixaAberto: rules.exigeCaixaAberto,
  };
}

export type ComandaRow = { id: string; number: number; clientName: string; status: string; totalCents: number; openedAt: string; closedAt: string | null; closedByName: string | null };

export async function listDayComandas(day: string): Promise<{ abertas: ComandaRow[]; fechadas: ComandaRow[]; canceladas: number }> {
  const rows = await db.select({ c: comandas, closedByName: teamNameOf(comandas.closedBy) }).from(comandas).where(eq(comandas.day, day)).orderBy(asc(comandas.number));
  const openIds = rows.filter((r) => r.c.status === "aberta").map((r) => r.c.id);
  const items = openIds.length ? await db.select().from(comandaItems).where(and(inArray(comandaItems.comandaId, openIds), isNull(comandaItems.cancelledAt))) : [];
  const row = (r: (typeof rows)[number]): ComandaRow => ({
    id: r.c.id, number: r.c.number, clientName: r.c.clientName ?? "Cliente de balcão", status: r.c.status, openedAt: r.c.openedAt.toISOString(), closedAt: r.c.closedAt?.toISOString() ?? null, closedByName: r.closedByName,
    totalCents: r.c.status === "aberta"
      ? comandaTotals(items.filter((i) => i.comandaId === r.c.id).map((i) => ({ id: i.id, label: i.label, qty: Number(i.qty), unitCents: i.unitCents, discountCents: i.discountCents })), r.c.discountCents).totalCents
      : r.c.totalCents ?? 0,
  });
  return { abertas: rows.filter((r) => r.c.status === "aberta").map(row), fechadas: rows.filter((r) => r.c.status === "fechada").map(row), canceladas: rows.filter((r) => r.c.status === "cancelada").length };
}

export type CaixaHoje = { day: string; mode: CaixaMode; rules: CaixaRules; open: CashSummary | null; totals: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof dayTotals>>; comandas: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listDayComandas>> };

export async function getCaixaHoje(now = new Date()): Promise<CaixaHoje> {
  const day = todayBahia(now);
  const [mode, rules, open, totals, list] = await Promise.all([getCaixaMode(), getCaixaRules(), getOpenCashTx(db), dayTotals(day), listDayComandas(day)]);
  return { day, mode, rules, open: open ? await cashSummary(open.id) : null, totals, comandas: list };
}

export type CashSessionRow = { id: string; day: string; status: string; openingCents: number; openedByName: string | null; closedByName: string | null; closedAt: string | null; differenceCents: number | null; expected: Record<string, number> | null; counted: Record<string, number> | null };

export async function listCashSessions(limit = 30): Promise<CashSessionRow[]> {
  const rows = await db.select({ s: cashSessions, openedByName: teamNameOf(cashSessions.openedBy), closedByName: teamNameOf(cashSessions.closedBy) }).from(cashSessions).orderBy(desc(cashSessions.openedAt)).limit(limit);
  return rows.map(({ s, openedByName, closedByName }) => ({ id: s.id, day: s.day, status: s.status, openingCents: s.openingCents, openedByName, closedByName, closedAt: s.closedAt?.toISOString() ?? null, differenceCents: s.differenceCents, expected: s.expected, counted: s.counted }));
}

/** Pacotes da cliente para "Sessão de pacote" na comanda: com sessão e ainda válidos; os vencidos aparecem marcados (a gestão dá novo prazo). */
export async function clientPackagesFor(contactId: string, day: string) {
  if (!isUuid(contactId)) return [];
  const rows = await db.select().from(clientPackages).where(and(eq(clientPackages.contactId, contactId), isNull(clientPackages.cancelledAt), sql`${clientPackages.totalSessions} > ${clientPackages.usedSessions}`))
    .orderBy(sql`${clientPackages.validUntil} asc nulls last`);
  return rows.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, label: `${p.packageName} · ${p.serviceLabel ?? ""}`.replace(/ · $/, ""), remaining: p.totalSessions - p.usedSessions, validUntil: p.validUntil, expired: !!p.validUntil && p.validUntil < day }));
}
```

No `AgendaItem`: `/** Comanda aberta deste horário (6C): "falta fechar". */ openComandaId?: string | null;`. Em `getAgenda`, depois de montar os itens de horário, buscar as comandas dos horários do período e preencher:

```ts
  const apptIds = out.filter((i) => i.appointmentId).map((i) => i.appointmentId!);
  const links = apptIds.length ? await db.select({ appointmentId: comandaItems.appointmentId, comandaId: comandas.id, status: comandas.status })
    .from(comandaItems).innerJoin(comandas, eq(comandas.id, comandaItems.comandaId))
    .where(and(inArray(comandaItems.appointmentId, apptIds), isNull(comandaItems.cancelledAt), inArray(comandas.status, ["aberta", "fechada"]))) : [];
  for (const i of out) {
    const l = links.filter((x) => x.appointmentId === i.appointmentId);
    i.paid = l.some((x) => x.status === "fechada");
    i.openComandaId = l.find((x) => x.status === "aberta")?.comandaId ?? null;
```

- [ ] **Passo 7: As ações (`src/app/(app)/caixa/actions.ts`)**

```ts
"use server";
import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { z } from "zod";
import { assertPerm, type SessionUser } from "@/server/auth/session";
import type { Perm } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { PAY_METHODS } from "@/lib/comanda-math";
import {
  addItem, addPayment, cancelComanda, CashClosedError, closeComanda, discardComanda, joinTodayAppointments, openComandaForAppointment, openWalkInComanda,
  removeItem, removePayment, reopenForCorrection, setComandaClient, setComandaDiscount, updateItem, ITEM_KINDS,
} from "@/server/domain/caixa/comandas";
import { addMovement, closeCash, openCash, reopenCash } from "@/server/domain/caixa/cash";
import { saveCaixaSettings } from "@/server/domain/caixa/mode";
import { extendPackage, savePackageDef, setPackageSessionValue } from "@/server/domain/caixa/packages";
import { applyCommissionToCategory, setCommission } from "@/server/domain/caixa/commission";
import { clientPackagesFor, getComandaDetail } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { userErrorMessage } from "@/server/user-error";

export type ActionResult<T = undefined> = { ok: true; data?: T } | { ok: false; error: string; needsCash?: boolean };

const actorOf = (u: SessionUser) => ({ id: u.id, perms: u.perms });
const uuid = (v: string) => z.string().uuid().parse(v);
const cents = z.number().int().min(0).max(100_000_000);

async function run<T>(perm: Perm, fn: (u: SessionUser) => Promise<T>, opts: { fallback?: string; revalidate?: boolean } = {}): Promise<ActionResult<T>> {
  try {
    const u = await assertPerm(perm);
    const data = await fn(u);
    if (opts.revalidate !== false) { revalidatePath("/caixa"); revalidatePath("/agenda"); }
    return { ok: true, data };
  } catch (e) {
    const error = userErrorMessage(e, "caixa", opts.fallback ?? "Não foi possível registrar. Tente de novo.");
    return e instanceof CashClosedError ? { ok: false, error, needsCash: true } : { ok: false, error };
  }
}

const itemInput = z.object({
  kind: z.enum(ITEM_KINDS), serviceId: z.string().uuid().nullish(), label: z.string().max(120).nullish(), professionalId: z.string().uuid().nullish(),
  qty: z.number().positive().max(99).optional(), unitCents: cents.nullish(), discountCents: cents.nullish(), discountPercent: z.number().min(0).max(100).nullish(),
  clientPackageId: z.string().uuid().nullish(), packageDefId: z.string().uuid().nullish(),
});
const itemPatch = itemInput.omit({ kind: true, serviceId: true, clientPackageId: true, packageDefId: true }).partial();

/* Comanda: quem opera o caixa (caixa.operar). */
export const comandaDetailAction = async (id: string) => run("caixa.operar", () => getComandaDetail(uuid(id)), { revalidate: false });
export const openComandaAction = async (appointmentId: string) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => openComandaForAppointment(uuid(appointmentId), actorOf(u)));
export const openWalkInAction = async (input: { contactId?: string | null; clientName?: string | null; requestKey: string }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => openWalkInComanda(z.object({ contactId: z.string().uuid().nullish(), clientName: z.string().max(120).nullish(), requestKey: z.string().uuid() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const joinTodayAction = async (comandaId: string) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => joinTodayAppointments(uuid(comandaId), actorOf(u)));
export const addItemAction = async (comandaId: string, input: z.input<typeof itemInput>) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => addItem(uuid(comandaId), itemInput.parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const updateItemAction = async (itemId: string, patch: z.input<typeof itemPatch>) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => updateItem(uuid(itemId), itemPatch.parse(patch) as Parameters<typeof updateItem>[1], actorOf(u)));
export const removeItemAction = async (itemId: string) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => removeItem(uuid(itemId), actorOf(u)));
export const setDiscountAction = async (comandaId: string, input: { cents?: number | null; percent?: number | null }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => setComandaDiscount(uuid(comandaId), z.object({ cents: cents.nullish(), percent: z.number().min(0).max(100).nullish() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const setClientAction = async (comandaId: string, input: { contactId?: string | null; clientName?: string | null }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => setComandaClient(uuid(comandaId), z.object({ contactId: z.string().uuid().nullish(), clientName: z.string().max(120).nullish() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const addPaymentAction = async (comandaId: string, input: { method: string; valueCents: number; installments?: number | null }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => addPayment(uuid(comandaId), z.object({ method: z.enum(PAY_METHODS), valueCents: cents, installments: z.number().int().nullish() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const removePaymentAction = async (paymentId: string) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => removePayment(uuid(paymentId), actorOf(u)));
export const closeComandaAction = async (comandaId: string, input: { saveChangeAsCredit?: boolean }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => closeComanda(uuid(comandaId), { saveChangeAsCredit: !!input.saveChangeAsCredit }, actorOf(u)), { fallback: "Não foi possível fechar a comanda. Tente de novo." });
export const discardComandaAction = async (comandaId: string) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => discardComanda(uuid(comandaId), actorOf(u)));
export const clientPackagesAction = async (contactId: string, day: string) => run("caixa.operar", () => clientPackagesFor(uuid(contactId), z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).parse(day)), { revalidate: false });

/* Caixa do dia (opcional, 7.9). */
export const openCashAction = async (input: { openingCents: number; notes?: string | null }) => run("caixa.operar", (u) => openCash(z.object({ openingCents: cents, notes: z.string().max(300).nullish() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const movementAction = async (input: { kind: "sangria" | "suprimento"; valueCents: number; reason: string }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => addMovement(z.object({ kind: z.enum(["sangria", "suprimento"]), valueCents: cents, reason: z.string().max(200) }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const closeCashAction = async (input: { counted: Record<string, number | null>; notes?: string | null }) =>
  run("caixa.operar", (u) => closeCash(z.object({ counted: z.object({ pix: cents.nullish(), credito: cents.nullish(), debito: cents.nullish(), dinheiro: cents.nullish() }), notes: z.string().max(300).nullish() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));

/* Gestão do caixa (caixa.gerir). */
export const cancelComandaAction = async (comandaId: string, input: { reason: string }) => run("caixa.gerir", (u) => cancelComanda(uuid(comandaId), { reason: z.string().max(300).parse(input.reason) }, actorOf(u)));
export const reopenForCorrectionAction = async (comandaId: string, input: { reason: string }) => run("caixa.gerir", (u) => reopenForCorrection(uuid(comandaId), { reason: z.string().max(300).parse(input.reason) }, actorOf(u)));
export const reopenCashAction = async (id: string) => run("caixa.gerir", (u) => reopenCash(uuid(id), actorOf(u)));
export const extendPackageAction = async (id: string, input: { validUntil: string }) => run("caixa.gerir", (u) => extendPackage(uuid(id), { validUntil: z.string().max(10).parse(input.validUntil) }, actorOf(u)));

/* Configuração (config.editar): modo, pacotes à venda, valor por sessão e regras de comissão. Valem mesmo com o caixa no Avec. */
export const saveCaixaSettingsAction = async (input: { mode: "avec" | "crm"; exigeCaixaAberto: boolean }) =>
  run("config.editar", (u) => saveCaixaSettings(z.object({ mode: z.enum(["avec", "crm"]), exigeCaixaAberto: z.boolean() }).parse(input), u.id));
const defItem = z.object({ serviceId: z.string().uuid(), sessions: z.number().int(), unitCents: cents.nullable() });
export const savePackageDefAction = async (input: { id?: string | null; name: string; priceCents: number | null; validityDays: number | null; items: z.input<typeof defItem>[]; active?: boolean }) =>
  run("config.editar", (u) => savePackageDef(z.object({ id: z.string().uuid().nullish(), name: z.string().max(120), priceCents: cents.nullable(), validityDays: z.number().int().nullable(), items: z.array(defItem).max(20), active: z.boolean().optional() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const setPackageSessionValueAction = async (id: string, unitCents: number | null) => run("config.editar", (u) => setPackageSessionValue(uuid(id), cents.nullable().parse(unitCents), actorOf(u)));
export const setCommissionAction = async (input: { professionalId: string; serviceId: string; bp: number | null }) =>
  run("config.editar", (u) => setCommission(z.object({ professionalId: z.string().uuid(), serviceId: z.string().uuid(), bp: z.number().int().nullable() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
export const applyCommissionCategoryAction = async (input: { professionalId: string; category: string; bp: number | null }) =>
  run("config.editar", (u) => applyCommissionToCategory(z.object({ professionalId: z.string().uuid(), category: z.string().max(80), bp: z.number().int().nullable() }).parse(input), actorOf(u)));
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom
import { afterEach, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import type { ComandaDetail } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";

afterEach(cleanup);
const detail = (over: Partial<ComandaDetail> = {}): ComandaDetail => ({
  id: "c1", number: 1, day: "2026-10-15", status: "aberta", contactId: "p1", clientName: "Paula Souza", visitId: null, discountCents: 0, items: [], payments: [],
  totals: { subtotalCents: 12000, discountCents: 0, totalCents: 12000, missingPrice: [] }, paidCents: 0, remainingCents: 12000, creditBalance: 0,
  closedByName: null, closedAt: null, changeCents: null, cancelReason: null, cashOpen: false, exigeCaixaAberto: false, ...over,
});

it("receber: valor começa no que falta; parcelas só no crédito; crédito da cliente só com saldo", () => {
  const onAdd = vi.fn();
  render(<PaymentPanel detail={detail()} pending={false} saveChange={false} onSaveChange={() => {}} onAdd={onAdd} onRemove={() => {}} />);
  expect((screen.getByLabelText("Valor recebido (R$)") as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("120,00");
  expect(screen.queryByRole("option", { name: "Crédito da cliente" })).toBeNull();
  expect(screen.queryByLabelText("Parcelas")).toBeNull();
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Forma de pagamento"), { target: { value: "credito" } });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Parcelas"), { target: { value: "3" } });
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Valor recebido (R$)"), { target: { value: "50" } });
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Receber" }));
  expect(onAdd).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ method: "credito", valueCents: 5000, installments: 3 });
});

it("troco aparece e, com cliente, oferece guardar como crédito", () => {
  const onSaveChange = vi.fn();
  render(<PaymentPanel detail={detail({ payments: [{ id: "x", method: "dinheiro", valueCents: 15000, installments: null }], paidCents: 15000, remainingCents: 0, creditBalance: 2000 })}
    pending={false} saveChange={false} onSaveChange={onSaveChange} onAdd={() => {}} onRemove={() => {}} />);
  expect(screen.getByText(`Troco: ${money(3000)}`)).toBeTruthy();
  expect(screen.getByRole("option", { name: "Crédito da cliente" })).toBeTruthy();
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText("Guardar o troco como crédito da cliente"));
  expect(onSaveChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
});
```

```tsx
// @vitest-environment jsdom

const a = vi.hoisted(() => ({
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `src/lib/money-input.ts`**

```ts
/** O valor que a recepção digita ("120", "120,50", "R$ 1.234,56") em centavos. Negativo ou texto: null. */
export function parseMoneyInput(text: string): number | null {
  const t = text.replace(/R\$|\s/g, "").replace(/\./g, "").replace(",", ".");
  if (!/^\d+(\.\d{0,2})?$/.test(t)) return null;
  return Math.round(Number(t) * 100);
}
export const moneyInputValue = (cents: number | null): string => (cents == null ? "" : (cents / 100).toFixed(2).replace(".", ","));
```

- [ ] **Passo 4: `src/components/caixa/payment-panel.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input, Select } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { PAY_LABEL, PAY_METHODS, type PayMethod } from "@/lib/comanda-math";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { moneyInputValue, parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";
import type { ComandaDetail } from "@/server/queries/caixa";

/** Receber (7.1): várias formas na mesma comanda; o que falta, o troco e "Guardar o troco como crédito da cliente". */
export function PaymentPanel({ detail, pending, saveChange, onSaveChange, onAdd, onRemove }: {
  detail: ComandaDetail; pending: boolean; saveChange: boolean; onSaveChange: (v: boolean) => void;
  onAdd: (p: { method: PayMethod; valueCents: number; installments: number | null }) => void; onRemove: (paymentId: string) => void;
}) {
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<PayMethod>("pix");
  const [value, setValue] = useState(moneyInputValue(detail.remainingCents));
  const [installments, setInstallments] = useState("1");
  useEffect(() => { setValue(moneyInputValue(detail.remainingCents)); }, [detail.remainingCents]);
  const change = Math.max(0, detail.paidCents - detail.totals.totalCents);
  const methods = PAY_METHODS.filter((m) => m !== "credito_cliente" || (detail.creditBalance ?? 0) > 0);
  const open = detail.status === "aberta";
  return (
    <section aria-label="Pagamento" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {detail.payments.map((p) => (
          <li key={p.id} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-paper-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm">
            <span>{PAY_LABEL[p.method as PayMethod] ?? p.method}{p.installments && p.installments > 1 ? ` em ${p.installments}x` : ""}</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-3 tabular">{money(p.valueCents)}{open && <button type="button" className="text-xs text-alert hover:underline" onClick={() => onRemove(p.id)}>Tirar</button>}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <dl className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto] gap-y-1 text-sm">
        <dt>Total</dt><dd className="font-semibold tabular">{money(detail.totals.totalCents)}</dd>
        <dt>Recebido</dt><dd className="tabular">{money(detail.paidCents)}</dd>
        {detail.remainingCents > 0 && <><dt className="text-alert">Falta</dt><dd className="font-semibold text-alert tabular">{money(detail.remainingCents)}</dd></>}
      </dl>
      {change > 0 && (
        <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-ok-soft px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ok">
          <p className="font-semibold">{`Troco: ${money(change)}`}</p>
          {detail.contactId && open && (
            <label className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2"><input type="checkbox" checked={saveChange} onChange={(e) => onSaveChange(e.target.checked)} />Guardar o troco como crédito da cliente</label>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {open && (
        <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto] sm:items-end">
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Forma de pagamento
            <Select value={method} onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value as PayMethod)} className="h-11">
              {methods.map((m) => <option key={m} value={m}>{PAY_LABEL[m]}</option>)}
            </Select>
          </label>
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Valor recebido (R$)
            <Input inputMode="decimal" value={value} onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)} className="h-11" />
          </label>
          {method === "credito" && (
            <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Parcelas
              <Select value={installments} onChange={(e) => setInstallments(e.target.value)} className="h-11">
                {Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, k) => <option key={k} value={String(k + 1)}>{k + 1}x</option>)}
              </Select>
            </label>
          )}
          {method === "credito_cliente" && <p className="text-xs text-muted sm:col-span-3">{`Saldo de crédito: ${money(detail.creditBalance ?? 0)}`}</p>}
          <Button variant="secondary" disabled={pending} onClick={() => {
            const cents = parseMoneyInput(value);
            if (!cents) return;
            onAdd({ method, valueCents: cents, installments: method === "credito" ? Number(installments) : null });
          }}>Receber</Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 5: `src/components/caixa/comanda-sheet.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { ActionSheet, FieldError, SheetLabel } from "@/components/queue/action-sheet";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input, Select } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Segmented } from "@/components/ui/segmented";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import {
  addItemAction, addPaymentAction, cancelComandaAction, clientPackagesAction, closeComandaAction, comandaDetailAction, discardComandaAction, joinTodayAction,
  openCashAction, removeItemAction, removePaymentAction, reopenForCorrectionAction, setDiscountAction, updateItemAction,
} from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import type { ComandaDetail } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { moneyInputValue, parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";
import { PaymentPanel } from "./payment-panel";

export type ComandaCatalog = {
  services: { id: string; label: string }[]; professionals: { id: string; name: string; active: boolean }[];
  packageDefs: { id: string; name: string; priceCents: number | null }[]; canGerir: boolean;
};
type Kind = "servico" | "produto" | "pacote_venda" | "pacote_sessao";
const KIND_LABEL: Record<Kind, string> = { servico: "Serviço", produto: "Produto", pacote_venda: "Pacote", pacote_sessao: "Sessão de pacote" };

/**
 * A comanda (7.1): itens com profissional e preço (sem preço não fecha), somar serviço, produto, venda e sessão de pacote, desconto,
 * receber em várias formas e Finalizar. Fechada: só a gestão cancela ou reabre para corrigir. Recarrega do servidor a cada ação.
 */
export function ComandaSheet({ comandaId, open, onClose, catalog }: { comandaId: string | null; open: boolean; onClose: () => void; catalog: ComandaCatalog }) {
  const [d, setD] = useState<ComandaDetail | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [needsCash, setNeedsCash] = useState(false);
  const [opening, setOpening] = useState("");
  const [saveChange, setSaveChange] = useState(false);
  const [cancelReason, setCancelReason] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!comandaId) return;
    const r = await comandaDetailAction(comandaId);
    if (r.ok) setD(r.data ?? null); else setError(r.error);
  }, [comandaId]);
  useEffect(() => { if (open) { setError(null); setNeedsCash(false); void load(); } }, [open, load]);

  /** Roda uma ação e recarrega; erro aparece no alto da folha. */
  const act = (fn: () => Promise<{ ok: boolean; error?: string }>, done?: string) => start(async () => {
    setError(null);
    const r = await fn();
    if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error ?? "Não foi possível registrar. Tente de novo."); return; }
    if (done) toast(done);
    await load();
  });
  const finish = () => start(async () => {
    if (!d) return;
    setError(null);
    const r = await closeComandaAction(d.id, { saveChangeAsCredit: saveChange });
    if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); setNeedsCash(!!r.needsCash); return; }
    toast(`Comanda ${d.number} fechada`);
    setNeedsCash(false);
    await load();
  });
  const openCashAndFinish = () => start(async () => {
    const cents = parseMoneyInput(opening || "0");
    if (cents == null) { setError("Digite o troco inicial (pode ser zero)."); return; }
    const r = await openCashAction({ openingCents: cents });
    if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
    setNeedsCash(false);
    finish();
  });

  const isOpen = d?.status === "aberta";
  const title = d ? `Comanda ${d.number} · ${d.clientName}` : "Comanda";
  const footer = d && (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
      {isOpen && <Button variant="ghost" disabled={pending} onClick={() => act(() => discardComandaAction(d.id), "Comanda descartada")}>Descartar</Button>}
      {isOpen && <Button disabled={pending || d.totals.missingPrice.length > 0 || d.remainingCents > 0} onClick={finish}>Finalizar</Button>}
      {d.status === "fechada" && catalog.canGerir && (
        <>
          <Button variant="secondary" disabled={pending} onClick={() => setCancelReason("")}>Cancelar comanda</Button>
          <Button variant="secondary" disabled={pending} onClick={() => { const reason = window.prompt("Motivo da correção"); if (reason) act(() => reopenForCorrectionAction(d.id, { reason }), "Comanda reaberta para corrigir"); }}>Reabrir para corrigir</Button>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <ActionSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title} footer={footer}>
      {!d ? <p className="text-sm text-muted">Carregando…</p> : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
          {error && <p role="alert" className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-alert-soft px-3 py-2 text-sm text-alert">{error}</p>}
          {needsCash && (
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-gold/40 bg-gold-soft/40 p-3">
              <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Troco inicial (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={opening} onChange={(e) => setOpening(e.target.value)} className="h-11 w-36" /></label>
              <Button variant="gold" disabled={pending} onClick={openCashAndFinish}>Abrir o caixa de hoje</Button>
            </div>
          )}
          {d.status !== "aberta" && (
            <p className="text-sm text-graphite">{d.status === "fechada" ? `Fechada${d.closedByName ? ` por ${d.closedByName}` : ""}.` : `Cancelada: ${d.cancelReason ?? ""}`}</p>
          )}
          <section aria-label="Itens" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            {d.items.map((i) => (
              <div key={i.id} className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line px-3 py-2">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                  <span className="min-w-0 truncate font-medium">{i.label}{i.qty !== 1 ? ` × ${i.qty}` : ""}</span>
                  <span className="tabular">{i.totalCents == null ? "—" : money(i.totalCents)}</span>
                </div>
                {isOpen && (
                  <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2">
                    {(i.kind === "servico" || i.kind === "pacote_sessao") && (
                      <Select aria-label={`Profissional de ${i.label}`} value={i.professionalId ?? ""} className="h-10" onChange={(e) => act(() => updateItemAction(i.id, { professionalId: e.target.value || null }))}>
                        <option value="">Escolher profissional</option>
                        {catalog.professionals.filter((p) => p.active || p.id === i.professionalId).map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>)}
                      </Select>
                    )}
                    {i.kind !== "pacote_sessao" && <PriceField label={i.label} cents={i.unitCents} onCommit={(unitCents) => act(() => updateItemAction(i.id, { unitCents }))} />}
                    <button type="button" className="ml-auto text-sm text-alert hover:underline" onClick={() => act(() => removeItemAction(i.id))}>Tirar</button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
            {d.totals.missingPrice.map((l) => <p key={l} className="text-sm text-alert">{`Falta o preço de ${l}.`}</p>)}
            {isOpen && d.contactId && <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => act(() => joinTodayAction(d.id), "Horário juntado")}>Juntar horário de hoje</button>}
          </section>
          {isOpen && <AddItem comanda={d} catalog={catalog} pending={pending} onAdd={(input) => act(() => addItemAction(d.id, input))} />}
          {isOpen && <DiscountField comanda={d} pending={pending} onApply={(cents) => act(() => setDiscountAction(d.id, { cents }))} />}
          <PaymentPanel detail={d} pending={pending} saveChange={saveChange} onSaveChange={setSaveChange}
            onAdd={(p) => act(() => addPaymentAction(d.id, p))} onRemove={(id) => act(() => removePaymentAction(id))} />
          {cancelReason !== null && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-alert/40 p-3">
              <SheetLabel><label htmlFor="cancelar-comanda">Motivo do cancelamento</label></SheetLabel>
              <Input id="cancelar-comanda" value={cancelReason} onChange={(e) => setCancelReason(e.target.value)} maxLength={300} />
              <FieldError>{cancelReason !== null && !cancelReason.trim() ? "Escreva o motivo." : null}</FieldError>
              <Button variant="danger" disabled={pending || !cancelReason.trim()} onClick={() => act(() => cancelComandaAction(d.id, { reason: cancelReason }), "Comanda cancelada")}>Cancelar a comanda (desfaz pacote, crédito e comissão)</Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </ActionSheet>
  );
}

function PriceField({ label, cents, onCommit }: { label: string; cents: number | null; onCommit: (cents: number) => void }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState(moneyInputValue(cents));
  useEffect(() => { setV(moneyInputValue(cents)); }, [cents]);
  return (
    <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-graphite">{`Preço de ${label} (R$)`}
      <Input inputMode="decimal" value={v} className="h-10 w-28" onChange={(e) => setV(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={() => { const c = parseMoneyInput(v); if (c != null && c !== cents) onCommit(c); }} />
    </label>
  );
}

function DiscountField({ comanda, pending, onApply }: { comanda: ComandaDetail; pending: boolean; onApply: (cents: number) => void }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState(moneyInputValue(comanda.discountCents || null));
  return (
    <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
      <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Desconto na comanda (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={v} onChange={(e) => setV(e.target.value)} className="h-11 w-36" /></label>
      <Button variant="secondary" disabled={pending} onClick={() => { const c = parseMoneyInput(v || "0"); if (c != null) onApply(c); }}>Aplicar desconto</Button>
    </div>
  );
}

function AddItem({ comanda, catalog, pending, onAdd }: { comanda: ComandaDetail; catalog: ComandaCatalog; pending: boolean; onAdd: (i: Record<string, unknown> & { kind: Kind }) => void }) {
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<Kind>("servico");
  const [serviceId, setServiceId] = useState("");
  const [pro, setPro] = useState("");
  const [label, setLabel] = useState("");
  const [price, setPrice] = useState("");
  const [defId, setDefId] = useState("");
  const [pkgId, setPkgId] = useState("");
  const [pkgs, setPkgs] = useState<{ id: string; label: string; remaining: number; expired: boolean }[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (kind !== "pacote_sessao" || !comanda.contactId) return;
    void clientPackagesAction(comanda.contactId, comanda.day).then((r) => setPkgs(r.ok ? r.data ?? [] : []));
  }, [kind, comanda.contactId, comanda.day]);
  const kinds = (Object.keys(KIND_LABEL) as Kind[]).filter((k) => comanda.contactId || (k !== "pacote_venda" && k !== "pacote_sessao"));
  const submit = () => {
    const unitCents = price ? parseMoneyInput(price) : undefined;
    if (kind === "servico") onAdd({ kind, serviceId, professionalId: pro || null, qty: 1, ...(unitCents != null ? { unitCents } : {}) });
    else if (kind === "produto") onAdd({ kind, label, unitCents: unitCents ?? null, qty: 1, ...(pro ? { professionalId: pro } : {}) });
    else if (kind === "pacote_venda") onAdd({ kind, packageDefId: defId, qty: 1 });
    else onAdd({ kind, clientPackageId: pkgId, professionalId: pro || null, qty: 1 });
  };
  return (
    <section aria-label="Somar item" className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line p-3">
      <Segmented label="Tipo do item" value={kind} onChange={(v) => setKind(v)} options={kinds.map((k) => ({ value: k, label: KIND_LABEL[k] }))} />
      {kind === "servico" && (
        <Select aria-label="Serviço" value={serviceId} onChange={(e) => setServiceId(e.target.value)} className="h-11">
          <option value="">Escolher serviço</option>
          {catalog.services.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}
        </Select>
      )}
      {kind === "produto" && <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Produto<Input value={label} maxLength={120} onChange={(e) => setLabel(e.target.value)} className="h-11" /></label>}
      {kind === "pacote_venda" && (
        <Select aria-label="Pacote" value={defId} onChange={(e) => setDefId(e.target.value)} className="h-11">
          <option value="">Escolher pacote</option>
          {catalog.packageDefs.map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}{p.priceCents != null ? ` · ${money(p.priceCents)}` : ""}</option>)}
        </Select>
      )}
      {kind === "pacote_sessao" && (
        <Select aria-label="Pacote da cliente" value={pkgId} onChange={(e) => setPkgId(e.target.value)} className="h-11">
          <option value="">{pkgs.length ? "Escolher pacote" : "A cliente não tem pacote com sessão"}</option>
          {pkgs.map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id} disabled={p.expired}>{p.label} · faltam {p.remaining}{p.expired ? " (vencido)" : ""}</option>)}
        </Select>
      )}
      {(kind === "servico" || kind === "pacote_sessao" || kind === "produto") && (
        <Select aria-label="Profissional" value={pro} onChange={(e) => setPro(e.target.value)} className="h-11">
          <option value="">{kind === "produto" ? "Profissional (opcional)" : "Escolher profissional"}</option>
          {catalog.professionals.filter((p) => p.active).map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>)}
        </Select>
      )}
      {(kind === "servico" || kind === "produto") && <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Preço (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} placeholder={kind === "servico" ? "o do serviço" : ""} className="h-11 w-36" /></label>}
      <Button variant="secondary" disabled={pending} onClick={submit}>Somar à comanda</Button>
    </section>
  );
}
```

(O teste do produto espera `{ kind: "produto", label: "Shampoo", unitCents: 4590, qty: 1 }`: sem profissional escolhida, o objeto não leva `professionalId`.)

- [ ] **Passo 6: `src/components/caixa/comanda-provider.tsx`**

```tsx
"use client";
import { createContext, useContext, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { openComandaAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import { ComandaSheet, type ComandaCatalog } from "./comanda-sheet";

type Ctx = { openForAppointment: (appointmentId: string) => void; openById: (id: string) => void };
const C = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);
/** null fora do provider (caixa no Avec ou sem `caixa.operar`): quem usa esconde o botão. */
export const useComanda = () => useContext(C);

export function ComandaProvider({ catalog, children }: { catalog: ComandaCatalog; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [id, setId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const value: Ctx = {
    openById: setId,
    openForAppointment: (appointmentId) => void openComandaAction(appointmentId).then((r) => {
      if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
      setId(r.data!.comandaId);
      if (r.data!.created) toast(`Comanda ${r.data!.number} aberta`);
    }),
  };
  return (
    <C.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
      <ComandaSheet comandaId={id} open={!!id} onClose={() => { setId(null); router.refresh(); }} catalog={catalog} />
    </C.Provider>
  );
}
```

`cash-close-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ActionSheet } from "@/components/queue/action-sheet";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { closeCashAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import { cashDifference, DRAWER_METHODS, PAY_LABEL } from "@/lib/comanda-math";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";

type Drawer = (typeof DRAWER_METHODS)[number];

/** Fechar o caixa (7.4, regra 6): para cada forma, o do sistema ao lado do contado (dinheiro obrigatório) e a diferença. */
export function CashCloseSheet({ open, onClose, summary }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; summary: { expected: Record<Drawer, number>; openComandas: number } }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState<Record<Drawer, string>>({ pix: "", credito: "", debito: "", dinheiro: "" });
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const counted = Object.fromEntries(DRAWER_METHODS.map((m) => [m, text[m] ? parseMoneyInput(text[m]) : null])) as Record<Drawer, number | null>;
  const diff = cashDifference(summary.expected, counted);
  const n = summary.openComandas;
  const submit = () => start(async () => {
    if (counted.dinheiro == null) { setError("Digite o dinheiro contado."); return; }
    const r = await closeCashAction({ counted, notes: notes.trim() || null });
    if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
    toast(`Caixa fechado. Diferença: ${money(r.data!.differenceCents)}`, r.data!.differenceCents === 0 ? "ok" : "info");
    onClose();
    router.refresh();
  });
  return (
    <ActionSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title="Fechar caixa" footer={<Button loading={pending} onClick={submit}>Fechar caixa</Button>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {n > 0 && <p className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-gold-soft/60 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gold-ink">{`${n} ${n === 1 ? "comanda aberta fica" : "comandas abertas ficam"} para o próximo caixa.`}</p>}
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-alert">{error}</p>}
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead><tr className="text-left text-muted"><th className="py-1 font-medium">Forma</th><th className="font-medium">Sistema</th><th className="font-medium">Contado</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {DRAWER_METHODS.map((m) => (
              <tr key={m} className="border-t border-line/70">
                <td className="py-2">{PAY_LABEL[m]}</td>
                <td className="tabular">{money(summary.expected[m])}</td>
                <td><Input aria-label={`${PAY_LABEL[m]} contado (R$)`} inputMode="decimal" value={text[m]} onChange={(e) => { setError(null); setText((t) => ({ ...t, [m]: e.target.value })); }} className="h-10 w-28" /></td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {counted.dinheiro != null && <p className={diff.totalCents === 0 ? "text-sm text-ok" : "text-sm font-semibold text-alert"}>{`Diferença: ${money(diff.totalCents)}`}</p>}
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Observação (opcional)<Input value={notes} maxLength={300} onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)} /></label>
      </div>
    </ActionSheet>
  );
}
```

`movement-sheet.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { ActionSheet } from "@/components/queue/action-sheet";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { movementAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import { parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";

/** Sangria (tira dinheiro do caixa) ou Suprimento (põe), sempre com motivo. */
export function MovementSheet({ open, onClose, kind }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; kind: "sangria" | "suprimento" }) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const title = kind === "sangria" ? "Sangria" : "Suprimento";
  const submit = () => start(async () => {
    const cents = parseMoneyInput(value);
    if (!cents) { setError("Digite um valor maior que zero."); return; }
    const r = await movementAction({ kind, valueCents: cents, reason });
    if (!r.ok) { setError(r.error); return; }
    toast(`${title} registrada`); onClose(); router.refresh();
  });
  return (
    <ActionSheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title} footer={<Button loading={pending} onClick={submit}>Registrar</Button>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-alert">{error}</p>}
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Valor (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={value} onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Motivo<Input value={reason} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)} placeholder={kind === "sangria" ? "ex.: depósito no banco" : "ex.: troco"} /></label>
      </div>
    </ActionSheet>
  );
}
```

`src/components/caixa/caixa-today.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { openCashAction, openWalkInAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import type { CaixaHoje, ComandaRow } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { PAY_LABEL, PAY_METHODS } from "@/lib/comanda-math";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";
import { useComanda } from "./comanda-provider";
import { CashCloseSheet } from "./cash-close-sheet";
import { MovementSheet } from "./movement-sheet";

const HORA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });

/** Caixa de hoje (7.1, 7.9): total do dia por forma, comandas abertas e fechadas, "Nova comanda" e o caixa aberto (opcional). */
export function CaixaToday({ data }: { data: CaixaHoje }) {
  const comanda = useComanda();
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState<null | "fechar" | "sangria" | "suprimento">(null);
  const [opening, setOpening] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const newComanda = () => start(async () => {
    const r = await openWalkInAction({ requestKey: crypto.randomUUID() });
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    comanda?.openById(r.data!.comandaId);
  });
  const openCash = () => start(async () => {
    const cents = parseMoneyInput(opening || "0");
    if (cents == null) { toast("Digite o troco inicial (pode ser zero).", "error"); return; }
    const r = await openCashAction({ openingCents: cents });
    if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
    toast("Caixa aberto"); router.refresh();
  });
  const list = (title: string, rows: ComandaRow[], empty: string) => (
    <section aria-label={title} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper">
      <h2 className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold">{title} · {rows.length}</h2>
      {rows.length ? (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-line/70">
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.id}>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => comanda?.openById(r.id)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-paper-2/70">
                <span className="w-10 shrink-0 text-sm font-semibold tabular">{r.number}</span>
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{r.clientName}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted">{HORA.format(new Date(r.closedAt ?? r.openedAt))}</span>
                <span className="w-24 text-right font-medium tabular">{money(r.totalCents)}</span>
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">{empty}</p>}
    </section>
  );
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Button loading={pending} onClick={newComanda}>Nova comanda</Button>
        {data.open ? (
          <>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSheet("sangria")}>Sangria</Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSheet("suprimento")}>Suprimento</Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSheet("fechar")}>Fechar caixa</Button>
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
            <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-graphite">Troco inicial (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={opening} onChange={(e) => setOpening(e.target.value)} className="h-10 w-28" /></label>
            <Button variant="secondary" loading={pending} onClick={openCash}>Abrir caixa</Button>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted">{data.rules.exigeCaixaAberto ? "Para fechar comanda, abra o caixa." : "Opcional: só para conferir o dinheiro no fim do dia."}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <section aria-label="Total do dia" className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6">
        <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-3"><p className="text-xs text-muted">Total do dia</p><p className="text-lg font-semibold tabular">{money(data.totals.totalCents)}</p></div>
        {PAY_METHODS.map((m) => (
          <div key={m} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-3"><p className="text-xs text-muted">{PAY_LABEL[m]}</p><p className="font-medium tabular">{money(data.totals.byMethod[m])}</p></div>
        ))}
      </section>
      {list("Comandas abertas", data.comandas.abertas, "Nenhuma comanda aberta.")}
      {list("Comandas fechadas", data.comandas.fechadas, "Nenhuma comanda fechada hoje.")}
      {data.open && <CashCloseSheet open={sheet === "fechar"} onClose={() => setSheet(null)} summary={{ expected: data.open.expected, openComandas: data.comandas.abertas.length }} />}
      {(sheet === "sangria" || sheet === "suprimento") && <MovementSheet open onClose={() => setSheet(null)} kind={sheet} />}
      {!data.comandas.abertas.length && !data.comandas.fechadas.length && <Empty title="Ainda sem vendas hoje">Abra a comanda pelo horário na Agenda ou use "Nova comanda" para venda sem horário.</Empty>}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/(app)/caixa/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requirePerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { CAIXA_OFF } from "@/lib/caixa-settings";
import { getCaixaHoje } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { listBookableServices } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { listProfessionals } from "@/server/domain/professionals";
import { listPackageDefs } from "@/server/domain/caixa/packages";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/page-header";
import { LiveRefresh } from "@/components/shell/live";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { ComandaProvider } from "@/components/caixa/comanda-provider";
import { CaixaToday } from "@/components/caixa/caixa-today";

export const metadata = { title: "Caixa" };
const DIA = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long", timeZone: "UTC" });

/** Caixa (7.1, 7.9): só vendas. Hoje: total por forma, comandas, Nova comanda e o caixa aberto (opcional). */
export default async function CaixaPage() {
  const user = await requirePerm("caixa.operar");
  const data = await getCaixaHoje();
  const tabs = (
    <nav aria-label="Caixa" className="flex gap-2 text-sm">
      <Link href="/caixa/fechamentos" className="font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline">Fechamentos</Link>
      {can(user.perms, "comissao.ver") && <Link href="/caixa/comissao" className="font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline">Comissão</Link>}
      {(can(user.perms, "config.editar") || can(user.perms, "caixa.gerir")) && <Link href="/caixa/pacotes" className="font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline">Pacotes</Link>}
    </nav>
  );
  if (data.mode !== "crm") return (
    <main className="pb-12">
      <PageHeader title="Caixa" actions={tabs} />
      <div className="px-5 lg:px-8"><Empty title={CAIXA_OFF}>{can(user.perms, "config.editar") ? "A gestão liga em Configurações › Caixa. Pacotes à venda e comissão já podem ser preparados." : "Por enquanto, receba no Avec."}</Empty></div>
    </main>
  );
  const [services, pros, defs] = await Promise.all([listBookableServices(), listProfessionals(), listPackageDefs({ activeOnly: true })]);
  const catalog = {
    services: services.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, label: s.label })), professionals: pros.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name, active: p.active })),
    packageDefs: defs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, name: d.name, priceCents: d.priceCents })), canGerir: can(user.perms, "caixa.gerir"),
  };
  const day = new Date(`${data.day}T12:00:00Z`);
  return (
    <main className="pb-12">
      <LiveRefresh scopes={["caixa"]} />
      <PageHeader title="Caixa" subtitle={`${DIA.format(day).replace(/^./, (c) => c.toUpperCase())} · ${data.open ? "caixa aberto" : "caixa fechado"}`} actions={tabs} />
      <div className="px-5 lg:px-8">
        <ComandaProvider catalog={catalog}><CaixaToday data={data} /></ComandaProvider>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { requirePerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { listCashSessions } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/page-header";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { ReopenCashButton } from "@/components/caixa/reopen-cash-button";

export const metadata = { title: "Fechamentos" };
const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "UTC" });

/** Histórico de caixas: quem abriu e fechou, o esperado, o contado e a diferença. Reabrir é da gestão (caixa.gerir). */
export default async function FechamentosPage() {
  const user = await requirePerm("caixa.operar");
  const rows = await listCashSessions(60);
  const canReopen = can(user.perms, "caixa.gerir");
  return (
    <main className="pb-12">
      <PageHeader title="Fechamentos" subtitle="Os últimos caixas" />
      <div className="px-5 lg:px-8">
        {rows.length ? (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper">
            <table className="w-full min-w-[640px] text-sm">
              <thead><tr className="text-left text-muted"><th className="px-4 py-2 font-medium">Dia</th><th className="font-medium">Abriu</th><th className="font-medium">Fechou</th><th className="font-medium">Diferença</th><th /></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {rows.map((r) => (
                  <tr key={r.id} className="border-t border-line/70">
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 tabular">{DM.format(new Date(`${r.day}T12:00:00Z`))}</td>
                    <td>{r.openedByName ?? "—"}</td>
                    <td>{r.status === "aberto" ? "Aberto" : r.closedByName ?? "—"}</td>
                    <td className={r.differenceCents ? "font-semibold text-alert tabular" : "tabular"}>{r.differenceCents == null ? "—" : money(r.differenceCents)}</td>
                    <td className="pr-4 text-right">{canReopen && r.status === "fechado" && <ReopenCashButton id={r.id} />}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : <Empty title="Nenhum caixa ainda">O caixa é opcional: abra em Caixa › Abrir caixa quando quiser conferir o dinheiro.</Empty>}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { reopenCashAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";

export function ReopenCashButton({ id }: { id: string }) {
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  return <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" loading={pending} onClick={() => start(async () => {
    const r = await reopenCashAction(id);
    toast(r.ok ? "Caixa reaberto" : r.error, r.ok ? "ok" : "error");
    router.refresh();
  })}>Reabrir</Button>;
}
```

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { saveCaixaSettingsAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import type { CaixaMode } from "@/lib/caixa-settings";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

const MODES: { value: CaixaMode; title: string; text: string }[] = [
  { value: "avec", title: "No Avec (como hoje)", text: "A comanda e o caixa do CRM ficam desligados. Pacotes à venda e comissão já podem ser preparados." },
  { value: "crm", title: "No CRM", text: "A recepção abre a comanda pelo horário, recebe e finaliza no CRM. Não lance a mesma venda no Avec, senão os relatórios dobram." },
];

/** Configurações › Caixa (3.1): onde a Casa recebe e se fechar comanda exige o caixa aberto (7.9: por padrão, não exige). */
export function CaixaSettingsForm({ mode, exigeCaixaAberto }: { mode: CaixaMode; exigeCaixaAberto: boolean }) {
  const [m, setM] = useState(mode);
  const [exige, setExige] = useState(exigeCaixaAberto);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  return (
    <section className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-5">
      <h2 className="font-display text-[22px] font-medium">Onde a Casa recebe</h2>
      <fieldset className="mt-4 grid gap-2.5">
        <legend className="sr-only">Modo do caixa</legend>
        {MODES.map((o) => (
          <label key={o.value} className={cn("flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border px-4 py-3", m === o.value ? "border-ink bg-paper-2/70" : "border-line-strong hover:bg-paper-2/50")}>
            <input type="radio" name="caixa-modo" value={o.value} checked={m === o.value} onChange={() => setM(o.value)} aria-label={`${o.title}. ${o.text}`} className="mt-1" />
            <span><span className="font-medium">{o.title}</span><span className="block text-sm text-graphite">{o.text}</span></span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </fieldset>
      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line px-4 py-3">
        <span className="text-sm">Exigir caixa aberto para fechar comanda<span className="block text-xs text-muted">Desligado: o caixa do dia é só para conferir o dinheiro, quando a Casa quiser.</span></span>
        <Switch checked={exige} label="Exigir caixa aberto para fechar comanda" onChange={setExige} />
      </div>
      <p className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm">
        <Link href="/caixa/pacotes" className="font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline">Pacotes à venda</Link>
        <Link href="/caixa/comissao?aba=regras" className="font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline">Regras de comissão</Link>
      </p>
      <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end">
        <Button loading={pending} disabled={m === mode && exige === exigeCaixaAberto} onClick={() => start(async () => {
          const r = await saveCaixaSettingsAction({ mode: m, exigeCaixaAberto: exige });
          toast(r.ok ? "Caixa salvo" : r.error, r.ok ? "ok" : "error");
        })}>Salvar</Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  const nav = screen.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Filtrar quem já pagou" });
  expect(within(nav).getByRole("link", { name: "Sem horário marcado" }).getAttribute("aria-current")).toBe("page");
  expect(screen.queryByText(new RegExp(money(40000).replace("$", "\\$")))).toBeNull();
```

```tsx
"use client";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { defaultBookingTime } from "@/lib/agenda-now";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";
import type { PaidFilter, PaidRow } from "@/server/domain/caixa/paid";
import { canBookIn, useAgendaSheets } from "./agenda-sheets";

export type PaidChip = "sem_horario" | "vence_30" | "pacote" | "credito";
export const PAID_CHIPS: { value: PaidChip; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "sem_horario", label: "Sem horário marcado" }, { value: "vence_30", label: "Vence em 30 dias" }, { value: "pacote", label: "Pacote" }, { value: "credito", label: "Crédito" },
];
export function paidFilterOf(chip: PaidChip | null, withValues: boolean): PaidFilter {
  if (chip === "sem_horario") return { semHorario: true, withValues };
  if (chip === "vence_30") return { venceEm30: true, withValues };
  if (chip === "pacote" || chip === "credito") return { tipo: chip, withValues };
  return { withValues };
}

const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "UTC" });
const dm = (day: string) => DM.format(new Date(`${day}T12:00:00Z`));
const NEXT = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "short", day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", timeZone: "America/Bahia" });

/**
 * Agenda › "Já pagaram" (7.9): quem pagou e ainda não usou. O que mais importa é "Sem horário marcado" (pagou e não voltou): o botão
 * Agendar abre o Marcar já com a cliente. Valores só para quem tem `valores.ver` (a lista chega sem eles do servidor).
 */
export function PaidList({ rows, chip, chipHref, withValues, today, nowMs }: {
  rows: PaidRow[]; chip: PaidChip | null; chipHref: Record<"todos" | PaidChip, string>; withValues: boolean; today: string; nowMs: number;
}) {
  const { catalog, book } = useAgendaSheets();
  const canBook = canBookIn(catalog);
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <nav aria-label="Filtrar quem já pagou" className="-mx-5 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-5 pb-1 scroll-thin lg:mx-0 lg:px-0">
        {[{ value: null, label: "Todos" }, ...PAID_CHIPS].map((c) => (
          <Link key={c.label} href={chipHref[c.value ?? "todos"]} aria-current={chip === c.value ? "page" : undefined}
            className={cn("inline-flex h-11 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full border px-3.5 text-sm font-medium sm:h-9", chip === c.value ? "border-ink bg-ink text-paper" : "border-line-strong text-graphite hover:bg-paper-2")}>{c.label}</Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper"><Empty title="Ninguém por aqui">Quem pagou e ainda não usou (pacote, crédito ou serviço pago antes) aparece nesta lista.</Empty></div>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-line/70 rounded-[var(--radius-panel)] border border-line bg-paper">
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <li key={r.contactId} aria-label={r.name} className="flex flex-col gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:gap-5 sm:px-5">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/contatos/${r.contactId}`} className="font-medium hover:text-gold-ink">{r.name}</Link>
                <ul className="mt-1 flex flex-col gap-1">
                  {r.entries.map((e, k) => (
                    <li key={k} className="text-sm">
                      <span className={e.kind === "credito_avec" ? "text-gold-ink" : undefined}>{e.label}</span>
                      {(e.valueCents != null || e.paidOn) && (
                        <span className="block text-xs text-muted">{[withValues && e.valueCents != null ? money(e.valueCents) : null, e.paidOn ? `pago em ${dm(e.paidOn)}` : null].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}</span>
                      )}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3 sm:flex-col sm:items-end">
                {r.nextAppointmentAt
                  ? <span className="text-sm text-graphite">{`Próximo horário: ${NEXT.format(new Date(r.nextAppointmentAt)).replace(".", "")}`}</span>
                  : <span className="rounded-full bg-alert-soft px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-alert">Sem horário marcado</span>}
                {!r.nextAppointmentAt && canBook && (
                  <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => book({ day: today, time: defaultBookingTime(today, nowMs), client: { contactId: r.contactId, name: r.name } })}>Agendar</Button>
                )}
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Passo 3: `listPackagesNeedingAttention` (fim de `src/server/queries/caixa.ts`)**

```ts
export type AttentionPackage = { id: string; clientName: string; packageName: string; serviceLabel: string | null; remaining: number; validUntil: string | null; unitCents: number | null };

/** Caixa › Pacotes: o que pede a gestão (pacotes com sessão restante): vencidos (dar novo prazo) e sem valor por sessão (base da comissão). */
export async function listPackagesNeedingAttention(today: string): Promise<{ vencidos: AttentionPackage[]; semValor: AttentionPackage[] }> {
  const rows = await db.select().from(clientPackages).where(and(isNull(clientPackages.cancelledAt), sql`${clientPackages.totalSessions} > ${clientPackages.usedSessions}`))
    .orderBy(sql`${clientPackages.validUntil} asc nulls last`, asc(clientPackages.clientName));
  const map = (p: (typeof rows)[number]): AttentionPackage => ({ id: p.id, clientName: p.clientName ?? "Cliente", packageName: p.packageName, serviceLabel: p.serviceLabel, remaining: p.totalSessions - p.usedSessions, validUntil: p.validUntil, unitCents: p.unitCents });
  return { vencidos: rows.filter((p) => p.validUntil && p.validUntil < today).map(map), semValor: rows.filter((p) => p.unitCents == null && !(p.validUntil && p.validUntil < today)).map(map) };
}
```
