```tsx
import type { CommissionReport } from "@/server/domain/caixa/commission";
import { bpToPercent } from "@/lib/comanda-math";
import { money } from "@/lib/format";
import { Empty } from "@/components/ui/empty";

const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", timeZone: "UTC" });
const PEND = { sem_regra: "Sem regra", sem_valor_sessao: "Valor da sessão não definido", ok: "" } as const;

/** Relatório Comissão (7.1): por profissional, as linhas com base, % e valor; pendências à parte, sem somar. */
export function CommissionTable({ report }: { report: CommissionReport }) {
  if (!report.rows.length) return <Empty title="Nenhuma comissão no período">Só entram comandas fechadas no CRM.</Empty>;
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      {report.pending > 0 && <p className="rounded-[var(--radius-control)] bg-gold-soft/60 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-gold-ink">{`${report.pending} ${report.pending === 1 ? "linha sem regra ou sem valor da sessão" : "linhas sem regra ou sem valor da sessão"}: preencha em Regras ou em Pacotes.`}</p>}
      {report.rows.map((r) => (
        <section key={r.professionalId} aria-label={`Comissão de ${r.name}`} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper">
          <header className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2 border-b border-line px-4 py-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">{r.name}{!r.active && <span className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-muted">desativada</span>}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm tabular">Base {money(r.baseCents)} · <span className="font-semibold">Comissão {money(r.commissionCents)}</span></p>
          </header>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full min-w-[560px] text-sm">
              <thead><tr className="text-left text-muted"><th className="px-4 py-2 font-medium">Dia</th><th className="font-medium">Comanda</th><th className="font-medium">Cliente</th><th className="font-medium">Item</th><th className="font-medium">Base</th><th className="font-medium">%</th><th className="pr-4 font-medium">Valor</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {r.lines.map((l) => (
                  <tr key={l.itemId} className="border-t border-line/70">
                    <td className="px-4 py-2 tabular">{DM.format(new Date(`${l.day}T12:00:00Z`))}</td><td className="tabular">{l.comandaNumber}</td><td>{l.clientName}</td><td>{l.label}</td>
                    <td className="tabular">{l.baseCents == null ? "—" : money(l.baseCents)}</td><td className="tabular">{l.bp == null ? "—" : `${bpToPercent(l.bp)}%`}</td>
                    <td className="pr-4 tabular">{l.status === "ok" ? money(l.commissionCents) : <span className="text-gold-ink">{PEND[l.status]}</span>}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/components/caixa/commission-rules.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { applyCommissionCategoryAction, setCommissionAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import type { CommissionRuleRow } from "@/server/domain/caixa/commission";
import { bpToPercent, percentToBp } from "@/lib/comanda-math";

/** Caixa › Comissão › Regras (decisão 13): % por profissional nos serviços que ela faz, agrupados por categoria, com "Aplicar a …". */
export function CommissionRules({ rows }: { rows: CommissionRuleRow[] }) {
  return <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">{rows.map((r) => <ProRules key={r.professionalId} row={r} />)}</div>;
}

function ProRules({ row }: { row: CommissionRuleRow }) {
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const cats = [...new Set(row.services.map((s) => s.category?.trim() || "Outros"))];
  const save = (serviceId: string, text: string, before: number | null) => {
    const bp = text.trim() === "" ? null : percentToBp(text);
    if (text.trim() !== "" && bp == null) { toast("Use de 0% a 100%.", "error"); return; }
    if (bp === before) return;
    start(async () => { const r = await setCommissionAction({ professionalId: row.professionalId, serviceId, bp }); if (!r.ok) toast(r.error, "error"); else router.refresh(); });
  };
  return (
    <section aria-label={`Comissão de ${row.name}`} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-4">
      <h2 className="mb-3 font-semibold">{row.name}{!row.active && <span className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-muted">desativada</span>}</h2>
      {cats.map((cat) => <Category key={cat} cat={cat} row={row} pending={pending} onSave={save} onApply={(bp) => start(async () => {
        const r = await applyCommissionCategoryAction({ professionalId: row.professionalId, category: cat, bp });
        if (!r.ok) { toast(r.error, "error"); return; }
        toast(`${cat}: ${bp == null ? "sem regra" : `${bpToPercent(bp)}%`} em ${r.data!.updated} serviços`); router.refresh();
      })} />)}
    </section>
  );
}

function Category({ cat, row, pending, onSave, onApply }: { cat: string; row: CommissionRuleRow; pending: boolean; onSave: (serviceId: string, text: string, before: number | null) => void; onApply: (bp: number | null) => void }) {
  const [all, setAll] = useState("");
  const toast = useToast();
  const services = row.services.filter((s) => (s.category?.trim() || "Outros") === cat);
  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted">{cat}</h3>
        <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-graphite">{`% para todos de ${cat}`}<Input inputMode="decimal" value={all} onChange={(e) => setAll(e.target.value)} className="h-9 w-20" /></label>
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={pending} onClick={() => {
            const bp = all.trim() === "" ? null : percentToBp(all);
            if (all.trim() !== "" && bp == null) { toast("Use de 0% a 100%.", "error"); return; }
            onApply(bp);
          }}>{`Aplicar a ${cat}`}</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {services.map((s) => <Percent key={s.serviceId} label={s.label} bp={s.bp} onCommit={(t) => onSave(s.serviceId, t, s.bp)} />)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Percent({ label, bp, onCommit }: { label: string; bp: number | null; onCommit: (text: string) => void }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState(bpToPercent(bp));
  return (
    <label className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line px-3 py-2 text-sm">
      <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{label}</span>
      <Input aria-label={`${label} (%)`} inputMode="decimal" value={v} placeholder="sem regra" onChange={(e) => setV(e.target.value)} onBlur={() => onCommit(v)} className="h-9 w-20 text-right" />
    </label>
  );
}
```

`src/app/(app)/caixa/comissao/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requireAnyPerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { commissionReport, listCommissionRules } from "@/server/domain/caixa/commission";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/page-header";
import { CommissionTable } from "@/components/caixa/commission-table";
import { CommissionRules } from "@/components/caixa/commission-rules";
import { cn } from "@/lib/cn";

export const metadata = { title: "Comissão" };
const isDay = (v: unknown): v is string => typeof v === "string" && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v);

/** Comissão: o relatório por período (comissao.ver) e as regras por profissional e serviço (config.editar). */
export default async function ComissaoPage({ searchParams }: PageProps<"/caixa/comissao">) {
  const user = await requireAnyPerm("comissao.ver", "config.editar");
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const canReport = can(user.perms, "comissao.ver"), canRules = can(user.perms, "config.editar");
  const aba = (sp.aba === "regras" && canRules) || !canReport ? "regras" : "relatorio";
  const today = todayBahia(new Date());
  const from = isDay(sp.de) ? sp.de : `${today.slice(0, 8)}01`, to = isDay(sp.ate) ? sp.ate : today;
  const body = aba === "regras" ? <CommissionRules rows={await listCommissionRules()} /> : <CommissionTable report={await commissionReport({ from, to })} />;
  return (
    <main className="pb-12">
      <PageHeader title="Comissão" subtitle={aba === "relatorio" ? `De ${from.split("-").reverse().join("/")} a ${to.split("-").reverse().join("/")}` : "% por profissional e serviço"} />
      <div className="px-5 lg:px-8">
        <nav aria-label="Comissão" className="mb-5 inline-flex rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong p-1">
          {[["relatorio", "Relatório", canReport], ["regras", "Regras", canRules]].filter(([, , ok]) => ok).map(([v, label]) => (
            <Link key={v as string} href={`/caixa/comissao?aba=${v}`} aria-current={aba === v ? "page" : undefined}
              className={cn("inline-flex h-9 items-center rounded-[calc(var(--radius-control)-4px)] px-3.5 text-sm font-medium", aba === v ? "bg-ink text-paper" : "text-graphite hover:text-ink")}>{label as string}</Link>
          ))}
        </nav>
        {aba === "relatorio" && (
          <form className="mb-5 flex flex-wrap items-end gap-2 text-sm">
            <input type="hidden" name="aba" value="relatorio" />
            <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-graphite">De<input type="date" name="de" defaultValue={from} className="h-10 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong px-2" /></label>
            <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-graphite">Até<input type="date" name="ate" defaultValue={to} className="h-10 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong px-2" /></label>
            <button type="submit" className="h-10 rounded-[var(--radius-control)] border border-line-strong px-3 font-medium hover:bg-paper-2">Ver período</button>
          </form>
        )}
        {body}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

`src/components/caixa/package-def-editor.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input, Select } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { savePackageDefAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import { moneyInputValue, parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";

type Def = { id: string; name: string; priceCents: number | null; validityDays: number | null; active: boolean; items: { serviceId: string; sessions: number; unitCents: number | null }[] };
type Row = { serviceId: string; sessions: string; unit: string };

/** Um pacote à venda (7.1): nome, preço, validade e serviços × sessões, com valor por sessão opcional (base da comissão). */
export function PackageDefEditor({ def, services }: { def: Def | null; services: { id: string; label: string }[] }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(def?.name ?? "");
  const [price, setPrice] = useState(moneyInputValue(def?.priceCents ?? null));
  const [validity, setValidity] = useState(def?.validityDays ? String(def.validityDays) : "");
  const [active, setActive] = useState(def?.active ?? true);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Row[]>(def?.items.map((i) => ({ serviceId: i.serviceId, sessions: String(i.sessions), unit: moneyInputValue(i.unitCents) })) ?? [{ serviceId: "", sessions: "1", unit: "" }]);
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  const set = (k: number, patch: Partial<Row>) => setRows((l) => l.map((r, j) => (j === k ? { ...r, ...patch } : r)));
  const save = () => start(async () => {
    const r = await savePackageDefAction({
      id: def?.id ?? null, name, priceCents: price ? parseMoneyInput(price) : null, validityDays: validity ? Number(validity) : null, active,
      items: rows.filter((x) => x.serviceId).map((x) => ({ serviceId: x.serviceId, sessions: Number(x.sessions), unitCents: x.unit ? parseMoneyInput(x.unit) : null })),
    });
    toast(r.ok ? "Pacote salvo" : r.error, r.ok ? "ok" : "error");
    if (r.ok) router.refresh();
  });
  return (
    <section aria-label={def ? `Pacote ${def.name}` : "Novo pacote"} className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper p-4">
      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr]">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Nome do pacote<Input value={name} maxLength={120} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Preço (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={price} onChange={(e) => setPrice(e.target.value)} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm text-graphite">Validade (dias)<Input inputMode="numeric" value={validity} onChange={(e) => setValidity(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))} placeholder="sem validade" /></label>
      </div>
      {rows.map((r, k) => (
        <div key={k} className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-[2fr_1fr_1fr_auto] sm:items-end">
          <Select aria-label={`Serviço ${k + 1}`} value={r.serviceId} onChange={(e) => set(k, { serviceId: e.target.value })} className="h-11">
            <option value="">Escolher serviço</option>
            {services.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}
          </Select>
          <Input aria-label={`Sessões ${k + 1}`} inputMode="numeric" value={r.sessions} onChange={(e) => set(k, { sessions: e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "") })} className="h-11" />
          <Input aria-label={`Valor por sessão ${k + 1} (R$)`} inputMode="decimal" value={r.unit} placeholder="opcional" onChange={(e) => set(k, { unit: e.target.value })} className="h-11" />
          {rows.length > 1 && <button type="button" className="text-sm text-alert hover:underline" onClick={() => setRows((l) => l.filter((_, j) => j !== k))}>Tirar</button>}
        </div>
      ))}
      <button type="button" className="self-start text-sm font-medium text-gold-ink hover:underline" onClick={() => setRows((l) => [...l, { serviceId: "", sessions: "1", unit: "" }])}>+ Outro serviço no pacote</button>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><Switch checked={active} label="Pacote à venda" onChange={setActive} />{active ? "À venda" : "Fora de venda"}</span>
        <Button loading={pending} onClick={save}>Salvar pacote</Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`src/components/caixa/client-package-actions.tsx`:

```tsx
"use client";
import { useState, useTransition } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/field";
import { useToast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { extendPackageAction, setPackageSessionValueAction } from "@/app/(app)/caixa/actions";
import type { AttentionPackage } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { moneyInputValue, parseMoneyInput } from "@/lib/money-input";

const DM = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { day: "2-digit", month: "2-digit", year: "numeric", timeZone: "UTC" });

/** Pacotes das clientes que pedem a gestão: vencidos com sessão (dar novo prazo, `caixa.gerir`) e sem valor por sessão (`config.editar`). */
export function ClientPackageActions({ vencidos, semValor, canExtend, canValue }: { vencidos: AttentionPackage[]; semValor: AttentionPackage[]; canExtend: boolean; canValue: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
      {canExtend && <List title="Vencidos com sessão" empty="Nenhum pacote vencido com sessão." items={vencidos} render={(p) => <Extend p={p} />} />}
      {canValue && <List title="Sem valor por sessão" empty="Todos os pacotes têm valor por sessão." items={semValor} render={(p) => <Unit p={p} />} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function List({ title, empty, items, render }: { title: string; empty: string; items: AttentionPackage[]; render: (p: AttentionPackage) => React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section aria-label={title} className="rounded-[var(--radius-card)] border border-line bg-paper">
      <h2 className="border-b border-line px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold">{title} · {items.length}</h2>
      {items.length ? <ul className="divide-y divide-line/70">{items.map((p) => <li key={p.id} className="px-4 py-3">{render(p)}</li>)}</ul> : <p className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">{empty}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}

const Head = ({ p }: { p: AttentionPackage }) => (
  <p className="text-sm"><span className="font-medium">{p.clientName}</span> · {p.packageName}{p.serviceLabel ? ` (${p.serviceLabel})` : ""} · faltam {p.remaining}{p.validUntil ? ` · venceu em ${DM.format(new Date(`${p.validUntil}T12:00:00Z`))}` : ""}</p>
);

function Extend({ p }: { p: AttentionPackage }) {
  const [day, setDay] = useState("");
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2"><Head p={p} />
      <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-graphite">Novo prazo<Input type="date" value={day} onChange={(e) => setDay(e.target.value)} className="h-10" /></label>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" loading={pending} disabled={!day} onClick={() => start(async () => { const r = await extendPackageAction(p.id, { validUntil: day }); toast(r.ok ? "Novo prazo dado" : r.error, r.ok ? "ok" : "error"); router.refresh(); })}>Dar novo prazo</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Unit({ p }: { p: AttentionPackage }) {
  const [v, setV] = useState(moneyInputValue(p.unitCents));
  const [pending, start] = useTransition();
  const toast = useToast();
  const router = useRouter();
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2"><Head p={p} />
      <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
        <label className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-graphite">Valor por sessão (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={v} onChange={(e) => setV(e.target.value)} className="h-10 w-28" /></label>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" loading={pending} onClick={() => start(async () => { const c = parseMoneyInput(v); if (c == null) { toast("Digite o valor.", "error"); return; } const r = await setPackageSessionValueAction(p.id, c); toast(r.ok ? "Valor salvo" : r.error, r.ok ? "ok" : "error"); router.refresh(); })}>Salvar</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`src/app/(app)/caixa/pacotes/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { requireAnyPerm } from "@/server/auth/session";
import { can } from "@/lib/permissions";
import { todayBahia } from "@/lib/agenda";
import { listPackageDefs } from "@/server/domain/caixa/packages";
import { listBookableServices } from "@/server/queries/agenda";
import { listPackagesNeedingAttention } from "@/server/queries/caixa";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/page-header";
import { PackageDefEditor } from "@/components/caixa/package-def-editor";
import { ClientPackageActions } from "@/components/caixa/client-package-actions";

export const metadata = { title: "Pacotes" };

/** Pacotes à venda (config.editar) e os pacotes das clientes que pedem a gestão (novo prazo: caixa.gerir; valor por sessão: config.editar). */
export default async function PacotesPage() {
  const user = await requireAnyPerm("config.editar", "caixa.gerir");
  const canDefs = can(user.perms, "config.editar"), canExtend = can(user.perms, "caixa.gerir");
  const [defs, services, attention] = await Promise.all([canDefs ? listPackageDefs() : [], listBookableServices(), listPackagesNeedingAttention(todayBahia(new Date()))]);
  return (
    <main className="pb-12">
      <PageHeader title="Pacotes" subtitle="À venda e os das clientes" />
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-6 px-5 lg:px-8">
        {canDefs && (
          <section aria-label="Pacotes à venda" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <h2 className="font-display text-[22px] font-medium">Pacotes à venda</h2>
            {defs.map((d) => <PackageDefEditor key={d.id} def={d} services={services.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, label: s.label }))} />)}
            <PackageDefEditor def={null} services={services.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, label: s.label }))} />
          </section>
        )}
        <ClientPackageActions vencidos={attention.vencidos} semValor={attention.semValor} canExtend={canExtend} canValue={canDefs} />
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```

### Tarefa 6C.13: E2E do caixa e do "Já pagaram" (desktop e celular)

**Files:**
- Create: `e2e/caixa.spec.ts`
- Modify: `playwright.config.ts` (projeto `celular`: `caixa` no `testMatch`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: tudo das Tarefas 6C.1 a 6C.12 pela tela; `login`, `e2eSql` (`e2e/helpers.ts`); o reset do e2e (`caixa_modo = "crm"`, Maiara 9h–18h, Bia 24 h, Corte R$ 120,00).
- Produces: `e2e/caixa.spec.ts`.

- [ ] **Passo 1: `e2e/caixa.spec.ts`**

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { e2eSql, login } from "./helpers";

/**
 * Lote 6C, caixa só de vendas (7.9): abrir caixa → comanda pelo card → pagamento dividido → Finalizar → selo R$ → fechar caixa com
 * diferença → relatório de comissão (desktop); e a aba "Já pagaram" no desktop e no celular.
 */
const OFFSET_MS = 3 * 3_600_000;
const hoje = () => new Date(Date.now() - OFFSET_MS).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const agoraHM = () => new Date(Date.now() - OFFSET_MS).toISOString().slice(11, 16);
const em = (day: string, hm: string) => new Date(`${day}T${hm}:00-03:00`).toISOString();
const id = async (sql: string, p: unknown[] = []) => String((await e2eSql(sql, p))[0].id);

test.describe("Lote 6C: caixa", () => {
  test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
    for (const t of ["comanda_payments", "comanda_items", "client_credit_entries", "comandas", "cash_movements", "cash_sessions"]) await e2eSql(`delete from ${t}`);
    await e2eSql("delete from appointments where origin = 'crm'");
    await e2eSql("delete from visits");
    await e2eSql("delete from client_packages where origin = 'crm' or external_key like 'e2e:%'");
    await login(page);
  });

  test("desktop: abrir caixa, comanda pelo horário, pagamento dividido, Finalizar, selo R$, fechar com diferença e comissão", async ({ page, isMobile }) => {
    test.skip(isMobile, "desktop");
    const maiara = await id("select id from professionals where name = 'Maiara'");
    const corte = await id("select id from lists where kind = 'service' and label = 'Corte'");
    await e2eSql("update professional_services set commission_bp = 4000 where professional_id = $1 and service_id = $2", [maiara, corte]);
    const cliente = await id("insert into contacts (name, phone_e164) values ('Cris Caixa', '+5571988773001') on conflict (phone_e164) do update set name = excluded.name returning id");
    await e2eSql(`insert into appointments (contact_id, client_name, professional_id, service_id, service_label, starts_at, duration_min, price_cents, status, origin, external_key)
      values ($1, 'Cris Caixa', $2, $3, 'Corte', $4, 60, 12000, 'chegou', 'crm', 'e2e:' || gen_random_uuid())`, [cliente, maiara, corte, em(hoje(), agoraHM())]);

    await page.goto("/caixa");
    await page.getByLabel("Troco inicial (R$)").fill("100");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Abrir caixa" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText("Caixa aberto")).toBeVisible();

    await page.goto("/agenda?v=lista");
    await page.getByTestId("agenda-item").filter({ hasText: "Cris Caixa" }).locator('button[aria-haspopup="dialog"]').click();
    await page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Cris Caixa" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Abrir comanda" }).click();
    const comanda = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: /^Comanda \d+ · Cris Caixa$/ });
    await expect(comanda.getByText("Corte")).toBeVisible();
    await comanda.getByLabel("Forma de pagamento").selectOption("pix");
    await comanda.getByLabel("Valor recebido (R$)").fill("50");
    await comanda.getByRole("button", { name: "Receber" }).click();
    await comanda.getByLabel("Forma de pagamento").selectOption("dinheiro");
    await comanda.getByLabel("Valor recebido (R$)").fill("100");
    await comanda.getByRole("button", { name: "Receber" }).click();
    await expect(comanda.getByText(/^Troco: R\$\s30$/)).toBeVisible();
    await comanda.getByRole("button", { name: "Finalizar" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText(/^Comanda \d+ fechada$/)).toBeVisible();
    await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
    await page.reload();
    await expect(page.getByTestId("agenda-item").filter({ hasText: "Cris Caixa" }).getByTitle("Comanda fechada")).toBeVisible();
    expect((await e2eSql("select status from appointments where contact_id = $1", [cliente]))[0].status).toBe("compareceu");

    await page.goto("/caixa");
    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Fechar caixa" }).click();
    const fechar = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Fechar caixa" });
    await fechar.getByLabel("Dinheiro contado (R$)").fill("165"); // esperado: 100 + 100 − 30 = 170
    await fechar.getByRole("button", { name: "Fechar caixa" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByText(/^Caixa fechado\. Diferença: /)).toBeVisible();
    expect((await e2eSql("select difference_cents from cash_sessions"))[0].difference_cents).toBe(-500);

    await page.goto("/caixa/comissao");
    const maiaraSec = page.getByRole("region", { name: "Comissão de Maiara" });
    await expect(maiaraSec.getByRole("row").filter({ hasText: "Corte" })).toContainText("40%");
    await expect(maiaraSec).toContainText(/Comissão R\$\s48/);
  });

  test("Já pagaram: quem pagou e não voltou aparece com 'Sem horário marcado' e Agendar abre o Marcar com a cliente", async ({ page }) => {
    const nina = await id("insert into contacts (name, phone_e164) values ('Nina Pagou', '+5571988773002') on conflict (phone_e164) do update set name = excluded.name returning id");
    await e2eSql(`insert into client_packages (contact_id, client_name, package_name, service_label, total_sessions, used_sessions, price_cents, purchased_on, valid_until, origin, external_key)
      values ($1, 'Nina Pagou', 'Combo Método Fiaes', 'Corte', 4, 1, 40000, current_date - 30, current_date + 200, 'avec', 'e2e:' || gen_random_uuid())`, [nina]);
    await page.goto("/agenda?aba=pacotes"); // apelido antigo
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/aba=pacotes/);
    await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: /Já pagaram/ })).toHaveAttribute("aria-current", "page");
    const linha = page.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Nina Pagou" });
    await expect(linha.getByText(/Combo Método Fiaes · faltam 3 de 4 · vence/)).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByRole("navigation", { name: "Filtrar quem já pagou" }).getByRole("link", { name: "Sem horário marcado" }).click();
    await expect(page.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Nina Pagou" }).getByText("Sem horário marcado")).toBeVisible();
    await page.getByRole("listitem", { name: "Nina Pagou" }).getByRole("button", { name: "Agendar" }).click();
    const dlg = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: "Marcar horário" });
    await expect(dlg.getByRole("region", { name: "Cliente" })).toContainText("Nina Pagou");
  });
});
```

Em `playwright.config.ts`, no projeto `celular`, acrescentar `caixa` ao `testMatch`: `/(mobile|fila|conversa|cliente|contatos|jornada|hoje|a11y|agenda-base|agenda-viva|caixa)\.spec\.ts/`.
